# Naya audit — all verified findings by area (13 October 2026)

Companion to [2026-10-13-full-audit.md](2026-10-13-full-audit.md). Every finding below survived an independent adversarial verification pass (`verified` = confirmed or severity-adjusted by the verifier; `found-by-verifier` = missed by the auditor, added by the verifier; `unverified-low` = low-severity, title-skimmed only). Line numbers refer to commit 89aae18 plus the uncommitted working tree on 13 October 2026.

<a id="passenger"></a>

## Passenger app (apps/passenger)

**Health 6/10.** The passenger app is a small layer (about 2.6k lines) of expo-router routes over the shared @naya/ui kit. The core VTC flows are solid and well covered by e2e tests: verification gating, route editing, a server-priced quote, idempotent booking, live ride tracking, cancellation with fee acknowledgement, receipt and support. Most screens have proper loading, error and empty states, French copy, and accessibility labels. The Oct 6 feedback features are mostly thin wrappers around shared prototype screens: categories, wallet, family, SOS, dark mode and logos. Several of them are half-wired in the passenger app. Dark mode breaks the home screen and the ride status pill because colors are captured at module level or hard-coded. Wallet payments are described as card or cash on the quote, cancel sheet and receipt. Category, city and scheduling rules are hard-coded in the client instead of read from the city or catalog config. Demo-only affordances are not gated behind DEMO_MODE: the simulated SOS, test-card banners, '(démo)' ETAs, the logo gallery in Compte, and family driver-step simulation. The city has two sources of truth: device prefs and the server's user.cityId. There are no passenger unit tests. Pure logic (Luhn check, payment-state mapping, schedule slots, date masking) sits inside screen files. The uncommitted app.config change is a workable local workaround for a repo path with spaces. It is applied only to the passenger app and copies Expo internals.

**Strengths**

- Routes are thin and delegate to the shared @naya/ui kit (support, capture, auth, wallet, family, SOS), so passenger and driver behave the same way.
- Verification gating uses Expo Router Stack.Protected with an opaque cover while /me loads, so deep links survive (src/app/_layout.tsx:18-40); e2e deeplinks-passenger.spec.ts covers it.
- Pricing is server-authoritative: quotes are single-use (gcTime 0, retry false), refresh on expiry, and terms are frozen at booking. The cancellation fee is acknowledged and a CONFLICT re-shows the new fee (quote.tsx:58-85, CancelSheet.tsx:10-32).
- Booking and card-retry use useSingleFlight with a stable Idempotency-Key, so double taps and timeouts replay instead of duplicating (quote.tsx:88, receipt/[id].tsx:47).
- Privacy-conscious defaults: the session lives in SecureStore (device-only), the full card number never leaves the screen, location is foreground-only behind an explainer with a map-pin fallback, and native permission strings are in French.
- Most data screens have explicit loading, error and empty states (trips, receipt, scheduled, payments, verification-status).
- Accessibility basics are handled: labels on icon buttons, radio and tab roles, reduce-motion respected in navigators and the tab bar, Dynamic Type-aware tab bar spacing.
- Query keys are account-scoped through the qk factory, and AccountBoundary drops the previous account's cache on switch.
- Passenger e2e coverage is broad (S01-S16 in e2e/tests/passenger-flows.spec.ts, plus a cross-role feedback scenario).

**Findings (critical / high / medium)**

1. **Home screen is unreadable in dark mode: card styles are captured at module load** — 🟠 high · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: src/app/(app)/(tabs)/index.tsx:174-178 (const styles = { card: { backgroundColor: colors.surface ... }, search: { backgroundColor: colors.accent }, go: {...} }); packages/tokens/src/index.ts:48-50 (colors is a Proxy read at access time; themedStyles helper exists but is unused here); app.config.ts:12 userInterfaceStyle 'automatic'; packages/ui/src/core/theme.tsx:6 default mode 'system'
   - Detail: Reading colors.* at module scope freezes the light hex values. The default mode is 'system', so any iPhone in dark mode shows the main card in white (#FFFFFF) with dark-palette text ink #F4EDF5 ('On y va ?', greeting, quick pills). The 'Où allez-vous ?' pill stays light plum with dark inverse text (#201522), and the 'go' disc stays white with a pale-pink arrow. Driver and ui components already use themedStyles; this screen was not migrated. The feedback e2e only checks the Préférences background (e2e/tests/scenarios-feedback.spec.ts:94-99), so it never sees this.
   - Fix: Wrap styles in themedStyles(() => ({...})) or build them inside the component after useTheme(). Add a dark-mode screenshot or assertion for Home, Ride and Quote.
2. **Wallet and mobile-wallet payments are described as card or cash on the quote, cancel sheet and receipt** — 🟠 high · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: receipt/[id].tsx:16-23 (any non-cash method falls into the card branch: 'Carte · paiement confirmé', 'Réglez avec une autre carte'); CancelSheet.tsx:60 (non-card: 'Les frais éventuels sont à régler avec votre prochaine course.'); quote.tsx:190,193 (wallet shows the Banknote icon and 'Débitée à l’arrivée'); services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:199 (wallet reserved at booking), :457-458 (wallet cancellation fee settled immediately); packages/domain/src/entities.ts:292 PaymentMethodKind includes 'wallet' \| 'mobile_wallet'
   - Detail: The Oct 6 wallet feature added two payment kinds, but the passenger copy still branches on cash vs card. A passenger paying with Portefeuille Naya sees 'Carte · paiement confirmé'. On cancellation she is told the fee will be collected with her next ride, although it was already debited from her wallet. The quote says the money is taken on arrival, although it is held at confirmation. These are misleading payment claims.
   - Fix: Centralise a paymentMethodPresentation(kind) helper in packages/ui or domain (icon, timing copy, receipt title, fee copy) and use it on the quote, cancel sheet, receipt and payments screens. Show the available wallet balance and an insufficient-funds hint on the quote.
3. **The ride-screen SOS is a demo simulation in every build and sends the driver's or pickup position, not the passenger's** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:173 <SafetyButton rideId location={ride.driverLocation?.location ?? ride.route.stops[0]!.location} />; packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:84 label 'SOS · sécurité', :92 title 'SOS · démonstration', :126-127 'Appel urgence simulé', 'Position partagée (simulation)'; ride.tsx:178 'Suivi de démonstration · utilisez SOS pour tester une alerte.' (not gated); DEMO_MODE in packages/ui/src/core/apiBase.ts:4 is never checked
   - Detail: In a women's safety product, the primary safety control opens a sheet whose actions only record a demo alert. There is no tel: action inside the SOS sheet; the real 19 call is hidden in the Contact sheet. The position sent is the driver GPS or the pickup point, never the device location. It is shown even while searching. With EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO=0 the same simulated flow and the 'test an alert' caption would ship.
   - Fix: Gate the simulation behind DEMO_MODE. For the real build, put a direct call to 19 inside the SOS sheet, attach device foreground location, and show SOS only from driver_assigned onward. Write an ADR on what SOS guarantees before launch.
   - Verifier: The facts hold. ride.tsx:173 passes the driver's position or the pickup point. SafetyButton (packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:48-150) only records demo alerts, and DEMO_MODE is not checked there or at ride.tsx:178. tel:19 exists only in ContactSheet.tsx:20, which is rendered only when ride.driver exists (ride.tsx:193), so a passenger who is still searching has no call path. I lowered it to…
4. **Demo-only UI and internal tools are not gated by DEMO_MODE** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: payments.tsx:51 'Démo … aucun débit réel'; card/new.tsx:67 'bac à sable', :78 test-card banner; ContactSheet.tsx:16 'Démo'; quote.tsx:160 'Arrivée estimée · … (démo)'; payments.tsx:37 'Paiement portefeuille · démonstration'; account.tsx:34 'Recharges et paiements de démo', :36 'Identité · 6 pistes · Explorer les propositions de logo'; prototype.tsx:360 'Charger l’exemple de démo', :661 passenger 'Simuler les étapes de la chauffeuse'; welcome.tsx and account.tsx:43 do gate the dev launcher, which shows the gate exists but is applied inconsistently
   - Detail: A production profile (eas.json:33-38 EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO=0) would ship test-card instructions, demo labels, an internal logo exploration gallery, and the ability for a parent to perform the driver's child-handover steps.
   - Fix: Add a DemoOnly wrapper or flag-check helper in @naya/ui and apply it to every demo banner and simulation control. Move /brand behind DEMO_MODE or remove it from Compte once a logo is chosen.
5. **The passenger's city lives in device prefs and in server user.cityId, and they can diverge** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: lib/prefs.ts:17 cityId default 'rabat' (AsyncStorage, not cleared on sign-out: account.tsx:21-27); used by index.tsx:32, route.tsx:24, quote.tsx:52,60, places.tsx:23. Server city used by account.tsx:32 (me.data.city.name), wallet.tsx:4 (me.data.user.cityId ?? 'rabat') and payment availability services/demo-api/src/app.ts:360 (user.cityId)
   - Detail: On a fresh install, after reinstall, or when a second account signs in on the same device, Home shows Rabat while the server user is in Casablanca (or the reverse). Quotes are then priced with one city's rules while payment-method availability is computed for the other. route.tsx:25 has no fallback when the prefs city no longer exists, while index.tsx:39 falls back to the first city.
   - Fix: Make the server user.cityId the source of truth (read from useMe) and keep prefs only as an offline cache, or reset prefs on sign-in. Record the decision in an ADR.
6. **The 'arrived' notification never fires in background, but Préférences promises it** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: ride.tsx:46-59 schedules a local notification when AppState !== 'active' after a status change seen by polling; packages/ui/src/core/query.ts:15-17 sets focusManager unfocused in background (refetchInterval pauses; refetchIntervalInBackground is not set); no getExpoPushTokenAsync or setNotificationHandler in the passenger app; preferences.tsx:46 'Être prévenue à l’arrivée ? Même si l’app est en arrière-plan.'
   - Detail: In background no poll runs, so the status change is only seen once the app is active again, and by then no notification is shown. Users can miss the driver's arrival and incur the after-arrival fee. Scheduled dispatch and support replies have no notifications either.
   - Fix: Remove or qualify the promise until push is implemented. Plan server push (Expo push or APNs) for driver_assigned, driver_arrived, scheduled dispatch and support replies, and document it in INTEGRATIONS.md.
7. **Scheduling rules are hard-coded in the client instead of read from city.scheduling** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: SchedulePicker.tsx:10 defaults minLead 30 / maxDays 7, :19 hours 5..23 only; quote.tsx:187 and scheduled/[id].tsx:94 do not pass city rules; scheduled/[id].tsx:59 cutoff 60 min and :78 copy; quote.tsx:201 and scheduled/[id].tsx:77 'recherche 15 min avant le départ'; trips.tsx:48 'jusqu’à 7 jours'; the PublicCity.scheduling field exists (packages/api/src/client.ts:66) and is admin-editable (packages/domain/src/schemas.ts:165)
   - Detail: If an admin changes lead time, horizon or modify cutoff, the passenger UI offers wrong slots, wrongly disables 'Modifier l’horaire', and the server rejects with SCHEDULE_TOO_SOON or SCHEDULE_CUTOFF. Night slots (00:00-04:59) can never be scheduled, although the server allows them, which matters for early airport runs.
   - Fix: Pass city.scheduling into SchedulePicker and the cutoff logic. Move slot generation into packages/domain next to the scheduled state machine, and expose the dispatch lead from the server.
8. **The quote always starts on category 'standard', even if it is disabled or not offered in the city** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: quote.tsx:55 useState('standard'); :154 renders only enabled, in-city categories; services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:240-248 categoryFor throws when the category is disabled or outside the city; quote.tsx:157 always shows the car illustration; :160 ETA fallback 5
   - Detail: When an admin disables Standard (admin Services page), the first quote fails with 'Prix indisponible' and no pill is selected. Category icons (scooter, premium) are ignored and Naya Scooter is shown with a car.
   - Fix: Default to the first enabled category for the city (or one flagged as default in the catalog). Show catalog loading and error states, and use the category icon or illustration.
9. **/ride shows an endless spinner when there is no active ride** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: ride.tsx:86 if (!ride) return <ActivityIndicator/>; :42-44 only redirects when lastId.current was set during this mount
   - Detail: Opening /ride by deep link, state restoration, or a stale resume card after the ride ended leaves the user on a spinner. The back gesture is disabled for this screen (_layout.tsx:18) and there is no header.
   - Fix: When active.isSuccess && !ride && !lastId, show an empty state ('Aucune course en cours') with a button home, or redirect to the latest ride's receipt.
10. **A dispatched booking's 'Voir la course' opens a receipt that says 'Course terminée' for an active ride** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: scheduled/[id].tsx:71-72 pushes /receipt/[rideId] for any non-open booking with a rideId; receipt/[id].tsx:76 prints 'Course terminée' for every non-cancelled status; services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:787-800 dispatches 15 min before pickup
   - Detail: During the 15 minutes before pickup and throughout the trip, the passenger sees a 'finished' receipt with 'Paiement en préparation' instead of live tracking.
   - Fix: Route to /ride when the linked ride is active (check isRideActive or no_driver). Make the receipt handle non-terminal statuses explicitly.
11. **Emergency copy says '15 (SAMU)'** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: src/features/help/topics.ts:48 'Appelez le 19 (police) ou le 15 (SAMU)'; packages/ui/src/features/support.tsx:149 'Urgence : 19 (police) · 15 (SAMU)'
   - Detail: In Morocco, 15 is generally the Protection civile (fire and ambulance) line and SAMU is commonly listed as 141, with Gendarmerie at 177 outside cities. Safety copy must be exact. This needs local verification.
   - Fix: Confirm the numbers with the operations team, keep them as one constant in packages/domain, and include the Gendarmerie (177) for non-urban trips.
12. **Account deletion is only a generic support ticket** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: preferences.tsx:60 'Supprimer mon compte · écrire à l’assistance' pushes /support/new with no category, so it defaults to 'other' (support/new.tsx:8)
   - Detail: Apple guideline 5.1.1(v) requires that users can initiate account deletion in the app. A blank ticket with category 'Autre' is weak evidence of that, and the request is not pre-filled or tracked as a deletion.
   - Fix: Add an explicit deletion request flow: confirmation, server endpoint, status. At minimum, pre-fill category 'account' and a subject.
13. **No passenger unit tests; pure business logic is buried in screen files** — 🟡 medium · testing · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: find apps/passenger/src -name '*test*' returns nothing; jest.config.js:6 roots only add packages/ui; untested logic: card/new.tsx:12-23 (luhn), receipt/[id].tsx:14-24 (paymentState), SchedulePicker.tsx:10-30 (slots), identity.tsx:20-28 (toIso, maskDate), lib/draft.ts (stops), ride.tsx:95 (ETA); e2e/tests/scenarios-feedback.spec.ts:94-110 checks dark mode only on Préférences and writes screenshots to /tmp
   - Detail: The Oct 6 features (categories, wallet payment on rides, dark Home) have no passenger-level regression tests, and the wallet copy bugs above would have been caught by a paymentState test.
   - Fix: Extract these helpers into packages/domain or ui with unit tests. Add RNTL tests for the quote, cancel sheet and receipt. Extend e2e with a wallet-paid ride and dark-mode Home and Ride assertions.
14. **Ride status pill and verification hero ignore dark mode** — 🟡 medium · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: ride.tsx:118 backgroundColor 'rgba(255,255,255,0.96)' behind default ink text (dark ink #F4EDF5, so light text on white); verification-status.tsx:108-113 module-level toneColors (frozen light values), :120 borderColor 'rgba(46,32,44,0.06)'
   - Detail: In dark mode the ride status label at the top of the map is nearly invisible, and the verification hero discs keep light-palette backgrounds.
   - Fix: Use the colors.glass or surface tokens and themedStyles. Add a lint rule or grep check against rgba/hex literals and module-level colors.* in app code.
15. **Booking flow leaves stale screens in the stack: router.navigate('/') pushes a duplicate Home, and receipt back returns to the route editor or a stuck /ride** — 🟡 medium · bug · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: route.tsx:51 router.push('/quote'). quote.tsx:104 router.replace('/ride'), which gives the stack [(tabs), route, ride]. ride.tsx:43 and :192 router.replace to the receipt, giving [(tabs), route, receipt]. ride.tsx:117 router.navigate('/'). receipt/[id].tsx:68,71 header back calls router.back() when canGoBack. (app)/_layout.tsx:16 route is a fullScreenModal and :18 sets gestureEnabled false on ride. node_modules/expo-router/build/react-navigation/routers/StackRouter.js:188-197 (NAVIGATE reuses a route only if it is current or payload.pop is set) and :244-252 (otherwise it pushes a new route). node_modules/expo-router/build/global-state/router.js:58-60 (navigate dispatches a plain NAVIGATE).
   - Detail: After a normal ride, tapping the receipt's header back arrow reopens the route editor modal with an empty draft, instead of Home. If the passenger tapped 'Retour à l’accueil' during the ride, a second Home is pushed on top of a still-mounted /ride. Each 'resume ride' tap then pushes another /ride, so several RideScreens poll and redirect at once. When the ride ends, the hidden /ride replaces the top screen with the receipt, and the receipt's back arrow returns to the stale /ride. There ride is null, so it shows an endless spinner with no header, and the swipe gesture is disabled on iOS. No e2e test covers ride-back or resume-ride (grep finds no matches in e2e/).
   - Fix: Use router.dismissTo('/') (POP_TO) for the ride back button. Replace the whole booking modal (route, quote) when entering /ride, for example dismissTo('/') followed by push('/ride'), or make /ride a root-level screen. Make the receipt's back arrow behave like 'Terminé' (dismissTo('/')). Give /ride a no-active-ride empty state with a way home. Add an e2e test covering back to Home, resume, completion and receipt back.
16. **'Frais à régler avec votre prochaine course' is promised but never implemented** — 🟡 medium · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: features/ride/CancelSheet.tsx:60 ('Les frais éventuels sont à régler avec votre prochaine course.'). receipt/[id].tsx:19 ('… à régler avec votre prochaine course.'). services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:441-461: for cash, the fee payment is created as status 'pending' with provider 'cash', and no job is queued and no ledger entry is posted. grep for 'cancellation_fee' and 'prochaine course' across services/demo-api/src finds no code that adds pending fees to a later quote or ride, or asks the driver to collect them.
   - Detail: A passenger paying cash must accept the cancellation fee before confirming. She is then told it will be added to her next ride, but the next quote never includes it and the fee stays 'pending' forever. The driver who travelled to the pickup is never credited (the ledger entry is posted only for card or wallet fees). This is a cash-payment business rule that the UI states and the system does not implement.
   - Fix: Decide the cash-fee policy in an ADR: add it to the next fare, require card or wallet settlement, or waive it. Implement it on the server (outstanding balance on the user, shown on the next quote's breakdown) and make the copy match. Until then, change the copy so it does not promise collection.
17. **A ride can be booked from an implicit city-centre pickup the passenger never chose** — 🟡 medium · ux · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: (tabs)/index.tsx:20 (CITY_DEFAULT_PICKUP = 'Rabat · Centre-ville', Place Pietri, or Gare Casa-Port) and :44-47 (sets draft.pickup with source 'default'). lib/draft.ts:14,32 define pickupSource 'default'. The only consumer of pickupSource is index.tsx:82 (marker style). route.tsx:26 opens straight on the destination search, and route.tsx/quote.tsx never mark or block a default pickup. index.tsx:42 still shows the default pin after draft.reset() (quote.tsx:100), while the route editor then has no pickup.
   - Detail: Tapping 'Où allez-vous ?' and picking a destination leads to a quote whose pickup is the city centre, unless the passenger notices and edits it. In a real launch the driver goes to the wrong place and the passenger waits on the street or pays an after-arrival fee, which is a safety concern for this product. The behaviour is also inconsistent: after a first booking, Home keeps showing the default pickup pin while the route editor shows 'Choisir le départ'.
   - Fix: Do not set a default pickup silently. Either ask for location on first use (with the existing explainer) or open the route editor on the pickup field when pickupSource is 'default'. On the quote, show an explicit 'Départ : … · Modifier' confirmation when the pickup was not chosen by GPS, search or pin.
18. **The consent line cites terms and privacy policy, but neither is linked anywhere in the app** — 🟡 medium · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: (auth)/phone.tsx:48-52: 'En continuant, vous acceptez les conditions d’utilisation et la politique de confidentialité de Naya.' is plain Text with no link. grep for 'confidentialit\|privacy\|CGU\|openURL\|WebBrowser' in apps/passenger/src and packages/ui/src finds only this sentence, tel:19, and the OSM attribution (packages/ui/src/map/TileMap.tsx:146). The app collects identity documents and a selfie (features/verify/steps.ts), location and phone number.
   - Detail: Passengers accept documents they cannot open, and there is no privacy policy reachable in the app. Before a public release, App Store guideline 5.1.1(i) requires an accessible privacy policy, and Moroccan data-protection law (09-08, CNDP) requires informing people before collecting ID documents and biometrics-like selfies. Preferences only has a short privacy note (preferences.tsx:59).
   - Fix: Add 'Conditions d’utilisation' and 'Politique de confidentialité' links (expo-web-browser is already a plugin) on the phone screen and under Compte or Préférences. Add a short data-use notice before the selfie and ID capture. Track the CNDP declaration in the launch checklist or an ADR.

**Open questions raised**

- Is the passenger app ever meant to ship with EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO=0 in its current form? If so, which Oct 6 features (wallet top-up simulation, family driver-step simulation, logo gallery, SOS simulation) must be gated or removed?
- Which is the single source of truth for the passenger's city: server user.cityId or device prefs.cityId?
- Is 'Naya Scooter' a real launch category for a women-only VTC service, and should categories carry their own illustrations and ETAs from live supply instead of static etaMinutes?
- Morocco emergency numbers: confirm 15 vs 141 for SAMU, and whether Gendarmerie 177 should appear for trips outside the city.
- Is a support ticket acceptable as the in-app account deletion path for App Store review (guideline 5.1.1(v))?
- Should scheduled rides be bookable between 00:00 and 04:59? The client blocks this today and the server does not.
- Uncommitted change: will the repo move off the space-containing recovery path (making with-ios-build-paths unnecessary)? If not, should the driver app get the same plugin and appleTeamId?
- The pnpm-lock.yaml diff (+2.2k lines, mostly eslint packages) has no matching package.json modification in git status. Which install produced it, and should it be committed?
- Typecheck, lint and jest were not run because this audit was read-only without authorization to run them, so their current pass or fail state for apps/passenger is unverified (docs/MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md:37 claims TypeScript passed on Oct 6).
- Should identity details (name, document number) be correctable during more_info_requested, or is a rejection and reopen the intended path?
- Is Apple sign-in planned for v1? The button is wired only to the native prompt.

<a id="driver"></a>

## Driver app (apps/driver)

**Health 6.5/10.** The driver app (about 3,000 lines over 46 route and component files) covers the D01–D18 Figma flows end to end. The structure is sound: guards are server-authoritative (Stack.Protected on the dossier submission state), the offer countdown runs against the server deadline and corrects for device clock skew, financial actions use single-flight and idempotency keys, money is integer centimes throughout, dark mode works through a Proxy palette that every screen opts into with useTheme(), and demo surfaces are honestly labelled. The weak spots are the safety-critical and money-adjacent edges. First, background location sharing stops when the driver taps the "home (la course continue)" button, and it never stops on its own if the ride ends while the phone is locked. Second, the "masked contact" sheet has no contact action, even though the October audit already flagged it. Third, background offer alerts cannot fire: polling pauses in the background, notification permission is never requested, and the preference toggle is ignored. Fourth, the offer timer and copy assume 30 s, but the timeout is configurable per city (10–120 s). Fifth, the October 6 payment kinds (wallet, mobile_wallet) are still labelled "Carte" in earnings, history and the ledger, and are left out of "Crédité au portefeuille". The October 6 features (family, logos, SOS, theme, categories) are thin wrappers over packages/ui. Several of them appear in non-demo builds (logo gallery, simulated PSP sandbox, "load demo example"), and category eligibility is invisible to the driver. The app has no unit tests, no CI or formatter, and a lot of logic duplicated with apps/passenger (session/prefs/queries libs, root guard layout, TabBar, support/family/brand/dev wrappers). Its config has drifted from passenger and Expo defaults: an English microphone permission string, an unused Face ID permission string, background-location modes declared in standalone builds that never use them, and the uncommitted iOS build-path plugin applied to passenger only.

**Strengths**

- Offer countdown derives from the server deadline and corrects for device clock skew (X-Server-Time). It never resets on navigation, and accept is decided server-side (packages/api/src/react.ts:21-31, client.ts:279-292).
- Route guards use expo-router Stack.Protected with an opaque loading cover, so deep links survive while the account loads (apps/driver/src/app/_layout.tsx:17-44).
- Financial and ride actions use useSingleFlight (synchronous in-flight ref plus a stable idempotency key) and mutations never auto-retry (packages/ui/src/core/query.ts:33-38).
- Money is integer centimes everywhere, formatted by @naya/domain formatMoney with a proper minus sign. The wallet's four concepts (available/reserved/debt/pending) have one-line definitions (components/WalletCard.tsx:11-16).
- Loading, empty and error states are present on almost every data screen (earnings, wallet, ledger, rides, recharge, withdraw, ride-end).
- Demo surfaces are labelled honestly ('Simulation : aucun paiement réel', 'Trajet simulé', 'SOS · démonstration').
- Dark mode coverage is complete: every file that reads colors calls useTheme(), and module-level styles use themedStyles.
- Good accessibility baseline: CountdownRing exposes a timer role and live region, ChoiceRow uses radio semantics, the online state has a text label, and amounts carry accessibility labels.
- Sign-out from Account leaves the dispatch pool before revoking the token (account.tsx:27-34). Account-scoped query keys plus AccountBoundary prevent cross-account cache leaks.
- Playwright E2E covers the main driver money and ride scenarios S09–S16 on Expo web (e2e/tests/driver-flows.spec.ts).

**Findings (critical / high / medium)**

1. **Background location sharing stops when the driver goes 'home' mid-ride** — 🟠 high · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:56 `useEffect(() => () => void stopRideTracking(), [])`; ride.tsx:105 `const home = () => router.dismissTo('/')`; ride.tsx:145 accessibilityLabel="Retour à l’accueil (la course continue)"; app.config.ts:47 permission text promises sharing 'même écran verrouillé… jusqu’à la fin de la course'
   - Detail: The back button promises the ride continues, but dismissTo('/') unmounts the ride screen and its cleanup stops the background location task. Lock-screen sharing with the passenger ends until the driver reopens the ride via 'Reprendre'. Only the foreground stream in (app)/_layout keeps sending, and only while the app is open.
   - Fix: Tie background tracking to driver state (status.data.activeRide), not to the ride screen. Start and stop it from (app)/_layout's status watcher and remove the unmount cleanup in ride.tsx.
2. **Background 'Always' location tracking is never stopped if the ride ends while the app is killed or the ride screen is not mounted** — 🟠 high · security · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: Every stopRideTracking call is in apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx (:53, :56, :81, :99; grep finds no other caller). The ride screen is only mounted via restore when an activeRide exists (apps/driver/src/app/(app)/_layout.tsx:36-40) or when the user taps 'Reprendre'. expo-location's foreground service survives app kill by default (node_modules/expo-location/android/.../LocationTaskConsumer.kt:212, killServiceOnDestroy default false), and startLocationUpdatesAsync registrations persist across launches (backgroundLocation.ts:44 relies on hasStartedLocationUpdatesAsync for that). The task posts with no ride or online check (backgroundLocation.ts:16-27). The server accepts location from an of…
   - Detail: Scenario: the app is killed or crashes mid-ride, then the passenger cancels or support closes the ride. On the next launch there is no activeRide, so the ride screen never mounts and the location task keeps running indefinitely. On Android the 'Course Naya en cours' foreground notification stays; on iOS the blue location indicator stays. GPS fixes keep posting to /driver/location even after the driver goes offline, and GPS and the notification keep running after sign-out. This contradicts the permission string 'Le partage s’arrête à la fin de la course' (app.config.ts:46) and the Play background-location policy.
   - Fix: Reconcile tracking in (app)/_layout from driver status: start it when status.activeRide exists and stop it otherwise, including at app start and on sign-out. Also have the task stop itself when /driver/location reports no active ride. Pass killServiceOnDestroy:true. Server-side, ignore or refuse location from offline drivers with no active ride.
3. **Background location task keeps posting after the ride ends while the app is backgrounded** — 🟡 medium · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/lib/backgroundLocation.ts:16-31 (the task posts every fix with no ride/state check); stop is only called from ride.tsx:53,81,99 (requires JS UI to observe the end); notification body backgroundLocation.ts:51 'jusqu’à la fin de la course'
   - Detail: If the passenger cancels or the ride is completed while the phone is locked, the task keeps sending GPS every 5 s / 15 m. The Android foreground notification keeps saying 'Course Naya en cours' until the app is foregrounded and the ride screen notices. This contradicts the permission and notification promises.
   - Fix: In the task, stop updates when the server response (or a cheap status check) shows no active ride, or have /driver/location return a 'stop tracking' flag. Also stop on sign-out.
4. **'Contacter la passagère' promises masked calling but has no contact action** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:222-229 (subtitle 'Votre numéro reste masqué des deux côtés.', only button 'Compris'); ride.tsx:225 police number '19' as plain text, not tappable; (auth)/phone.tsx:11 'joindre vos passagères de façon masquée'; already flagged in docs/MOBILE-FEEDBACK-AUDIT.md:43 and :69
   - Detail: The driver cannot reach the passenger at all, and the copy implies a masked telephony feature that does not exist. The emergency number cannot be tapped. This misleading safety claim was flagged on Oct 6 and is still unfixed.
   - Fix: Either add a demo 'Appeler (simulé)' / in-app message action with honest labelling, or remove the masking claim. Make 19 a tel: link and include it in SOS.
   - Verifier: Verified ride.tsx:222-229: the subtitle claims masking, the only action is 'Compris', and '19' is plain text. phone.tsx:11 repeats the masking claim. docs/INTEGRATIONS.md:20 openly states masked calls are unavailable in the demo. The passenger ContactSheet already shows an honest 'Démo · fournisseur non configuré' banner and a dial link, so this is a driver-side parity and copy gap rather than a…
5. **Offer countdown ring and copy assume 30 s, but the timeout is configurable per city (10–120 s)** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/offer.tsx:117 `<CountdownRing seconds={seconds} total={30} />`; offer.tsx:82 'Le délai de 30 secondes est écoulé'; (app)/_layout.tsx:32 'Vous avez 30 secondes pour répondre.'; (tabs)/index.tsx:127 '30 s pour répondre'; server: packages/domain/src/schemas.ts:162 offerTimeoutSeconds min(10).max(120), services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:276
   - Detail: If an admin changes a city's offer timeout, the ring is clamped full for part of the time (or starts partly empty), and every message states the wrong delay. DriverOffer already carries createdAt and expiresAt, and status.city exposes offerTimeoutSeconds.
   - Fix: Use total = (expiresAt - createdAt)/1000 or d.city.offerTimeoutSeconds, and interpolate it into all copy.
6. **A later offer can be hidden behind the previous offer's 'expired/taken/withdrawn' screen** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/offer.tsx:25 localPhase state; :51-52 and :65 set it on errors; :32 snapshot switches to a new offer when the old one is no longer pending; :70 `const phase = localPhase ?? …` (never reset); (app)/_layout.tsx:30 does not re-push while pathname starts with '/offer'
   - Detail: After an accept fails with OFFER_EXPIRED or RIDE_ALREADY_ASSIGNED, localPhase stays set. If a new offer arrives while the driver is still on the terminal screen, the snapshot switches to the new pending offer, but the phase still renders the old terminal state. The new offer expires unseen. The 10 s haptic ref and accept error state are not reset either.
   - Fix: Reset localPhase, warned.current and accept/decline state whenever snapshot.id changes (key the screen body by offer.id).
7. **Background 'Nouvelle course' notification can never fire, and its preference is ignored** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/_layout.tsx:31-32 schedules a local notification only when AppState !== 'active'; polling that would detect the offer is paused in the background (packages/ui/src/core/query.ts:15-17 focusManager wired to AppState, no refetchIntervalInBackground in apps/driver/src/lib/queries.ts:33-42); no Notifications.requestPermissionsAsync anywhere (grep returns only _layout.tsx:11,32); profile/preferences.tsx:12 toggle 'Alerte quand l’application est en arrière-plan' is never read on the client
   - Detail: Drivers are shown a setting and a documented capability (docs/INTEGRATIONS.md:18 'Local notifications scheduled by the apps') that do not work. Offers that arrive while the app is backgrounded are only discovered when the app returns to the foreground.
   - Fix: Request notification permission in context (when going online), and respect user.notifications.offers. Be explicit that background offers need remote push, or relabel the toggle as unavailable in the demo.
8. **Wallet and mobile-wallet rides (Oct 6) shown as 'Carte' and left out of 'Crédité au portefeuille'** — 🟡 medium · bug · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/earnings.tsx:59 tile 'Crédité au portefeuille · Courses carte (net)' and :69 `r.method === 'cash' ? 'Espèces' : 'Carte…'`; rides/index.tsx:34; rides/[id].tsx:42 'Carte via Naya'; ledger/[id].tsx:13 'Revenu net · course carte' and :48; server services/demo-api/src/services/finance.ts:276-277 filters `x.method === 'card'`; PaymentMethodKind includes 'wallet' \| 'mobile_wallet' (packages/domain/src/entities.ts:292); offer.tsx:128 already distinguishes 'Portefeuille'
   - Detail: Rides paid from the passenger wallet or a Moroccan mobile wallet credit the driver, but earnings understate 'Crédité au portefeuille' and 'carte en attente'. History, detail and ledger also call them card rides. Only offer.tsx and ride-end were updated on Oct 6.
   - Fix: Add a shared paymentKindLabel(kind) in @naya/domain and use it everywhere. In earningsOf, treat every non-cash kind as electronic. Add an E2E case for a wallet-paid ride.
   - Verifier: Real. settleRide (rides.ts:625-629) credits ride_net_credit for wallet and mobile_wallet rides and confirms the payment immediately, but earningsOf counts walletCredited only for method === 'card' (finance.ts:276). The labels at earnings.tsx:59 and :69, rides/index.tsx:34, rides/[id].tsx:42 and ledger/[id].tsx:13 and :48 all say card. The ledger balance and the headline net are correct, though, a…
9. **'Revenus nets' counts failed and pending card rides, contradicting its own footnote** — 🟡 medium · bug · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/finance.ts:273 `net: total((x) => x.net)` sums every completed ride regardless of paymentStatus; apps/driver/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/earnings.tsx:64 footnote 'Net = espèces + crédits portefeuille − commissions espèces.'; also dashboard (tabs)/index.tsx:133-134
   - Detail: A ride whose card payment failed (ride-end says 'rien n’est crédité') still adds its net to the earnings headline and the dashboard tile. The displayed figure does not match the stated formula and overstates income.
   - Fix: Make the server compute net = cash net + confirmed electronic net and report pending separately, or change the footnote and label the headline 'Net estimé'. Add a domain unit test.
   - Verifier: Verified finance.ts:273: net sums every completed ride's net regardless of payment status. A failed card payment credits nothing (finance.ts:137-147 posts a credit only when confirmed), yet it stays in 'Revenus nets' (earnings.tsx:49, (tabs)/index.tsx:134), which contradicts the footnote at earnings.tsx:64 and the ride-end text at :70. Counting pending rides as estimated earnings is defensible. O…
10. **Driver can sign out in the middle of an active ride** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/account.tsx:30 `await api.driver.setOnline(false, null).catch(() => undefined)` swallows ACTIVE_RIDE_EXISTS (services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:306-307) and then logs out (:31-33); ConfirmDialog message :54 does not mention the ride
   - Detail: Signing out with an assigned or in-progress ride leaves the passenger with a ride no device can advance. The server explicitly refuses to go offline in that state, but the client ignores the refusal.
   - Fix: Disable sign-out (or show a blocking dialog with 'Reprendre la course') when status.activeRide exists. Surface the API error instead of swallowing it.
11. **Dashboard says 'Propositions en direct' while offers are blocked** — 🟡 medium · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/index.tsx:127 (online ⇒ 'Propositions en direct · 30 s pour répondre' regardless of `blocked`); server keeps an ineligible driver online: services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:303-308 (eligibility checked only when going online) and dispatch filter :262
   - Detail: When the debt limit is reached (or the account is suspended) while online, the driver stays 'en ligne' and is told offers are live, while a red banner says they are blocked.
   - Fix: When online && blocked, show 'En ligne · propositions suspendues' (or auto-offline server-side) and keep the toggle copy consistent.
12. **Service-category eligibility is invisible to drivers and defaults to all categories, including Scooter** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: Only usage in the driver app: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/offer.tsx:112; server default services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:262 and :370 `driverCategories[driver.id] ?? catalog.categories.map(c => c.id)`; packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:182-185 'Naya Scooter'
   - Detail: The Oct 6 'driver eligibility' feature exists only in the admin. A car driver is eligible for Naya Scooter rides by default, and the driver cannot see or request her categories.
   - Fix: Show eligible categories on profile/vehicle (read-only). Default eligibility should come from vehicle type, not 'all'. Decide what a scooter driver profile looks like.
13. **Simulated PSP page, logo-concept gallery, demo SOS and 'Charger l’exemple de démo' ship in non-demo builds** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/recharge/[id].tsx:23-25 auto-pushes /recharge/sandbox/[id] with no DEMO_MODE check; recharge/sandbox/[id].tsx:43 'Autoriser'; account.tsx:46 'Identité · 6 pistes' row not gated (next to :40 'Identité et permis'); ride.tsx:214 SafetyButton ('SOS · démonstration', packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:92); family 'Charger l’exemple de démo' prototype.tsx:356-362; root _layout.tsx:38 registers /dev unconditionally although DevLauncher claims 'not reachable in production'; server lets an owner self-approve her own recharge even with devMode off (services/demo-api/src/app.ts:599-605)
   - Detail: A production profile (eas.json production, EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO=0) would send real drivers to a fake payment page that can credit their wallet. It would also show an internal design review, labelled confusingly as 'Identité', and a demo SOS.
   - Fix: Gate these routes and rows behind DEMO_MODE, using Stack.Protected or a redirect. Server-side, refuse provider-sandbox outside devMode for everyone.
14. **Dashboard, offer and ride bottom sheets are absolutely positioned with no scroll and overflow at large text sizes** — 🟡 medium · a11y · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/index.tsx:107 (up to 2 stat tiles + restored + resume + cash + 2 reasons + GPS + demo button + toggle); offer.tsx:105; ride.tsx:154; Text allows 1.6x scaling (packages/ui/src/Text.tsx:24); map insets hard-coded bottomInset={380}/{470}/{440}
   - Detail: With Dynamic Type or several banners on a small phone (SE), the top of the sheet (title, countdown, fare) is pushed off-screen. Accept and decline stay reachable at the bottom, but the information needed to decide is not visible.
   - Fix: Wrap sheet content in a ScrollView with maxHeight, or use the shared BottomSheet. Measure the sheet height to feed bottomInset.
15. **Unneeded or English permission declarations in the driver binary** — 🟡 medium · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/app.config.ts:36 expo-camera without microphonePermission:false / recordAudioAndroid:false → Info.plist gets 'Allow $(PRODUCT_NAME) to access your microphone' and Android RECORD_AUDIO (node_modules/expo-camera/plugin/build/withCamera.js:6-14,32); app.config.ts:32 faceIDPermission although no Face ID feature exists (grep LocalAuthentication returns nothing); app.config.ts:45-47 isIosBackgroundLocationEnabled while standalone TestFlight builds never start background location (lib/backgroundLocation.ts:15,37)
   - Detail: The binary asks for microphone access in English for a feature that does not exist and declares Face ID usage for nothing. It also declares UIBackgroundModes:location in builds (demo-testflight) that never use it. These are App Review and Play policy risks, and they hurt the French-only experience.
   - Fix: Set microphonePermission:false and recordAudioAndroid:false, and remove faceIDPermission. Make the background-location flags conditional on EXPO_PUBLIC_STANDALONE !== '1'.
16. **'Always' location requested automatically at ride start with no prominent disclosure** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:44-49 calls startRideTracking() on mount; lib/backgroundLocation.ts:42 requestBackgroundPermissionsAsync() with no prior explanation screen; result 'foreground-only' only produces a toast (ride.tsx:48)
   - Detail: Google Play requires an in-app prominent disclosure before requesting ACCESS_BACKGROUND_LOCATION. On Android 11+ this call sends the driver to system settings in the middle of the pickup.
   - Fix: Ask once, in context, before the first ride (for example when going online) with a disclosure sheet. Persist the outcome and never interrupt the pickup.
17. **Background task hard-codes the SecureStore session key and JSON shape and bypasses the API client** — 🟡 medium · maintainability · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/lib/backgroundLocation.ts:20-27 reads 'naya.driver.session' and posts with raw fetch; the key and shape are defined in packages/ui/src/core/session.ts:36,46,53
   - Detail: A change to the session format or key silently breaks lock-screen tracking. The raw fetch has no timeout, no 401 handling and no server-clock observation.
   - Fix: Export a readStoredSession(role) helper and a headless API call from @naya/ui or @naya/api, and use them in the task.
18. **Driver app has no unit tests and no test script** — 🟡 medium · testing · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/package.json scripts: start/ios/android/web/typecheck/lint/doctor (no test); root package.json `pnpm -r --if-present test` skips the driver; only Playwright web E2E exists (e2e/tests/driver-flows.spec.ts:35-195)
   - Detail: Several pieces of driver-only logic are untested: offer phase and snapshot derivation (offer.tsx:30-87), next-stop computation (ride.tsx:119-129), isComplete and caseProgress (Dossier.tsx:24-40), reasonBanner mapping (index.tsx:21-34), Casablanca day boundaries (lib/time.ts), withdraw quick-amount logic (withdraw/index.tsx:57-64), and background tracking lifecycle. E2E runs on web, where location, background tasks and notifications are no-ops.
   - Fix: Extract pure helpers into lib/ or @naya/domain and add jest tests, mirroring packages/ui/src/__tests__. Add a test script so root `pnpm test` covers the driver.
19. **Android back dismisses a pending offer without declining it, and the offer is never shown again** — 🟡 medium · bug · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/_layout.tsx:55 configures offer as fullScreenModal with gestureEnabled:false, which only disables the iOS swipe; there is no BackHandler or usePreventRemove anywhere (grep over apps/driver/src and packages/ui/src returns nothing). The watcher sets seenOffer on arrival (:28-30) and never re-pushes the same offer. The dashboard has no pending-offer entry ((tabs)/index.tsx:141-144 shows only activeRide, cash and eligibility notes).
   - Detail: On Android, the system back gesture or button pops /offer. The offer stays pending on the server but the driver can no longer see or accept it, and it expires as a non-response, which delays the passenger's dispatch by the full timeout. The same missing back interception on /ride (:56) unmounts the ride screen and stops background tracking (see bg-tracking-stops-on-home). Android is the dominant platform in Morocco, the launch market.
   - Fix: Intercept hardware back on /offer (usePreventRemove or BackHandler) and route it to the decline sheet. Add a 'Proposition en attente' card on the dashboard whenever status.offer is pending, so an offer is always reachable.
20. **Offer screen stays on an expired offer, and hides the next one, when the server returns a newer offer first** — 🟡 medium · bug · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/offer.tsx:32 replaces the snapshot with a different offer only when snapshot.status !== 'pending'. /driver/status returns only the newest offer (services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:417-419 currentOfferFor, sorted by createdAt). When the old offer is expired by runRideTimers (rides.ts:810-814) and a new offer is created before the next 1.5 s poll, the client never sees the old offer's 'expired' status. The pending screen has only Accept, disabled when the time is up (:178), and Refuser (:179). Refuser then fails with OFFER_NOT_PENDING, which sets the sticky localPhase 'withdrawn' (:65) with the copy 'La passagère a annulé sa demande avant votre réponse.' (:83).
   - Detail: The driver is stuck on a dead offer showing 'Délai écoulé' with no way back except Refuser. That path shows a false reason (passenger cancelled) and keeps the new offer hidden, because the watcher already marked it seen at (app)/_layout.tsx:29. The new offer then expires unseen. This is separate from offer-sticky-local-phase: it needs no accept error, only a newer offer arriving in the same poll window, which is likely at busy times.
   - Fix: Key the offer body by offer.id and switch to status.data.offer whenever its id differs and the local snapshot's deadline has passed. Give the expired state a back control, and map OFFER_NOT_PENDING to neutral copy ('Proposition plus disponible') unless the server says the passenger cancelled.

**Open questions raised**

- Should a driver be allowed to sign out, or go 'home', while a ride is active? What is the intended background-tracking behaviour when the ride screen is not open?
- Should 'Revenus nets' include rides whose card or wallet payment is pending or failed, or only confirmed money? The footnote and the number currently disagree.
- What is the intended offer timeout policy? Is a per-city admin setting (10–120 s) a real requirement, or should it be fixed at 30 s?
- Should Naya Famille trips interact with dispatch? For example: block normal offers before a scheduled family pickup, show the next family trip on the dashboard, notify the driver.
- Is the six-logo gallery meant for drivers at all, or only for internal review (admin/website)? Should it disappear once a concept is chosen?
- Should drivers rate passengers, and should passenger verification or rating appear on the offer card for a women-only service?
- Which vehicle types map to which service categories (e.g. who drives 'Naya Scooter')? Should eligibility be derived from the vehicle dossier?
- How should drivers manage payout accounts (RIB) in production? Through self-service with verification, or support only?
- Is the production EAS profile expected to work soon (it points to the placeholder https://api.naya.example)? Which demo surfaces must be removed before it does?
- For the uncommitted plugins/with-ios-build-paths.js and appleTeamId: should the driver app get the same change? (Not verified: the plugin file content was not reviewed in this area.)

<a id="admin"></a>

## Admin dashboard (apps/admin)

**Health 6/10.** The admin is a 3.9k-line Vite 8 + React 19 + React Router 7 app with route-level code splitting. All server data goes through TanStack Query against the demo API. The A00–A13 core is well built: fine-grained admin permissions (the server checks almost every admin endpoint), money kept as integer centimes and basis points, version checks with conflict banners for verification decisions and city rules, an Idempotency-Key and a two-step confirm on finance corrections, a required reason on every city/zone/rules/provider change, and a hash-chained audit log shown inline on detail pages. The weak spots are new or cross-cutting. (1) The shared Dialog focus trap re-runs on every re-render, so typing into any dialog field that is not the first one sends the keystrokes to the first field; I reproduced this in headless Chromium against the real component, and it affects verification refusals, ticket resolution, corrections and city dialogs. (2) Commit 89aae18 made "Rejetée" the default ticket outcome, so "Résoudre" now rejects by default, which should break e2e S14; the admin e2e suite has not been re-run since. (3) The approval "Note interne" is shown to the passenger. (4) SOS alerts exist only inside the config.edit-gated Services page, so the support agent cannot see them. (5) The 529-line Prototype.tsx Services page cannot take decimal amounts or comma-separated lists in its catalog editor, and it changes pricing, dispatch eligibility and family-driver assignment with no reason, confirmation or version check. Permission checks are also uneven: driver balances and commission figures reach roles without finance.read. Some UI claims overstate auditing: document views are said to be logged on the server, and they are not. Docs are stale: the screen captures predate the redesign, and /services is missing from SCREENS.md. The separate apps/web /backoffice duplicates most admin sections with no auth, no shared domain model and no decision on which backoffice is the real one.

**Strengths**

- Clear role model: AdminPermission strings (packages/domain/src/entities.ts:53-61). requirePermission enforces them on the server for almost every admin endpoint (services/demo-api/src/app.ts:675-891, services/*.ts), and an explicit A00-denied page names the missing permission (apps/admin/src/components/Layout.tsx:152-176)
- Version checks with clear conflict banners for verification decisions (CaseReview.tsx:43,50-55,92-96) and rule edits (Rules.tsx:131,141-145,165)
- Finance corrections follow good practice: finance.correct permission, reason ≥10 chars, two-step confirm showing balance before/after, Idempotency-Key header (Finance.tsx:170-254)
- Every city/zone/rules/provider change requires a written reason and is audited. The rules editor previews fares with the real pricing engine (computeFare) and shows a readable diff before saving (Rules.tsx:85-104,214-245)
- Audit log is hash-chained and checked on every read, with an integrity badge and a before/after/hash drawer. Audit history is embedded in case, person, ride and ticket pages (Audit.tsx)
- Private documents are fetched with the bearer token and shown through blob URLs, never public links. The server sniffs magic bytes and rejects SVG uploads (lib/api.ts:58-63, services/demo-api/src/services/uploads.ts:10-35)
- Money is always integer centimes and basis points, with a single parse helper for comma or dot input (lib/format.ts:17-24)
- Route-level code splitting with Suspense skeletons. Lists show consistent loading, empty and error states
- Accessible primitives: labelled Field with aria-describedby and role=alert errors, sr-only table captions, aria-sort, a focus-visible outline, and reduced-motion handling (ui.tsx, index.css:8,22-30)
- People filters are debounced and synced to the URL (People.tsx:16-33)
- E2E covers login, permission denial, session expiry, the verification flows, ticket resolution, city creation, dynamic pricing, corrections and providers (e2e/tests/admin-*.spec.ts)

**Findings (critical / high / medium)**

1. **Typing in any dialog field after the first one is redirected to the first field** — 🔴 critical · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/components/ui.tsx:305-331: useFocusTrap's effect depends on [open, onEscape], and :334 passes a new inline arrow `() => !busy && onClose()` on every render. So on every keystroke the cleanup refocuses the previous element (:327) and setTimeout re-focuses focusables()[0] (:312). Reproduced in headless Chromium with the real Dialog/Input/Textarea components: after keyboard.type('Prix conforme') into the 2nd field, state was {"outcome":"Rejetéerix conforme","note":"P"} and activeElement was the first field. Affected dialogs: Ticket.tsx:115-127 (Issue input before Note), CaseReview.tsx:229-258 (reason select or checkboxes before message), Finance.tsx:224-238 (driver select before…
   - Detail: Admins cannot type normally in the reject dialog, the more-info dialog, the ticket resolution note, the correction amount and reason, or the city/zone/status forms. Keystrokes land in the first field and corrupt it (outcome text, or the selected option through native select type-ahead). The result can be wrong data sent to users: a garbled outcome, or a different rejection reason or driver.
   - Fix: Keep onEscape in a ref and give the trap effect only [open] as a dependency. Focus the first element only on open and restore focus only on close. Add one e2e that uses keyboard.type/pressSequentially in a multi-field dialog.
2. **'Résoudre' now rejects tickets by default (regression from 89aae18)** — 🟠 high · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Ticket.tsx:12 OUTCOMES[0]='Rejetée'; Ticket.tsx:22 useState(OUTCOMES[0]); services/demo-api/src/services/support.ts:85 sets status 'rejected' when the outcome matches /^(rejected\|rejetée)$/i. Per `git log -S"Rejetée"`, commit 89aae18 prepended 'Rejetée'. e2e/tests/admin-operations.spec.ts:16-21 (S14) expects 'Résolue · Tarif confirmé' and status 'resolved'. The last Playwright report (e2e/playwright-report, 2026-10-06 06:06) contains a single test (the feedback scenario), so the admin suite was not re-run.
   - Detail: An agent who opens 'Résoudre la demande ?', writes a note and confirms actually rejects the passenger's ticket, with the banner 'Rejetée · Rejetée'. Whether a ticket is rejected is inferred from free text, so 'Rejeté' or 'Rejet' counts as resolved.
   - Fix: Make Résoudre and Rejeter two explicit actions, or use a required select with no default. Send an explicit status/outcome code instead of matching on text. Re-run the full admin e2e suite and keep it in CI.
3. **SOS alerts sit in the Services page behind config.edit, so support cannot see or act on them** — 🟠 high · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/router.tsx:75 gates /services with config.edit; services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:878 and :928 require config.edit for GET /admin/prototype and POST /admin/prototype/alerts/:id; services/demo-api/src/seed.ts:301 shows the support agent lacks config.edit. Searching apps/admin/src finds no SOS reference outside pages/Prototype.tsx:238-300. Prototype.tsx:262-290 shows 'Prendre en charge'/'Résoudre' whatever a.status is, with hard-coded notes and one shared busy spinner. Location is shown as raw lat/lng (:258-260), with no map, nav badge or Overview card.
   - Detail: For a women-only service, the safety queue is buried in a configuration tab only an administrator can open, polled only while that tab is visible, and handled with canned notes. This contradicts the support role's purpose and the 'Admin can take ownership' claim in docs/MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md:13.
   - Fix: Add a dedicated Sécurité/SOS queue page with its own permission (for example safety.respond) granted to support. Add a nav badge and an Overview card. Allow only valid state transitions, require a note, and show a map.
4. **Services catalog editor cannot take decimal amounts or comma-separated lists** — 🟠 high · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:491-501 displays String(Number(row[name])/100), and :512-517 stores Math.round(Number(val)*100). Typing '2.' re-renders as '2', so the decimal point disappears, and '2,5' becomes NaN. :507-511 runs split(',').map(trim).filter(Boolean) on every keystroke, so 'rabat,' collapses to 'rabat' and a second city id or feature cannot be typed. Enums (icon/category/kind) are free-text inputs (labels at :39,50,52) validated only on the server (services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:41,63,70). The id field stays editable on existing rows, and the server upserts by id (prototype.ts:906-908), so editing an id creates a duplicate.
   - Detail: Category fares and commissions, plan prices and city lists, all of which feed live quotes and dispatch, can only be entered by pasting whole values. A typo produces NaN and a server error.
   - Fix: Replace the generic Object.entries form with typed per-kind forms: string state plus toCentimes for money, selects for enums and city ids, a read-only id when editing, and client-side zod validation.
5. **City commission set on the Rules page no longer applies to app rides; the always-set category commission overrides it** — 🟠 high · bug · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:127 `commissionBp: quote.service?.commissionBp ?? rules.commissionBp`; rides.ts:85 always sets service.commissionBp when a category is given. The category commission is required, not nullable (services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:45; packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:203 standard=1500, :217 premium=1800). The passenger app always sends a category: apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:55 useState('standard') and :60 api.quotes.create(cityId, stops, categoryId). Casablanca city commission is 1800 (packages/domain/src/cities.ts:27), and e2e S15 asserts rule-commission '18' (e2e/tests/admin-operations.spec.ts:37).
   - Detail: Every ride booked from the passenger app now takes its commission from the global (not per-city) service category. In Casablanca, a Standard ride is charged 15%, while the Rules page shows and versions 18%. Its example 'Commission 18 % … net chauffeuse' (Rules.tsx:227) is wrong for real rides. Edits to the city commission go through versioning, a required reason and audit, but no longer affect any app ride. Driver net, debt and Overview commission KPIs all follow the category value. Finance and ops will see two commission sources of truth.
   - Fix: Make the category commission nullable, meaning it inherits the city value, or move per-category commission into the versioned CityRules (per city). Show the effective commission on the Rules page and on the ride detail. Add an API test showing that a city commission change affects a Standard quote.
6. **Clearing a category fare in the Services editor saves 0 MAD instead of falling back to the city fare** — 🟠 high · bug · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:470 dialog text 'Vide sur un tarif = tarif de la ville'. Prototype.tsx:512-517 returns null only when `val === '' && value === null`, where `value` is the original value. For a non-null original such as Scooter baseFare 600 (packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:190), clearing gives Math.round(Number('')*100) = 0. The server accepts 0 (prototype.ts:26 cents min(0)). rides.ts:79 uses `category.baseFare ?? city.rules.baseFare`, and 0 does not fall back. packages/domain/src/pricing.ts:19-25 then computes max(0+0+0, 0) = 0.
   - Detail: An admin who empties the Scooter or Confort fare fields to 'use the city fare', as the dialog tells them to, sets that category's base, per-km, per-minute and minimum fares to 0. Passengers are then quoted 0 MAD (or only the parts left non-zero) for that category, and the change saves without any confirmation, diff or reason.
   - Fix: Keep string state per field. Treat an empty value as null for every nullable fare field, whatever its initial value. Parse with toCentimes. Show a fare preview (as on the Rules page) and reject a zero minimum fare.
7. **The approval 'Note interne (facultative)' is shown to the passenger** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/CaseReview.tsx:256 labels the approval textarea 'Note interne (facultative)'; services/demo-api/src/services/verification.ts:193-196 stores input.message as decision.message; apps/passenger/src/app/(verify)/verification-status.tsx:49 renders c.decision?.message as the welcome hero text (the driver app does the same in apps/driver/src/components/Dossier.tsx:200,212).
   - Detail: Reviewers will write internal remarks (for example 'photo floue, à surveiller') that the passenger then sees as her welcome message.
   - Fix: Split the decision into internalNote (audit only) and userMessage, or relabel the field 'Message à la personne (facultatif)'.
   - Verifier: Real for the passenger. CaseReview.tsx:256 labels the field 'Note interne (facultative)', verification.ts stores input.message as decision.message, and the passenger app's verification-status.tsx:49 renders it as the 'Bienvenue sur Naya' hero. The driver claim is wrong: Dossier.tsx:200 and :212 render decision.message only for more_info_requested and rejected, not for approval. The leak is limite…
8. **The 'Remboursement accordé' outcome tells the user she was refunded, but no money moves** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Ticket.tsx:12 offers 'Remboursement accordé'; services/demo-api/src/services/support.ts:80-92 resolveTicket only sets status/resolution, posts the message 'Demande résolue : Remboursement accordé. …' and appends audit. There is no ledger, payment or wallet operation.
   - Detail: This is a misleading payment claim to passengers, and finance has no record of the promised refund.
   - Fix: Remove or rename the outcome, or connect it to a real refund flow (passenger wallet credit or provider refund) with an amount, the finance permission and an idempotency key.
   - Verifier: Real: support.ts:79-92 only changes status, resolution, message and audit. Grepping services/demo-api/src, packages/api/src and packages/domain/src for 'refund\|rembours' finds no refund or passenger-credit capability anywhere. However, the outcome is a free-text label the agent chooses, not something the system asserts, and an agent could refund out of band. The real gap is that no refund tool e…
9. **Service-category pricing and commission changes skip the reason/confirm/version pattern used for city rules** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: Category overrides drive quotes (services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:79) and dispatch eligibility (rides.ts:262,370). PUT /admin/prototype/catalog/:kind (prototype.ts:890-924) needs no reason or expectedVersion, and its audit via log() carries no before/after/reason (prototype.ts:269-288). Compare with Rules.tsx:260-278 and the Cities reason dialogs (Cities.tsx:92-96).
   - Detail: Two governance models exist for pricing configuration. Catalog edits are not explained, cannot be diffed in the audit drawer, and can overwrite concurrent edits silently.
   - Fix: Apply the rules pattern (diff, reason ≥10 chars, expectedVersion, before/after in audit) to every catalog write, or fold category pricing into versioned CityRules.
10. **Safety-relevant Services actions apply instantly, with no confirmation or reason** — 🟡 medium · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: The family dedicated driver is reassigned on <select> change (apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:314-320). The dropdown lists every city driver (:322-328), but the server accepts only verified, active drivers (services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:957-975), so wrong picks end in error toasts. Driver category checkboxes post on change (Prototype.tsx:206-225), and unchecking every box leaves the driver with no offers (rides.ts:262).
   - Detail: Changing who transports a child is a single accidental select change, with no reason recorded.
   - Fix: Open a confirm dialog with a required reason. Only list eligible drivers. Warn before removing a driver's last category.
11. **Prototype.tsx is one 529-line page mixing 9 unrelated concerns, with inconsistent naming** — 🟡 medium · maintainability · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:69-71 and :101-125 hold the tab union and nav. One file covers catalogs, driver categories, families, SOS, passenger wallets and logos (:127-426). The feature has four names: Prototype.tsx / PrototypePage, route 'services' (router.tsx:75), 'Services et familles' (Layout.tsx:22, which reuses the Users icon from :16), and api.adminPrototype (packages/api/src/client.ts:462). Mutations use a hand-rolled run()/busy (:76-89) instead of useMutation. There is no loading state, only isError (:127). The title can read 'undefined · plan' (:309). Formatting (Prettier multi-line) differs from the rest of the admin's single-line style.
   - Detail: The page is hard to review, test or extend, and its naming hides that it holds production-relevant operations (SOS, family assignment, pricing).
   - Fix: Split into pages/services/{Catalogs,DriverCategories,Families,Safety,PassengerWallets}.tsx with typed sub-routes. Use useMutation per action. Move the logo gallery to a design page. Pick one name across route, file and API, and add a formatter config.
12. **Driver balances and commission KPIs reach roles without finance.read** — 🟡 medium · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/People.tsx:71 renders balances from GET /admin/people, which returns wallet for every driver regardless of permission (services/demo-api/src/app.ts:688). The profile hides the same data (app.ts:700,706; Person.tsx:71,97 'Les montants sont masqués pour votre rôle'). Overview.tsx:37-40 shows volume and commissions, and GET /admin/overview only checks for an admin session (app.ts:647-649). The support role has no finance.read (services/demo-api/src/seed.ts:301).
   - Detail: Role boundaries are applied inconsistently, so the 'masked for your role' message is misleading.
   - Fix: Filter wallet fields and financial KPIs on the server by finance.read, and render them conditionally in the UI. Add an e2e asserting the support role sees no amounts.
13. **'À traiter' queue and Overview count include rejected and awaiting-user tickets** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Support.tsx:26 sends status=open; services/demo-api/src/app.ts:801 treats open as status !== 'resolved', which includes 'rejected' and 'awaiting_user'. The Overview openTickets count uses the same rule (app.ts:667, shown at Overview.tsx:40), while Ticket.tsx:31 treats rejected as closed.
   - Detail: Rejected tickets stay in the work queue forever and inflate the 'demande(s) support' count. The 'Réponse attendue' segment overlaps with 'À traiter'.
   - Fix: Define open as open\|in_progress (excluding awaiting_user, resolved and rejected) in a single shared domain helper.
14. **Correction Idempotency-Key stays the same after the admin edits driver or amount** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Finance.tsx:173 memoizes the key once per dialog; :216 'Modifier' returns to the form where driver, direction and amount can change; :184 sends the same key. services/demo-api/src/services/finance.ts:291-298 returns the original entry on duplicate without comparing the payload.
   - Detail: If the first confirm timed out but succeeded (client timeout is 15s), a retry with a different driver or amount silently replays the first correction, and the toast reports success for an amount that was never posted.
   - Fix: Derive a new key whenever the confirmed payload changes. Have the server reject key reuse with a different payload (409) and store a payload hash.
15. **Full-admin demo credentials are rendered on the login page in every build** — 🟡 medium · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Login.tsx:11-14 hard-codes the passwords; :56-74 renders them unconditionally. README.md:145-146 repeats them.
   - Detail: Any deployed admin is effectively open to anyone who loads the page, including config, finance-correction and identity-decision rights.
   - Fix: Gate the list behind import.meta.env.DEV or an explicit VITE_DEMO_ACCOUNTS flag, and document that it must be off for any shared deployment.
16. **At 1024px, key table columns (status, amount) are pushed off-screen** — 🟡 medium · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/components/Layout.tsx:110 (lg:pl-[280px]) and :141 (lg:px-10) leave about 664px of content at 1024px. Rides has 7 columns (Rides.tsx:63-71). docs/screens/admin/A06-rides-1024.png shows Statut and Montant cut off behind horizontal scroll.
   - Detail: The most important columns are hidden on laptop-size screens, with no visible scroll affordance.
   - Fix: Collapse the sidebar to icons between 1024 and 1279px, choose column priorities per table, and pin the identifier and amount columns.
17. **Passenger stored value is missing from Finance and Person pages** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Finance.tsx:15,40-43 only model DriverRow. Person.tsx:70 shows the wallet card only for drivers. Passenger wallets appear only in Prototype.tsx:378-399, behind config.edit, with no totals or ledger.
   - Detail: Prepaid passenger money (a liability) is invisible to finance roles and cannot be reconciled or corrected.
   - Fix: Add passenger wallets to Finance (totals, ledger, pending top-ups) under finance.read, and to the passenger profile.
18. **Admin cannot suspend accounts or act on live rides** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Person.tsx:30 shows a 'Compte suspendu' badge but offers no action. Searching services/demo-api finds 'suspend' only in eligibility checks (services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:213,219). Ride.tsx is read-only, polling every 5s (:14).
   - Detail: Safety incidents and disputes cannot be acted on from the back office.
   - Fix: Add audited suspend/reactivate with a reason, and decide which live-ride interventions (cancel, reassign, contact) the admin needs.
19. **UI claims document views and every action are audited, but they are not** — 🟡 medium · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/components/DocViewer.tsx:44 says 'accès journalisé côté serveur', yet readUpload and GET /uploads/:id write no audit (services/demo-api/src/services/uploads.ts:56-70; app.ts:307-312). Login.tsx:41 says 'Chaque action est inscrite au journal d’audit', but startReview (services/demo-api/src/services/verification.ts:158-170) and failed admin logins (services/demo-api/src/services/auth.ts:133-135) are not audited.
   - Detail: These are false compliance claims about access to identity documents, which matters under Moroccan data-protection law 09-08.
   - Fix: Audit document views (actor, upload, purpose), review start and failed logins, or soften the copy until they are.
20. **Mobile/tablet menu overlay: no focus trap or Escape, and a near-invisible close button** — 🟡 medium · a11y · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/components/Layout.tsx:119-133: the overlay has no role=dialog/aria-modal, no Escape handler and no focus trap. The close IconButton (:125) inherits text-ink #29232D (ui.tsx:42; packages/tokens/tailwind-preset.js:7) on #302532, about 1.1:1 contrast. The Lock icon uses text-muted on the dark background (:44), and section labels use text-white/45 (:30).
   - Detail: Below 1024px, keyboard and low-vision users cannot reliably close the menu or read restricted markers.
   - Fix: Reuse the Drawer component for the mobile nav, give its icons text-white, and raise label contrast to at least 4.5:1.
21. **apps/web /backoffice duplicates most admin sections with a separate, unauthenticated, localStorage-only model** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/web/components/backoffice.tsx:53-59,310-314 has nav entries Vue d’ensemble, Courses, Chauffeuses, Passagères, Vérifications, Finance, Support, Journal and Paramètres. apps/web/README.md:18-20 says 'deliberately open', localStorage Zustand 'naya-web-prototype-v1', 'not connected to the existing demo API'. apps/web imports nothing from @naya/* and has its own string statuses (apps/web/lib/store.ts:34,47) and its own commission setting (:331). README.md:190 presents both.
   - Detail: There are two diverging backoffices with different terms (Journal d’activité vs Journal d’audit), statuses, permissions (none vs RBAC) and features (CSV export and Leaflet maps only in web; audit chain, versioned rules and idempotent corrections only in admin). Stakeholders may review the wrong one, and fixes will not carry over.
   - Fix: Write an ADR naming apps/admin the canonical operations console. Relabel or remove the web /backoffice (or keep it as a design exploration behind auth), and port its useful ideas (CSV export, work queue, maps) into apps/admin.
22. **Admin docs and captures are out of date** — 🟡 medium · docs · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: docs/screens/admin/*.png were last changed in f0f8d46 (2026-09-30), before the dark-sidebar redesign 0e0b066 (2026-10-01); for example docs/screens/admin/A06-rides-1024.png shows the old light sidebar. docs/SCREENS.md:53-70 lists A00–A13 but not /services. README.md:170 claims admin 11 tests '39 / 39', which S14 now contradicts. VITE_API_URL (apps/admin/src/lib/api.ts:5) is undocumented, and there is no apps/admin README.
   - Detail: New sessions with Claude or Codex will build on outdated screenshots and claims.
   - Fix: Re-capture admin screens, add /services and its permission to SCREENS.md, document environment variables and roles in an apps/admin README, and update the verification table only after a full run.
23. **No unit or component tests in admin, and e2e masks keyboard bugs** — 🟡 medium · testing · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/package.json:6-11 has no test script. Untested logic includes toCentimes/fromCentimes (lib/format.ts:17-25), fromForm/changes (Rules.tsx:46-104), rectangle (Cities.tsx:16-25) and the CatalogEditor parsing (Prototype.tsx:503-519). E2E uses fill() (e2e/tests/admin-operations.spec.ts:13,17). The last Playwright report contains 1 test. There is no ESLint or Prettier config in the repo, although People.tsx:31 carries an eslint-disable comment.
   - Detail: The regressions above (default rejection, focus trap, catalog parsing) would have been caught by small tests.
   - Fix: Add vitest plus Testing Library for admin pure functions and dialogs, use keyboard.type in at least one e2e per dialog, run the admin e2e project in CI, and add ESLint (react-hooks) and Prettier.
