**Open questions raised**

- Which backoffice is canonical going forward, apps/admin or apps/web /backoffice? Should the web one be deleted, relabeled as a design exploration, put behind auth, or connected to the demo API?
- Should support agents handle SOS and safety alerts? If so, which new permission (for example safety.respond), and should alerts surface on the Overview and in the nav?
- Should roles without finance.read see driver balances (People list) and commission and volume KPIs (Overview)?
- Will the admin ever be deployed somewhere reachable by others (for example Vercel)? If so, the demo credential list and production sourcemaps must be gated.
- Should the 'Remboursement accordé' ticket outcome trigger a real refund or passenger-wallet credit with an amount, or be removed?
- Should service-category pricing and commission be folded into versioned CityRules, or get its own versioning, reason and confirmation flow?
- Should viewing identity documents be audited, to make the DocViewer claim true and satisfy law 09-08? Should support agents see identity document numbers at all?
- Is 'Aujourd’hui' meant as the calendar day in Africa/Casablanca rather than a rolling 24h window?
- Is tablet or mobile (<1024px) a supported admin form factor, or only 1024+ desktop?
- Which admin interventions are in scope for launch: account suspension and reactivation, live-ride cancellation or reassignment, contacting the parties?
- I could not run typecheck or e2e under the read-only rules. Should the admin e2e suite be run now to confirm the expected S14 failure and catch any other regressions from 89aae18?

<a id="web"></a>

## Website + backoffice prototype (apps/web)

**Health 5/10.** apps/web is a good-looking Next.js 16 marketing landing page plus an open `/backoffice`. It ships as two large client components (landing.tsx is 881 lines, backoffice.tsx 1,754) backed by one Zustand store saved to localStorage. It does not use the demo API or any @naya/* package, so it re-implements prices, money, statuses, verification and scheduling. Several of those copies break the domain rules: pricing is a made-up formula based on list position, commission and money use floating-point numbers, verification needs 3 documents where the domain needs 7, and scheduling allows 1 minute of notice where the domain requires 30. It is also a second backoffice next to apps/admin, which has login, permissions and all the Oct-6 features. Most accessibility basics are solid: focus trapping, inert backgrounds, Escape handling, reduced motion, skip links, and Leaflet maps that load lazily and safely. Most items in UI-UX-AUDIT.md really are fixed in the code. The main problems are in data and logic. The seed data is pinned to 2026-10-05, so the default "Aujourd'hui" overview and the Finance page are now empty. The saved data has no schema version. `crypto.randomUUID` crashes every action when the site is opened over plain http on the local network. The Playwright spec is out of date and cannot pass. Tooling is mixed: npm and pnpm, two lockfiles, root `dev`/`build` scripts that only run the website, and a dev log full of module-resolution errors. The docs are thorough but stacked in layers, contain absolute /Volumes links, and no ADR records the two-backoffice decision.

**Strengths**

- Careful dialog and drawer accessibility: focus moves in, Tab is trapped, Escape closes, the background becomes inert, and focus returns to the trigger (apps/web/components/ui.tsx:86-178, 7-59; backoffice.tsx:253-262,340).
- Leaflet is safe for server rendering: a type-only import, a dynamic import('leaflet') once the map scrolls into view, ResizeObserver cleanup, mouse-wheel zoom disabled, keyboard markers, and popups built with textContent so there is no HTML injection (apps/web/components/map.tsx:5,44-86,125-150).
- The tile provider can be swapped with NEXT_PUBLIC_MAP_TILE_URL / NEXT_PUBLIC_MAP_ATTRIBUTION, and the OSM tile-policy caveat is documented (map.tsx:27-31; README.md:30).
- Reduced motion is handled both in GSAP (gsap.matchMedia with 'no-preference') and in CSS overrides; Leaflet animations also check the preference (landing.tsx:92-125; globals.css:3908-3922; map.tsx:96,167).
- The prototype is consistently honest: DÉMO labels, map disclaimers, fictional-document notices, 'Aucun paiement' notes and the legal modal (landing.tsx:246-249,362-365; map.tsx:216-220; operations.tsx:186-211).
- The store is small and readable. Mutations write audit events, cancellation and rejection reasons are stored, and approval is blocked until every document is accepted (lib/store.ts:353-414), with unit tests covering this (tests/store.test.mjs).
- The work queue sorts by urgency then age and expands to every pending item, which fixes the original audit finding (operations.tsx:55-64,115-124).
- Images use next/image with AVIF/WebP, explicit sizes and preload on the hero (next.config.ts:5; landing.tsx:253-260,478-484).
- CSV exports follow the current section and filters and use a UTF-8 BOM with ';' so French Excel opens them correctly (ui.tsx:226-246; backoffice.tsx:172-245).
- Design decisions, the audit and its follow-up are written down in detail (apps/web/DESIGN.md, UI-UX-AUDIT.md:118-164).

**Findings (critical / high / medium)**

1. **Seed data is pinned to 2026-10-05, so the default overview (and Finance) are empty on any later day** — 🟠 high · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/web/lib/store.ts:67-171 (every seed ride has date '2026-10-05' or '2026-10-04'); apps/web/components/operations.tsx:21-22 (hardcoded 2026-10-05 ticket timestamps); apps/web/components/backoffice.tsx:80 (period defaults to 'today'), 144-153 (today = current Africa/Casablanca date; 'week' = last 7 days), 488-512 and 527-546 (KPIs, chart and donut use periodRides/completed). Today (2026-10-13) the 'today' and 'week' filters both match zero seeded rides.
   - Detail: Opening /backoffice now shows 00 completed rides, 0 MAD, an empty chart and an empty donut. Data only appears under 'Tout l'historique'. Because the seeds are copied into localStorage on first visit, clearing storage does not help. Demos look broken and operators cannot see the intended story.
   - Fix: Generate seed dates and times relative to 'now' in Africa/Casablanca when seeding or resetting (e.g. today minus N minutes). Add an in-app 'Réinitialiser la démo' action. Default the overview to a period that has data, or show an explicit empty-state hint pointing to 'Tout l'historique'.
2. **Two diverging operator consoles: apps/web /backoffice (open, localStorage) and apps/admin (login, RBAC, demo API)** — 🟠 high · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/router.tsx:42 (auth redirect to /connexion) and 58-78 (verifications/:id, personnes, courses/:id, support/:id, finance, villes/:id/regles, services, paiements, audit); apps/web/components/backoffice.tsx:52-74 (overview, courses, chauffeuses, passageres, verifications, finance, support, parametres, journal); apps/web/components/landing.tsx:197-204 and 573-575 (public landing links to /backoffice); README.md:12-16 (layout omits apps/web) and 190; docs/MOBILE-FEEDBACK-AUDIT.md:7 (apps/web explicitly out of scope).
   - Detail: None of the Oct 6 features (service categories, Naya Famille, passenger wallet, SOS, disputes, the Services page) exist in the web backoffice. Its vocabulary, verification model and finance math also differ from the admin. The public landing page advertises the less capable, unauthenticated console, and the root README does not list apps/web in its layout. No ADR records why both exist or which one is canonical.
   - Fix: Decide in an ADR: either (a) make apps/web marketing-only and link the demo to apps/admin, or (b) rebuild the web backoffice on @naya/api against the demo API. Until then, label it 'prototype visuel' and remove it from the public header.
3. **Every store mutation throws over plain-http LAN URLs because crypto.randomUUID needs a secure context** — 🟡 medium · bug · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: apps/web/lib/store.ts:291-295 (const event = ... id: crypto.randomUUID()), used by addRide (349), addDriver (377), verifyDriver (397-402), reviewDocument (413), setDriverOnline (420), replyTicket (438), resolveTicket (444), updateTicket (450-455), saveSettings (462).
   - Detail: crypto.randomUUID only exists in secure contexts (HTTPS or localhost). Opening the dev server from a phone at http://192.168.x.x:3000, the natural way to demo a mobile-first site, makes it undefined. Booking, driver applications, document review, support replies and settings then throw inside event handlers and silently do nothing.
   - Fix: Use a fallback id generator (crypto.getRandomValues-based, or `${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`), or a shared id helper. Add a note to the README for LAN testing.
   - Verifier: The mechanism holds. store.ts:292 calls crypto.randomUUID() inside event(), and every mutating action calls it, including updateRide at :358, which the auditor left out. randomUUID is [SecureContext], so it is undefined over plain http on a non-localhost host. The throw happens inside set(), so the action does nothing. It only matters in one setup, though: plain-http, non-localhost serving such a…
4. **The website re-implements pricing, money, statuses, verification and scheduling and contradicts packages/domain** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: apps/web/package.json:11-22 (no @naya/* dependencies); lib/store.ts:6-54 (own Ride/Driver/Ticket types with French-string statuses); landing.tsx:89 (price = 38 + \|indexOf(from)-indexOf(to)\| x 12) and 702 (duration = price/4) versus packages/domain/src/pricing.ts:10-36 computeFare and cities.ts:5-19 (Rabat base 10, 4/km, 2/min, minimum 30 MAD); landing.tsx:749-752 (schedule min = now + 1 min, no max) versus cities.ts:17 (minLeadMinutes 30, maxDaysAhead 7); store.ts:22-26 (3 documents) and verifyDriver 379-407 versus packages/domain/src/machines/verification.ts:16-20,54-55 (7 items, person + vehicle approvals); store.ts:330 (commission 15 as a global percent) versus cities.ts:10,27 (1500 bp…

Prices, minimum fares, scheduling windows, verification requirements and commission rates all differ from what the mobile apps, demo API and admin enforce. Every rule change has to be made twice and will drift. Stakeholders comparing the two surfaces will see conflicting numbers.
   - Fix: Add @naya/domain as a workspace dependency and import computeFare/splitFare, formatMoney, RIDE_STATUS_LABELS, the verification ITEM_LABELS/REQUIRED_ITEMS, city rules and service categories. Store amounts in centimes and rates in basis points. At minimum, make the landing estimate call computeFare with approximate distances between the 6 neighborhoods.
   - Verifier: Every cited divergence checks out: - apps/web/package.json has no @naya/* deps. - Pricing: price = 38 + \|Δindex\|×12 (landing.tsx:89) and duration = price/4 (702), vs computeFare (pricing.ts:10-36) with RABAT_RULES base 10 / 4 per km / 2 per min / minimum 30 (cities.ts:5-19). - Scheduling: min now+1 min with no max (749-752) vs minLeadMinutes 30 / maxDaysAhead 7. - Verification: 3 documents vs R…
5. **Finance 'Volume total' is silently filtered by the overview's hidden 'today' period** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/web/components/backoffice.tsx:80 (period state, default 'today'), 152 (completed = periodRides filtered to Terminée), 821 (<Finance rides={completed} .../>), 1273-1279 (label 'Volume total', note 'Courses terminées uniquement'), 220-229 (finance export also uses `completed`). The Finance section renders no period control.
   - Detail: Finance claims to show total volume and commissions but only shows rides completed today (Africa/Casablanca). It has no on-screen control or label saying so. Combined with the stale seed dates, it currently shows 0 MAD and 'Aucun mouvement'.
   - Fix: Give Finance its own explicit period selector and caption (e.g. 'Courses terminées · 7 derniers jours'). Do not reuse the overview's period state.
6. **Money display uses floats, mixed rounding and inconsistent separators (contradicts docs/FINANCE.md)** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/web/lib/store.ts:475-476 (money = Intl 'fr-MA', maximumFractionDigits 0 + ' MAD'); `node -e "new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-MA',{maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(12345)"` gives "12.345" and format(9.75) gives "10"; backoffice.tsx:1268-1291 (fee = total*commission/100 shown via money(), rounded to whole MAD), 1364-1366 (per-row `.toFixed(2)` + ' MAD' gives '11.70 MAD' with an English decimal point), 226-227 and 1326-1327 (CSV floats); docs/FINANCE.md:7-8 and packages/domain/src/money.ts:55-70 (centimes, half-away rounding, narrow-nbsp grouping, comma decimals).
   - Detail: On the same Finance screen, thousands use a dot ('12.345 MAD') while row decimals also use a dot ('11.70 MAD'), so amounts read ambiguously in French. The commission KPI (e.g. '38 MAD' for the 2026-10-05 seed set) does not match the sum of the rows (38.10). Amounts are binary floats and never centimes. SSR and the browser can also format Intl output differently.
   - Fix: Store centimes, compute with splitFare/applyBp and display with formatMoney from @naya/domain everywhere: KPIs, table and CSV.
7. **Persisted store has no version/migrate, no reset action and no cross-tab sync (lost updates)** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/web/lib/store.ts:467-471 (persist { name: 'naya-web-prototype-v1', partialize, skipHydration } with no version or migrate); ad-hoc compatibility shims for an older shape at store.ts:432 and operations.tsx:241 (`t.responses ?? (t.reply ? [...] : [])`); README.md:18 and landing.tsx:871-873 ('clear the site's local storage to reset').
   - Detail: Seed or schema changes (new fields, the date fix above) never reach browsers that already hold v1 data; the code already carries hand-written shape shims. zustand persist writes the whole state on every set and does not listen to `storage` events. A backoffice tab opened earlier will overwrite a booking made from the landing in another tab on its next action, so the booking is lost.
   - Fix: Add `version` + `migrate` (or reseed on version bump), a visible 'Réinitialiser la démo' button, and a `window.addEventListener('storage', () => useNaya.persist.rehydrate())` sync.

11. **npm-managed subproject inside the pnpm workspace: two lockfiles, version drift, root scripts that only run the web app, and broken dev sessions** — 🟡 medium · dx · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: package.json:22-23 ("dev": "npm run dev --prefix apps/web", "build": "npm run build --prefix apps/web"); README.md:190 ('Run `npm run dev` from the root ... `npm install --prefix apps/web`'); apps/web/README.md:7-14 (mixes pnpm web, npm --prefix and ../../node_modules/.bin/next); apps/web/package-lock.json resolves lucide-react 1.52.0 while pnpm-lock.yaml:616-660 resolves 1.49.0 (and pnpm-workspace.yaml:13 has a minimumReleaseAgeExclude policy for lucide-react@1.49.0 that npm bypasses); apps/web/node_modules has dangling bins (`node_modules/.bin/next -> ../next/dist/bin/next` missing, empty @types/@fontsource dirs); apps/web/.next-dev/dev/logs/next-development.log shows "Can't resolve 'next…
   - Detail: Root `pnpm dev` / `pnpm build` look like monorepo commands but only touch the website, via npm. Installing with npm creates a nested node_modules that conflicts with pnpm's hoisted layout, and the dev log shows that breaking a running dev server. The two lockfiles disagree on versions and the npm path skips the workspace's supply-chain policy.
   - Fix: Delete apps/web/package-lock.json and apps/web/node_modules. Use only `pnpm web` / `pnpm web:build`. Rename the root dev/build scripts (e.g. `dev:web`) or make `build` run `pnpm -r build`. Fix README.md:190 and apps/web/README.md.
12. **5,900 lines of hand-written CSS with a 184-selector override layer, hardcoded colors and three conflicting palettes** — 🟡 medium · maintainability · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/web/app/globals.css (4,126 lines; only 7 custom properties at :5-13); apps/web/app/refinements.css (1,750 lines, 'Audit follow-up' overrides; 184 of its selectors also exist in globals.css by script count); about 200 distinct hex literals across both files; dead selectors from removed designs (journey-preview, map-car, map-markers, priorities, priority-row, signature-car, stars, trust-avatar, manifesto, avatar-stack, help-card, car-note); tailwind.config.ts:6 (muted '#7A7178') versus globals.css:10 / refinements.css:3 ('--muted: #655863') versus packages/tokens/src/index.ts:11-12 (ink '#29232D', muted '#716977'); Tailwind is used only for preflight plus w-full/sr-only/text-right.
   - Detail: Any visual change needs both files checked, specificity fights are likely, and the website palette has drifted from the shared design tokens used by mobile and admin. Tailwind is configured but its theme is unused and contradicts the CSS.
   - Fix: Merge refinements.css into globals.css, delete dead selectors, and move colors and type sizes to CSS custom properties generated from @naya/tokens (or adopt its Tailwind preset). Either use Tailwind properly or drop it, keeping only a reset.

16. **Web Finance recomputes every past commission from the current global setting and ignores the cash-debt model** — 🟡 medium · bug · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: apps/web/lib/store.ts:273,331 (one global `commission: number` = 15) and 458-464 (saveSettings overwrites it). apps/web/components/backoffice.tsx:1268-1269 (fee = total*commission/100 at render), 1364-1366 (per-row commission and net from the current setting), 226-227 and 1326-1327 (CSV exports likewise), 1409 (Settings copy: 'Appliquée aux courses terminées dans la vue finance'), 1286-1299 ('Revenus chauffeuses' = total − fee; cash only noted as 'Inclus dans le volume total'). Contrast: packages/domain/src/entities.ts:278-286 (FrozenTerms.commissionBp, 'Commercial terms frozen at booking. Later rule changes never alter them.') and docs/FINANCE.md:29-34 (a cash ride creates a −commission wa…
   - Detail: Rides store no commission snapshot. If an operator changes the commission in Paramètres from 15 % to 20 % (or 0 %), every historical ride's commission, driver net and reconciliation CSV is rewritten. Naya's past revenue silently changes and earlier exports no longer match. The view also treats cash and card rides the same, as 'net to the driver'. In the real model, the driver keeps the cash fare and owes the commission as debt. Stakeholders comparing the web backoffice with the admin Finance page see different numbers for the same rides.
   - Fix: Snapshot commissionBp (and the gross fare in centimes) on each ride when it is created or completed, and compute splits with splitFare from @naya/domain. Show card net and cash commission debt separately, as docs/FINANCE.md does. Alternatively, drop the web Finance page and point to apps/admin once the canonical-console ADR is decided.

**Open questions raised**

- Is apps/web meant to become the production marketing site (naya domain), or stay a pitch prototype? That decides whether to fix SEO/performance or freeze it.
- Which backoffice is canonical for demos and future work: apps/admin (auth, RBAC, demo API, Services page) or apps/web /backoffice? Should the web one be removed or rebuilt on @naya/api?
- Should the website booking demo use the demo API or embedded domain logic (computeFare, service categories, Naya Famille) so web and mobile tell the same story?
- Which of the 6 new logo concepts (packages/assets) is final? The website still ships copies of the older lockup (public/images/logo.svg == packages/assets/brand/naya-lockup.svg).
- What is the intended wording for children in the FAQ, now that Naya Famille supports child trips with handover to authorized recipients?
- Was webpack chosen over Turbopack for a specific incompatibility (hoisted pnpm, Tailwind 3)? It is not documented anywhere.
- Is the website meant to support dark mode like the mobile apps (packages/tokens darkColors)?
- Hero crop and tablet composition (UI-UX-AUDIT P2 items) could not be checked statically; a browser pass at 375/768/1024/1440 is still needed, plus a production Lighthouse/CrUX run, since none has ever been recorded.

<a id="ui-kit"></a>

## Shared mobile UI kit (packages/ui)

**Health 6/10.** packages/ui is a source-only kit (main: src/index.ts, one barrel, ~5k LOC) shared by the passenger and driver Expo apps. It contains design primitives, ride and money components, the map adapter, session/query/theme infrastructure, and whole feature screens (auth, support, verification capture, dev launcher, and an 800-line prototype module for SOS, the passenger wallet and Naya Famille). The primitives are carefully built: icon buttons require an accessibilityLabel, Reduce Motion and Reduce Transparency are respected, motion uses tokens, money is in centimes with tabular numbers, the session lives in SecureStore, account caches are isolated, and financial actions go through a single-flight idempotency hook. Several high-severity problems remain. DateField erases its own input as you type, which affects the driver identity birth date. useSingleFlight's reset() rotates the idempotency key, and callers reset on error, so a retry after a timeout can repeat a withdrawal or recharge. The onboarding is unreadable in dark mode. The embedded demo backend and its fixture ID photos are statically bundled into every native build, production included. The simulated SOS, wallet and family screens are not gated by DEMO_MODE and are mounted on the live ride screens. Other gaps: dark-mode literals in the verification flows, toasts hidden under modal sheets, no iOS VoiceOver announcements, and a theming model that depends on every component calling useTheme(). Tests cover about 10 behaviours and miss DateField, session, theme, prototype, support and map. One tested component, WalletSummary, is not used by any app.

**Strengths**

- Accessibility is built into the primitives: IconButton requires accessibilityLabel (Button.tsx:131-133), ListRow keeps trailing controls outside the pressable to avoid nested buttons (List.tsx:79-90), hitSlopFor/minTouch give 44/48-pt targets (a11y.ts:25-31), and FieldRow and DocumentPicker switch to a column at large text sizes (Form.tsx:100-104, DocumentPicker.tsx:27-29).
- Reduce Motion and Reduce Transparency are respected across press, segmented, sheet, form-label, toast, skeleton, onboarding paging and map animations (PressableScale.tsx:25-37, Glass.tsx:21-27, useAnimatedCoordinate.ts:14).
- Money is shown from centimes through formatMoney with tabular numerals and a spoken minus sign (Ride.tsx:30-37). The fare breakdown shows every component and the surge reason before the total (Ride.tsx:151-169).
- useSingleFlight blocks same-frame double taps with a synchronous ref, and mutations are never auto-retried (useSingleFlight.ts:13-30, query.ts:33-38).
- The session token is kept in Keychain/Keystore with AFTER_FIRST_UNLOCK_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY and is never written to plain storage (session.ts:5-23). AccountBoundary clears only the previous account's cache and has a regression test (AccountBoundary.tsx:11-18, components.test.tsx:171-186).
- Picked images are resized to ≤1600 px and re-encoded as JPEG, which strips EXIF and location (uploads.ts:17-51).
- The map adapter falls back cleanly (Apple / Google / tiles), shows OSM attribution, and documents the production tile requirement (NayaMap.tsx:13-28, TileMap.tsx:17-24).
- Motion uses monotonic easing from shared tokens with no springs (motion.ts:5-14).
- Comments explain intent throughout, which makes the code readable for later agents.

**Findings (critical / high / medium)**

1. **DateField wipes its own digits as you type (driver identity birth date)** — 🟠 high · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/Form.tsx:131-135 `useEffect(() => { if (value && digitsFromIso(value) !== digits) setDigits(digitsFromIso(value)); }, [value]);` together with Form.tsx:144-148 `onChange(isoFromDigits(d) ?? d)`, which sends partial non-ISO digits to the parent. Consumer: apps/driver/src/app/docs/identity.tsx:45 `<DateField ... value={field.value} onChange={field.onChange} />` (react-hook-form echoes the value). No test covers DateField; e2e birth-date covers only the passenger FormField (e2e/tests/passenger-flows.spec.ts:43).
   - Detail: Typing '0' calls onChange('0'). The parent sets value='0'. The effect then compares digitsFromIso('0') (which is '', because it is not ISO) with digits '0' and calls setDigits(''), clearing the field. Each later keystroke is cleared the same way. Backspacing a saved ISO date clears the whole date. Only a one-shot paste or fill of 8 digits works, which is why web e2e would not catch it. This blocks the driver's identity details step on a real device keyboard.
   - Fix: Only resync from props when `value` is a complete ISO date that differs from isoFromDigits(digits), or keep the raw digits fully internal and emit '' until the date is complete. Add RNTL tests for typing one character at a time and for backspacing.
2. **Onboarding (first screen) is unreadable in dark mode** — 🟠 high · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/WelcomeJourney.tsx:33 root gradient `['#F1E6EF', '#FAF8FA', colors.background]` with locations [0, 0.55, 1]. :45 stage gradients are hard-coded light. :80 border '#FFFFFF'. :55-56 hero title tone ink and body tone muted resolve to darkColors.ink '#F4EDF5' and muted '#BBAFBE' (packages/tokens/src/index.ts:41). Both apps set userInterfaceStyle 'automatic' (apps/passenger/app.config.ts:12, apps/driver/app.config.ts:12) and use WelcomeJourney (apps/*/src/app/(auth)/welcome.tsx).
   - Detail: With the phone in dark mode (the default 'system' theme), the title sits at about 55% of the screen height, where the background is #FAF8FA. Near-white text on a near-white background is about 1.05:1 contrast, and the body text about 1.9:1. The stage label (accent #E5B6D4) on a light stage is also faint. This is the first screen every new user sees.
   - Fix: Derive the gradients from tokens (e.g. selected/mauveSoft to background) or add dark variants in tokens. Add a dark-mode native capture of welcome to the review set.
3. **useSingleFlight.reset() rotates the idempotency key, and callers reset on every error, so timeout retries can duplicate withdrawals and recharges** — 🟡 medium · bug · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/core/useSingleFlight.ts:5-10 promises that 'a retry after a timeout or a reconnect replays the same request'. :35-38 `reset` sets `key.current = newIdempotencyKey(); mutation.reset();`. Callers reset in onError: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/withdraw/index.tsx:33, apps/driver/src/app/(app)/recharge/index.tsx:37, apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/receipt/[id].tsx:55, apps/passenger/src/features/ride/CancelSheet.tsx:28. Server dedupe works only on the same key (services/demo-api/src/app.ts:133-136). The test (components.test.tsx:141-167) never calls reset.
   - Detail: reset() does two jobs: it clears the mutation's error state for the UI and it starts a new intent. Callers want the first and get the second. If a withdrawal or recharge reaches the server but the response is lost to a timeout, the error handler rotates the key and the next tap creates a second withdrawal or recharge. The hook's main guarantee is lost exactly in the case it exists for.
   - Fix: Split the API into clearError() (mutation.reset only) and newIntent() (rotate key). Rotate automatically after success and after definitive 4xx rejections, and keep the key after network or 5xx errors. Rotate when vars change, otherwise the server returns IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (app.ts:135). Update the 4 callers and add tests for timeout-then-retry and changed-amount-retry.
   - Verifier: The hook defect is real. useSingleFlight.ts:35-38 rotates the key on reset, and all 4 callers reset in onError (withdraw/index.tsx:33, recharge/index.tsx:37, receipt/[id].tsx:55, CancelSheet.tsx:28). The duplicate-money outcome does not happen on the demo server, because domain invariants block a second operation. Recharge throws PENDING_OPERATION when one is already pending (services/demo-api/sr…
4. **The embedded demo backend and fixture ID photos ship in every native build, production included** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/core/Providers.tsx:18 statically imports `./localDemo`, which resolves to packages/ui/src/core/localDemo.native.ts:1-3 (imports `@naya/demo-api/embedded` and `../../../../services/demo-api/src/fixtures.native`). Use is runtime-gated only (Providers.tsx:28). services/demo-api/src/fixtures.native.ts:3-19 requires 15 JPEGs (ID fronts and backs, selfies, licences). services/demo-api/src/embedded.ts:18-21 has `devMode: true`, `providerSecret: 'on-device-fictional-demo'`. packages/ui/package.json:12 depends on @naya/demo-api.
   - Detail: Metro cannot drop a statically imported module behind a runtime flag. The production EAS profile (apps/passenger/eas.json `production`: EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO=0) therefore still bundles Hono, the full demo rule engine, seed data, demo accounts and fixture identity images. This makes binaries larger, puts demo credentials and logic in a store binary, and couples the UI kit to the demo server: a production app cannot be built without services/demo-api. localDemo.native.ts also reaches into another package's src through a relative path, bypassing its exports map.
   - Fix: Move the embedded transport out of packages/ui into the apps (or a @naya/demo-embedded package). Select it at build time, e.g. a Metro resolver alias or a `localDemo.standalone.native.ts` chosen by an env-based resolver, so non-demo builds never import it. Export fixtures.native through demo-api's package exports. Remove @naya/demo-api from packages/ui dependencies.
   - Verifier: Confirmed: Providers.tsx:18 imports './localDemo' statically. localDemo.native.ts:2-3 pulls in @naya/demo-api/embedded and fixtures.native, which requires 15 JPEGs (fixtures.native.ts:3-19). apps/passenger/metro.config.js enables no tree-shaking, and packages/ui/package.json lists @naya/demo-api. But EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO=0 is inlined, so STANDALONE_DEMO is false and localDemoOptions is never called (…
5. **Simulated SOS, self-confirmed wallet top-ups and family simulations are not DEMO_MODE-gated and are mounted on the live ride screens** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:49-150 (SafetyButton: 'SOS · sécurité' opens 'SOS · démonstration', with simulated actions at :125-130 and no DEMO_MODE check). Mounted unconditionally at apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:173 and apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:214. prototype.tsx:264-276 shows 'Simuler la confirmation/le refus' of the passenger's own top-up. :659-663 lets the passenger 'Simuler les étapes de la chauffeuse'. Entry rows are ungated at apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/account.tsx:34-36. The server resolve route has no devMode guard (services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:494); passenger family advance is forbidden outside devMode (prototype.ts:659-660).
   - Detail: MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md defines these as prototype simulations. However, the components gate nothing themselves (contrast capture.tsx:160, which checks DEMO_MODE). In a build with EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO=0, a passenger or driver mid-ride would see an SOS control that calls nobody. That is a misleading safety affordance for a women's-safety product. The wallet screen would also offer a self-credit button.
   - Fix: Gate SafetyButton, PassengerWalletScreen simulations and the family 'simulate' controls on DEMO_MODE, or add a `simulated` prop. Outside demo mode, the SOS must at least offer a real `tel:` action (police / emergency) before any simulated flow. Gate the server routes on devMode too.
   - Verifier: The facts hold. SafetyButton has no DEMO_MODE check (prototype.tsx:49-150). It is mounted unconditionally at apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:173 and apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:214. The account rows (account.tsx:34-35) are ungated, and the server's wallet resolve route has no devMode guard (prototype.ts:494-517). However, the sheet labels itself honestly: 'SOS · démonstration' / 'Les…
6. **Identity verification surfaces have light-only literals that break in dark mode** — 🟡 medium · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/DocumentPicker.tsx:68 selected tile `backgroundColor: '#FDF9FB'` with label tone 'ink' (:50) = '#F4EDF5' in dark, so the selected label is invisible. packages/ui/src/features/capture.tsx:125 guide frame `backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)'` with muted guidance text (:127-129) gives about 1.4:1 over the dark background.
   - Detail: Both apps' identity screens (apps/passenger/src/app/(verify)/identity.tsx:79, apps/driver/src/app/docs/identity.tsx:39) and capture steps use these. Users in dark mode cannot read which document type is selected or what the photo guidance says.
   - Fix: Replace with tokens (e.g. colors.selected / colors.mauveSoft) and add a dark-mode snapshot of the verify flow.
7. **Toasts render beneath Modal-based sheets, so errors raised inside sheets are invisible** — 🟡 medium · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: ToastHost is mounted in the root tree at packages/ui/src/core/Providers.tsx:77. Sheet and ConfirmDialog use RN `<Modal>` (BottomSheet.tsx:65, :185). Errors inside the SOS sheet are reported only by toast (prototype.tsx:75-76).
   - Detail: RN Modal presents in a separate native window or view controller above the root view, so a toast fired while a Sheet is open is hidden behind it. The SOS confirm failure and any app sheet that relies on toast() show no feedback.
   - Fix: Render a ToastHost inside Sheet/ConfirmDialog, or show inline StatusBanner errors in sheets. Alternatively, implement sheets without RN Modal (portal plus overlay).
8. **Live-region announcements are Android-only; VoiceOver users miss toasts, countdowns and loading states** — 🟡 medium · a11y · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: Only `accessibilityLiveRegion` is used (6 occurrences, e.g. Toast.tsx:45, Ride.tsx:316, Button.tsx:97, Form.tsx:87). `grep announceForAccessibility` finds nothing in packages/ or apps/. Toast.tsx:31 says toasts 'announce themselves to screen readers'. docs/DESIGN.md:44 claims 'live regions for countdowns and errors'.
   - Detail: On iOS, accessibilityLiveRegion is ignored and role 'alert' does not trigger an announcement. Confirmations ('Demande envoyée'), the offer countdown every 10 s and OTP errors that appear without focus moving are not spoken.
   - Fix: Call AccessibilityInfo.announceForAccessibility (or announceForAccessibilityWithOptions with queue) in ToastHost, CountdownRing thresholds, Button loadingLabel and error captions. Add a test that mocks AccessibilityInfo.
9. **Theme switching depends on every component calling useTheme(), a mutable Proxy palette, and a per-access style factory; cold start flashes light** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/tokens/src/index.ts:48 `colors = new Proxy(...)` keyed on a module-level `scheme`. :50 `themedStyles` re-runs `factory()` (all StyleSheet.create entries) on every property access. Every component begins with a bare `useTheme();` (e.g. Text.tsx:25, Button.tsx:41, List.tsx:52); Text.tsx:26-35 shows codemod mis-indentation. prototype.tsx:34-47 needs a Proxy workaround for a style constant. ThemeController mounts only after fonts and session are ready (Providers.tsx:62-70) and loads the saved mode asynchronously (theme.tsx:37-48).
   - Detail: A component that reads `colors` without calling useTheme(), a memoized component, or a module-level constant (Material.tsx:64-73) keeps stale colours after a theme change, and nothing enforces this. Each style access allocates the whole stylesheet of the module (e.g. Form.tsx:324-340 has 15 entries, accessed about 6 times per FormField render). A persisted 'dark' choice is applied only after first paint, which causes a light flash on every cold start.
   - Fix: Record an ADR, then either move to a ThemeContext / useColors() hook returning memoized per-scheme StyleSheets (cache themedStyles output by scheme), or adopt NativeWind `dark:` variables. Load the saved theme before hiding the splash. Add a lint rule or test that flags `colors.` reads in components without useTheme.
10. **Demo and standalone flags default to ON when env vars are missing** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/core/apiBase.ts:4 `DEMO_MODE = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO !== '0'`, :5 `STANDALONE_DEMO = ... && process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_STANDALONE !== '0'`.
   - Detail: Any build without an explicit EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO=0 (a local release build, a new EAS profile, CI) becomes a standalone demo with fake data, the OTP prefilled and the dev launcher visible. Only the eas.json production profile sets it explicitly.
   - Fix: Make the flags opt-in (=== '1'), or fail the build in app.config.ts when the env is missing for store profiles. Document the flag matrix in docs/.
11. **VehicleImage always draws a plum car whatever the real vehicle colour, while labelling it with the real colour** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/Ride.tsx:173-176 passes `color` only to the label. packages/ui/src/Brand.tsx:25 renders `vehicleSvg(colors.accent, colors.surface)` for any car source. Used in DriverCard (Ride.tsx:206) on the passenger's live ride screen.
   - Detail: Passengers identify the car at pickup by plate, make and colour. Showing a plum car when the vehicle is 'Blanc' contradicts the text next to it and weakens a safety check.
   - Fix: Map vehicle.color to a fill (with a neutral fallback) in vehicleSvg, or show no colour-bearing art.
12. **Driver-marker interpolation re-renders the whole map on the JS thread every frame** — 🟡 medium · performance · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/map/useAnimatedCoordinate.ts:21-28 calls setValue on every requestAnimationFrame for 900 ms per location update. NayaMap.tsx:81-82 rebuilds both Polyline coordinate arrays inline on each render. TileMap.tsx:111 recomputes routePts each render.
   - Detail: About 54 React renders per GPS update re-send route polylines (hundreds of points) to react-native-maps, plus marker and Glass overlays. This causes jank and battery drain on the live trip screens that matter most, especially on Android.
   - Fix: Memoize polyline coordinates by route identity. Animate the marker natively (react-native-maps AnimatedRegion / MarkerAnimated, or a Reanimated shared value on the tile layer) instead of React state per frame.
13. **NayaMap refits the camera on every fitTo change with no 'user has panned' suppression** — 🟡 medium · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/map/NayaMap.tsx:36-52 refits whenever fitKey or center changes. apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:136 passes `fitTo={... car ? [car, pickup.location] ...}`, so each GPS tick changes fitKey. TileMap.tsx:35-46 behaves the same way.
   - Detail: While driving to pickup, every location update snaps the camera back, so the driver cannot pan to inspect the pickup area.
   - Fix: Track user gestures (onPanDrag / onRegionChange with isGesture) and pause auto-fit with a 'Recentrer' button. Debounce refits.
14. **Keyless Android and web use OSM public tiles with no pinch-zoom, and no build profile sets a Google key** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/map/TileMap.tsx:17-23 (default tile.openstreetmap.org; 'Production must use a licensed tile provider'). :86-96 has only pan and tap gestures, no pinch. NayaMap.tsx:13-16 selects tiles when `googleMapsConfigured` is false. GOOGLE_MAPS_ANDROID_KEY is read in apps/*/app.config.ts:3 but set in no eas.json profile.
   - Detail: Every Android build today, production included, would hit OSM's volunteer tile servers, which their usage policy forbids for apps. Users also cannot zoom the map.
   - Fix: Provision a Google Maps key (or a licensed tile provider via EXPO_PUBLIC_MAP_TILE_URL) for each EAS profile and add pinch / zoom buttons to TileMap. Fail release builds that would fall back to OSM.
   - Verifier: Confirmed: TileMap.tsx:23 defaults to tile.openstreetmap.org, and :86-96 has only Pan and Tap gestures (no pinch or zoom controls). NayaMap.tsx:13-16 selects tiles when googleMapsConfigured is false. The claim that 'every Android build, production included' uses OSM is not verifiable. GOOGLE_MAPS_ANDROID_KEY (app.config.ts:3) can come from EAS environment variables or secrets that would not appea…
15. **Documents are base64-encoded in JS with no client-side size limit** — 🟡 medium · performance · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/uploads.ts:22-27 accepts any PDF and :53-65 reads the whole file to base64 before upload. The server cap is checked only after transfer (packages/domain/src/schemas.ts:85 `max(14_000_000)`). normalizeImage decodes images twice (uploads.ts:44-48).
   - Detail: A large scanned PDF (20-40 MB) produces a 27-55 MB JS string. That risks out-of-memory on low-end Android, burns data on a Moroccan mobile plan, and ends in a generic validation error.
   - Fix: Check `asset.size` from DocumentPicker (≤10 MB) and show a French message before reading. Consider multipart or file upload instead of base64 JSON. Reuse the first ImageManipulator context for resizing.
16. **Ticket replies cannot include attachments, even when support asks the user for evidence** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/support.tsx:163 `api.support.message(ticketId, { body: text, attachments: [] })`. The Composer (:227-248) has no attach control. The 'awaiting_user' banner (:192) prompts a reply.
   - Detail: Disputes and evidence-required reasons (support.tsx:96) need photos. If support requests one later, the user has no way to send it in the thread.
   - Fix: Reuse the attach() logic from TicketCreateScreen in the Composer, limited to 3 files.
17. **Prototype actions create a fresh idempotency key per call, so retries create duplicates** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:33 `const key = () => \`demo-${Date.now()}-...\``, used for SOS (:72), top-up (:181), subscribe (:401) and family trip (:564). There is no useSingleFlight. All buttons share one `busy` flag (:63, :172, :310).
   - Detail: A double tap in the same frame or a retry after a timeout sends a new key each time. That can produce duplicate SOS alerts, top-ups, subscriptions or recurring trips. The shared busy flag also shows a spinner on every button at once.
   - Fix: Use useSingleFlight per action (after fixing its reset contract) and give each action its own pending state.
18. **features/prototype.tsx mixes three product domains, ad-hoc query keys and business rules in one 800-line shared file** — 🟡 medium · maintainability · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:49-150 SOS, :152-286 passenger wallet (passenger-only, yet in the shared kit), :288-800 family. Ad-hoc keys at :164, :167, :300, :307 instead of `qk`. The `panel` Proxy hack is at :34-47. Similar ad-hoc keys: features/support.tsx:71, features/Attachment.tsx:25, features/DevLauncher.tsx:26-27, features/auth.tsx:152.
   - Detail: Safety, payments and family each need their own hooks, query keys, schemas and tests before they become real features. Today none of this logic is tested, and any change risks the others.
   - Fix: Split into features/safety, features/passengerWallet and features/family, each with a hooks file using qk.* keys. Move passenger-only screens into apps/passenger. Replace `panel` with a themed style.
19. **UI kit tests cover about 10 behaviours and run only through the passenger app's Jest** — 🟡 medium · testing · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: Only packages/ui/src/__tests__/components.test.tsx (187 lines). packages/ui/package.json:7-9 has no test or lint script. Tests run because apps/passenger/jest.config.js:5 adds `../../packages/ui/src` to roots. There are no tests for core/session.ts, core/theme.tsx, Form.tsx DateField/PhoneField, BottomSheet, Toast, features/* (auth, support, capture, prototype), uploads.ts, or map/projection.ts (pure functions).
   - Detail: The DateField bug and the useSingleFlight reset contract would both have been caught by small tests. Pure projection/fitZoom math and session restore (corrupt JSON) are cheap to test.
   - Fix: Give packages/ui its own jest config and `test` script (so `pnpm -r test` runs it independently). Add tests for DateField typing, session restore/signOut, theme persistence, useSingleFlight newIntent semantics, fitZoom, and SupportCreate evidence-required validation.
20. **The UI kit declares none of its ~30 runtime dependencies, imports another package's src by relative path, and type-checks against the passenger app** — 🟡 medium · maintainability · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/package.json:10-15 lists only @naya/* workspace deps, yet src imports expo-*, react-native-maps, reanimated, zustand, @tanstack/react-query, lucide, etc. packages/ui/src/core/localDemo.native.ts:3 imports `../../../../services/demo-api/src/fixtures.native`. packages/ui/tsconfig.json:4 includes `../../apps/passenger/nativewind-env.d.ts`.
   - Detail: The kit works only because pnpm uses a hoisted nodeLinker and both apps happen to install every native module. A third consumer or a stricter linker breaks resolution, and native-module version drift between apps goes undetected.
   - Fix: Declare peerDependencies (RN, Expo modules, reanimated, query, zustand) with the versions the apps use. Export fixtures via demo-api package exports. Move the NativeWind type reference into packages/ui.
21. **Compact StatusBanner truncates title and message to 2 lines, cutting off correction notes and failure reasons** — 🟡 medium · ux · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/Feedback.tsx:41 compact Text has numberOfLines={2}. Dynamic server text passed to compact banners: packages/ui/src/features/capture.tsx:111 (reviewer correctionNote, e.g. seed.ts:308 'Le verso est coupé : reprenez la photo en ent…'), apps/driver/src/app/(app)/withdraw/[id].tsx:45 (`${failureReason} ${amount} à nouveau disponibles, aucun crédit ajouté.`), apps/driver/src/app/(app)/recharge/[id].tsx:61, apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/card/new.tsx:74 (error), apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:145 (driver cancellation reasonText), packages/ui/src/features/capture.tsx:166 (upload error).
   - Detail: The caption is 13 pt in about 290 pt of width, so two lines hold about 90 characters including the title. Reviewer correction notes, withdrawal failure explanations and error messages often exceed that and are cut with an ellipsis, at default text size and more at the 1.6× scale the kit allows. Sighted users lose why a document must be retaken or why money was not credited. Only the accessibilityLabel carries the full text.
   - Fix: Remove numberOfLines from compact banners, or use the full (non-compact) StatusBanner whenever the message is dynamic or server-supplied. Add a test that renders a long correction note.
22. **Sheet bodies are not scrollable, so tall or growing sheet content overflows off-screen at large text sizes** — 🟡 medium · a11y · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/BottomSheet.tsx:70 caps the sheet at maxHeight: height - insets.top - 24, but :87 renders children in a plain <View style={{ flexShrink: 1 }}> with no ScrollView and no overflow handling. 12 of 13 Sheet consumers contain no ScrollView (e.g. apps/passenger/src/features/ride/CancelSheet.tsx, packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx, apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx; only quote.tsx scrolls). The SOS sheet appends one log line per action without bound (prototype.tsx:139-143) under 4 buttons and 2 banners. Text scales up to 1.6× (Text.tsx:24).
   - Detail: With Dynamic Type enlarged, or as the SOS action log grows, content exceeds maxHeight. The shrunk container lets content spill below the screen edge and under the footer, which can make confirm and cancel actions unreachable. The safety and cancellation sheets are where that matters most.
   - Fix: Wrap Sheet children in a ScrollView (or the keyboard-aware variant) with flexShrink, keep the footer pinned, and add a large-font render test for CancelSheet and the SOS sheet.

**Open questions raised**

- I did not run typecheck or Jest, because the audit rules allow it only when the task says so. Please confirm that `pnpm --filter @naya/passenger test` passes and that DateField fails a one-character-at-a-time typing test on device.
- Is the eas.json 'production' profile (EXPO_PUBLIC_DEMO=0) ever built today? If it is, the SOS, wallet and family gating and the bundled demo backend become blocking. If not, are they acceptable until a real backend exists?
- The emergency numbers in support.tsx:149 need checking. In Morocco, 15 is usually Protection Civile (fire/ambulance), SAMU is 141, and 112 works from mobiles. What exact numbers should the app show and dial?
- Should packages/ui's WalletSummary be deleted in favour of apps/driver WalletCard, or should WalletCard move into the kit?
- Should Illustration keep accepting raster asset ids, or should the PNGs in packages/assets be removed now that vector art is the product direction?
- Does the Rabat launch need Arabic (RTL) or Darija copy? All strings are hard-coded French and layouts assume LTR (e.g. ArrowLeft back icons, row directions).
- Will the demo-api embedded mode be kept long-term, for example for sales demos? That affects whether it should become a separate package selected at build time.
- Should useSingleFlight rotate keys automatically on success, or should callers keep that control? The 4 app callers that reset on error need a decision before the hook changes.

<a id="domain"></a>

## Domain, API client, tokens, assets (packages/domain, api, tokens, assets)

**Health 6/10.** The core of packages/domain is solid and matches docs/FINANCE.md. Money is stored as integer centimes with basis-point rates, rounding is half away from zero (divRound/applyBp), the fare formula and the Rabat/Casablanca fixtures (100 / 120 / 107 / 128,40 MAD) match, the driver wallet is a pure projection, and the debt block is inclusive. The ride, offer, verification, transfer and scheduled state machines are small declarative tables, and the 4 domain test files cover these finance and machine invariants. The newer prototype layer (service categories, Naya Famille, passenger wallet, SOS, disputes) sits outside that model and has three serious problems: 1) the app always sends categoryId 'standard', whose fixed 15 % commission overrides the city commission (Casablanca charges 15 % instead of 18 %, and admin commission edits have no effect); 2) the API sends child handover codes to the driver; 3) wallet and mobile_wallet rides plus passenger top-ups that the passenger confirms herself credit drivers outside the documented ledger rules. The ride and verification machines are missing a few real-world paths: passenger no-show, aborting a ride that is in progress, and revoking an approved verification. Passports also still require a back-side photo. The API client is compact and handles idempotency well (one key per user intent through useSingleFlight). However, it parses JSON without a guard, its response types are not shared with the server, and query keys for prototype features are ad hoc. In tokens, the dark palette has an entry for every token, but hard-coded light colors in shared UI break dark-mode contrast (down to 1.10:1). The Tailwind/NativeWind stack is wired into both mobile apps but no mobile code uses className. In assets, about 6.8 MB of generated raster PNG/JPG files are still required, and therefore bundled, but the mobile apps never render them, and the provenance docs contradict each other. Test suites were not run (the task was read-only); I checked pure-function behavior with standalone scratch copies of the code.

**Strengths**

- Money is integer centimes and integer basis points, with one rounding rule (half away from zero, once, where derived) applied by divRound/applyBp (packages/domain/src/money.ts:26-44). FINANCE.md fixtures NY-001/NY-002, the withdrawal and recharge sequences and the debt threshold are asserted in packages/domain/test/wallet.test.ts.
- The driver wallet is a pure projection over ledger entries and pending withdrawals (wallet.ts:23-40), so releasing a hold never needs a compensating credit.
- State machines are declarative transition tables behind one makeMachine helper that throws a typed INVALID_TRANSITION (machines/machine.ts:6-23), so illegal transitions fail the same way everywhere.
- Commercial terms (breakdown, commission, cancellation policy, dynamic reason) are frozen in FrozenTerms at booking (entities.ts:279-290).
- Offer countdowns use the server's expiresAt plus a clock-skew estimate from X-Server-Time (client.ts:279-291, react.ts:21-31), so a wrong phone clock does not change the deadline.
- Idempotency keys are kept per user intent with a synchronous in-flight guard (packages/ui/src/core/useSingleFlight.ts:11-39); mutations are never retried automatically, and queries never retry 4xx responses (keys.ts:33-37, packages/ui/src/core/query.ts:33-38).
- Query keys are scoped by account (['naya', accountId, ...]), so logout can clear every cached account's data with one prefix (keys.ts:1-8).
- darkColors is typed Record<ColorToken,string>, so the compiler requires a dark value for every light token (tokens/src/index.ts:39-42). In light mode every text/surface pair passes WCAG AA (muted on background 4.93:1, status colours on their soft fills at least 5.2:1).
- The domain package's only runtime dependency is zod, and the same code runs on the Node demo API, in the in-process embedded demo and in all clients.
- French UI copy (labels, error messages, reasons) is centralised in the domain, and the onboarding and transport choices are documented in code comments.

**Findings (critical / high / medium)**

1. **Service category commission silently overrides the city commission for every app booking** — 🟠 high · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:12 (commissionBp: number, not nullable) and :203 (standard commissionBp 1500), :219 (premium 1800); apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:55 (default categoryId 'standard') and :60 (always sent); services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:85 and :127 (commissionBp: quote.service?.commissionBp ?? rules.commissionBp); packages/domain/src/cities.ts:27 (Casablanca 1800); docs/FINANCE.md:17-18 (Rabat 15 %, Casablanca 18 %); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:45 (admin category schema requires a commission); services/demo-api/test/embedded.test.ts:80 (finance scenarios quote without categoryId)
   - Detail: Category pricing fields fall back to the city when they are null, but the commission field has no fallback. Every quote from the passenger app carries a category, so the frozen commission always comes from the category. A Casablanca 'Naya Standard' ride is charged 15 % instead of the documented 18 %, and editing the city commission on the admin Rules page has no effect on rides booked in the app. The FINANCE fixtures do not catch this because the scenario tests create quotes without categoryId. Disabling 'standard' in admin also makes every initial quote fail, because the app hard-codes it as the default.
   - Fix: Make ServiceCategory.commissionBp nullable, meaning 'inherit the city rule', and set it to null for 'standard'. Record which rule produced the commission. Add a scenario test that books through a category in each city, and stop hard-coding 'standard' as the client default (use the first enabled category).
2. **Child handover verification codes are sent to the driver** — 🟠 high · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:55-60 (AuthorizedRecipient.verificationCode) and :61-69 (FamilyChild.recipients); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:250-257 (family() returns full FamilyChild objects, including recipients, to the driver role) and :699-706 (completion only checks recipient.verificationCode === input.code); packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:733-741 (only the UI hides the code from drivers); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:85-87 (4-digit code, no attempt limit)
   - Detail: The step labelled 'Remis à une personne autorisée' (handed to an authorized person) is meant to prove that an authorized adult received the child by giving a code to the driver. The driver's /prototype/family response already contains every recipient's code, so a driver can complete the handover without the recipient. The domain has only one FamilyChild type, with no driver-facing view without the secret. Codes are 4 digits, stored in plain text, and there is no limit on attempts.
   - Fix: Add a driver-facing type in the domain (for example FamilyChildForDriver = Omit<FamilyChild,'recipients'> & { recipients: { id; name; relationship }[] }) and return only that to drivers. Store a hash of the code, limit attempts, and add a test that the driver payload never contains verificationCode.
3. **Passenger-wallet and mobile-wallet money flows bypass the domain settlement rules and FINANCE.md** — 🟠 high · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/wallet.ts:56 (rideSettlement(method: 'cash' \| 'card')); packages/domain/src/entities.ts:292 (PaymentMethodKind includes 'wallet' and 'mobile_wallet'); services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:625-630 (payment confirmed immediately and ride_net_credit posted for wallet/mobile_wallet); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:494-508 (the passenger herself resolves her top-up as 'confirmed'; no provider callback, signature or devMode check); docs/FINANCE.md:32-42 (no ledger rows for these methods) and :55-58 (signed callbacks, no optimistic confirmation)
   - Detail: The domain only knows how to settle cash and card rides. The new methods are settled directly in demo-api: the passenger confirms her own top-up, those funds pay a ride, and the driver immediately gets a withdrawable ride_net_credit. Mobile-wallet rides are confirmed with no provider step at all. This contradicts the documented guarantees (only confirmed provider events post; no optimistic confirmation), and the money rules for these methods are documented nowhere.
   - Fix: Extend rideSettlement and add a passenger-wallet projection to the domain. Document wallet and mobile_wallet rows in FINANCE.md. Route top-up confirmation through the same signed provider-callback or dev-sandbox path that driver recharges use, and gate it to dev mode.
4. **Passenger no-show is handled as a driver cancellation: the passenger is re-matched and pays no fee** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/machines/ride.ts:21-22 (DRIVER_CANCEL leads to 'searching' from driver_assigned and driver_arrived); packages/domain/src/labels.ts:58 (driver reason 'passenger_unreachable'); services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:660 and :671 (driver cancel always re-dispatches)
   - Detail: When a driver at the pickup reports 'Passagère injoignable' (passenger unreachable), the ride goes back to searching and another driver is sent to the same no-show passenger. No fee is charged and the driver who travelled gets nothing, even though the policy has feeAfterArrival.
   - Fix: Add a NO_SHOW event (driver_arrived to cancelled, after a wait window) that charges feeAfterArrival to the passenger and credits the travelling driver. Keep DRIVER_CANCEL leading to searching only for reasons on the driver's side.
5. **A ride in progress can only be completed; there is no abort path for SOS, accident or admin** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/machines/ride.ts:23 (in_progress: { COMPLETE }); packages/domain/test/machines.test.ts:29 (test asserts that cancelling in_progress throws); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:790-846 (SOS creates an alert but never touches the ride)
   - Detail: After an SOS, an accident or a vehicle breakdown, the only legal end state is 'completed', which charges the full fare and posts commission. An administrator cannot end a stuck ride either.
   - Fix: Add SYSTEM_ABORT / ADMIN_END events from in_progress to a terminal state with fare-adjustment rules (prorated or zero), protect them with a permission, and record the reason. Document the rule in FINANCE.md.
6. **Required verification items ignore the document type: passport holders must upload a back side** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/machines/verification.ts:16-20 (passenger_identity always needs id_back); packages/domain/src/entities.ts:104 (documentType includes 'passport'); packages/ui/src/DocumentPicker.tsx:15 (passport offered); apps/passenger/src/features/verify/steps.ts:6 ('Pièce · verso')
   - Detail: A passport has no back side, yet assertSubmittable refuses submission without an id_back upload. Passport holders (foreign residents, visitors) are either blocked or pushed to upload a meaningless photo.
   - Fix: Make REQUIRED_ITEMS a function of (subject, identity.documentType), with passport needing selfie + id_front (photo page). Mirror the rule in the capture steps.
7. **Approved verification is final; the domain cannot express an expired licence or insurance** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/machines/verification.ts:13 (approved: {}); :54-55 (isDriverVerified only checks 'approved'); packages/domain/src/entities.ts:100-114 (no expiry dates on identity or vehicle details)
   - Detail: Insurance and driving licences expire, and a safety incident may require revoking a vehicle or identity approval. Today the only lever is suspending the whole User (entities.ts:39), which loses the reason and the audit trail at verification level.
   - Fix: Add REVOKE (approved to more_info_requested or rejected) and optional expiry fields for documents. Re-evaluate eligibility against expiry, and test it.
8. **Cash cancellation fees stay pending forever and the travelling driver is never credited** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:450-451 (cash fee payment gets provider 'cash' and providerRef null), :461 (only card fees are queued); services/demo-api/src/services/finance.ts:133 (callbacks look payments up by providerRef); docs/FINANCE.md:42 ('Cancellation fee confirmed: +fee − commission to the driver who travelled')
   - Detail: When a passenger paying cash cancels after the grace period, a pending cancellation_fee payment is created, but no flow can ever confirm it. The fee is shown to the passenger, never collected, and never credited to the driver. The domain and FINANCE.md say nothing about cash fees.
   - Fix: Decide the rule (collect on the next ride, charge a saved card, or waive cash fees) and add it to FINANCE.md and to the domain settlement helpers.
9. **No unit tests for time, phone, geo, schemas or prototype logic, and no tests for packages/api** — 🟡 medium · testing · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/test/ contains only money.test.ts, pricing.test.ts, wallet.test.ts, machines.test.ts; packages/api has no test directory; packages/domain/src/time.ts:44-50, phone.ts:20-34, prototype.ts:160-175 untested
   - Detail: Timezone conversion, +212 normalisation, zone membership of demo places, FAMILY_NEXT ordering, schema refinements (isAdult, decision rules), shouldRetry and the client's error mapping are important logic with no direct tests. The family and wallet flows are only exercised through 6 demo-api integration tests (services/demo-api/test/prototype.test.ts). There is no CI workflow (.github/workflows does not exist).
   - Fix: Add focused tests to domain/test: time (including the 2026-02-15 and 2026-03-22 Ramadan transitions), phone (00212, pasted +212, masking), geo (every PLACES entry against its zone), schemas, prototype invariants. Add api tests for request() error paths with a fake transport. Run pnpm -r test in CI.
10. **Response types exist only in the client and are never checked against the server** — 🟡 medium · maintainability · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/api/src/client.ts:56-231 (PublicCity, DriverStatus, WalletResponse, AdminOverview, …) and :324 (return json as T); services/demo-api/src/app.ts:148-186 (publicCity and driverStatus build plain object literals); grep finds no import of @naya/api in services/demo-api
   - Detail: The server and the client agree on shapes only by convention. A renamed or removed field on the server compiles fine and fails at runtime in the apps. The embedded standalone demo has the same exposure.
   - Fix: Move the response contracts into @naya/domain (or a contracts module) and have server handlers return them with satisfies / explicit return types. Optionally add zod response schemas for the riskiest endpoints in dev builds.
11. **SOS response, catalog pricing and family driver assignment all hide behind 'config.edit'** — 🟡 medium · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/entities.ts:53-61 (no safety or catalog permission); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:878, :892, :928, :949 (requirePermission(…, 'config.edit'))
   - Detail: Anyone allowed to answer an SOS can also change category pricing and commissions. A support agent without config.edit cannot see or answer SOS alerts, which is the opposite of the safety need.
   - Fix: Add AdminPermission values such as 'safety.respond', 'catalog.edit' and 'family.assign', and check those instead.
12. **Hard-coded light hex values in shared UI make text unreadable in dark mode** — 🟡 medium · a11y · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/WelcomeJourney.tsx:33 and :45 (light gradients #F1E6EF/#EBDFE8: in dark mode the accent text and BrandMark sit at 1.45:1 and 1.36:1); packages/ui/src/DocumentPicker.tsx:68 (selected tile background #FDF9FB with dark-mode ink text: 1.10:1, used in passenger (verify)/identity.tsx:79 and driver docs/identity.tsx:39); packages/ui/src/Ride.tsx:233 with packages/tokens/src/index.ts:41 (dark inverse #201522 on accentDeep #382233: 1.21:1, on #7A3F64: 2.28:1); apps/driver/src/components/WalletCard.tsx:30 (separate hard-coded gradients)
   - Detail: darkColors has a value for every token, but gradients and some backgrounds are written as raw hex in components, so onboarding (shown before login when the OS is in dark mode) and the identity document picker fail contrast. The token set has no gradient or 'selected strong' tokens, which pushes components to hard-code. Dark 'inverse' is dark, so it is only valid on the light accent, never on accentDeep.
   - Fix: Add gradient and selected-surface tokens (light and dark) to @naya/tokens. Replace the raw hex values (and lint for them). Document which surfaces 'inverse' may be used on. Delete or fix the unused WalletSummary card.
13. **NativeWind/Tailwind is wired into both mobile apps but no mobile code uses className** — 🟡 medium · performance · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/babel.config.js:8-9 (jsxImportSource 'nativewind' plus the css-interop babel plugin); apps/passenger/metro.config.js:3,6 (withNativeWind); apps/passenger/src/app/_layout.tsx:2 and apps/driver/src/app/_layout.tsx:2 (import global.css); apps/passenger/tailwind.config.js:4; grep finds 0 className usages in apps/passenger/src, apps/driver/src and packages/ui/src (only a type prop in PressableScale.tsx:12)
   - Detail: Every JSX element in both apps goes through the css-interop runtime, and builds carry the Tailwind pipeline, for no styling benefit. Styling actually uses @naya/tokens and StyleSheet.
   - Fix: Remove NativeWind from the mobile apps (babel, metro, global.css, tailwind.config, dependency) or adopt it deliberately. Record the decision in an ADR.
14. **About 6.8 MB of generated PNG/JPG files are still bundled into the mobile apps but never rendered** — 🟡 medium · performance · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/assets/src/images.ts:2-36 (require() of 5 illustrations, 5 cars, 10 characters, 2 avatars); packages/ui/src/Brand.tsx:21-27 (Illustration only uses `source` as a key to pick a vector SVG); packages/ui/src/map/Markers.tsx:14-15 (driver marker is inline SVG); packages/ui/src/Ride.tsx:178-179 (avatarFor returns null); du: illustrations 3.4M, cars 1.9M, characters 1.5M, avatars 28K; docs/MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md:12 (generated portraits 'no longer displayed')
   - Detail: Metro bundles every require()d image reachable from @naya/assets, so the binaries still ship the generated characters and illustrations the feedback asked to remove. The only raster file still in use is cars/naya-signature-top.png in admin (apps/admin/src/components/MiniMap.tsx:3).
   - Fix: Replace `source: number` with a string key (for example 'passengerWelcome' \| 'driverWallet' \| 'car'). Drop the image requires from the mobile barrel, or move the raster files to an archive or apps/web if marketing needs them, and keep only naya-signature-top.png for admin.
15. **The arrival fee cancels the promised free window, and 'arrived' can be declared anywhere with no proximity check** — 🟡 medium · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/pricing.ts:82-83 (driver_arrived always returns feeAfterArrival, with no grace or wait check); packages/domain/test/pricing.test.ts:49 (asserts a 15 MAD fee at the very instant of assignment); services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:550-558 (driverArrive has no location or distance check); services/demo-api/src/services/bot.ts:32 (the demo driver arrives 15 s after assignment); apps/passenger/src/features/help/topics.ts:18 ('gratuite ... 2 minutes après l'attribution', with no arrival caveat); finance.ts:155-158 (fee credited net to the driver)
   - Detail: The passenger is promised free cancellation for graceSeconds after assignment. As soon as the driver taps 'arrived', which she can do from anywhere right after accepting, any cancellation costs feeAfterArrival and the driver is credited the net fee. There is also no wait period after arrival before the fee applies. In the demo, the free window effectively lasts 15 s. This lets a driver game fees, and it contradicts the help copy.
   - Fix: Decide the rule and document it in docs/FINANCE.md. Options: keep cancellation free until the grace window ends even after arrival, and/or charge the arrival fee only after N minutes of waiting. Require the driver to be within a geofence of the pickup for ARRIVE. Add domain tests for an early arrival inside the grace window.
16. **Family subscriptions and family trips move no money: no payment, no ledger entry, no driver pay** — 🟡 medium · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:326-350 (subscription created and logged as 'paiement simulé', with no payment record); grep finds no postLedger or s.payments use in services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts; packages/domain/src/entities.ts:218 (PaymentPurpose = ride \| recharge \| withdrawal) and :441 (Payment.purpose = ride \| cancellation_fee); docs/FINANCE.md has no family or subscription section; plan prices 590 and 990 MAD at packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:228,242
   - Detail: The plans are priced at 590 and 990 MAD per month, but subscribing records no payment. Completed family trips credit nothing to the dedicated driver's wallet. Re-subscribing overwrites the previous subscription record in place (Object.assign at prototype.ts:341), which loses history. Nobody can explain how a family driver gets paid, or how a subscription is charged or refunded.
   - Fix: Add a subscription payment purpose and a driver remuneration rule (per trip or per plan) to the domain. Document the ledger rows in FINANCE.md, keep each subscription period as its own record, and test it.
17. **Family trips have no cancel, no-show or failed-handover state; a trip stuck at in_progress also stops the recurrence** — 🟡 medium · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:83-89 (FamilyTripStatus only progresses forward to completed) and :168-175 (FAMILY_NEXT is linear, not a makeMachine); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts (no cancel route, and the incident route at :765-788 only records a message); :731-760 (the next recurring trip is only created when a trip completes)
   - Detail: If no authorized adult is at the destination, the child is absent at pickup, or the parent needs to cancel, there is no legal transition. The trip stays open forever, counts against includedTrips, and the recurring schedule stops because the next trip is only generated on completion. For a child-transport product this escalation path is central.
   - Fix: Model family trips with makeMachine. Add CANCEL (parent, before en_route), NO_SHOW and HANDOVER_FAILED (with a mandatory escalation to an admin or support plus the parent), and an ADMIN_RESOLVE transition. Generate recurrences independently of completion. Add tests.
18. **Every driver serves every category by default (scooter, standard, Confort), and the vehicle has no type or class** — 🟡 medium · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:262 and :370 (driverCategories[driver.id] ?? all category ids); packages/domain/src/entities.ts:143-147 and :108-114 (Vehicle/VehicleDetails has no type or class); packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:181-222 (Naya Scooter is a two-wheeler and Naya Confort is a premium tier at 18 % commission)
   - Detail: Unless an admin maps a driver to categories by hand, a car driver gets 'Naya Scooter' requests and any car is dispatched for 'Naya Confort' at premium prices. Nothing in the domain links a verified vehicle to the categories it may serve, and vehicle verification does not record a two-wheeler or a comfort class.
   - Fix: Add a vehicle class to VehicleDetails, captured and reviewed in vehicle verification. Derive eligible categories from it, defaulting to 'standard' only. Validate the admin overrides against the class.

**Open questions raised**

- Should a service category ever override the city commission, or should 'standard' inherit the city rule? Which commission should a Casablanca Standard ride use: 15 % or 18 %?
- How is the women-only eligibility rule recorded and audited? IdentityDetails and User have no field for it, so it appears to rely only on the reviewer looking at the selfie (packages/domain/src/entities.ts:32-51,100-106).
- Should the passenger wallet, mobile wallet and Naya Famille subscription payments become part of the documented finance model (FINANCE.md ledger rows, driver pay for family trips), or stay explicitly 'prototype-only'?
- What should happen to cash cancellation fees, passenger no-shows, and rides in progress that must be aborted after an SOS?
- Should approved verifications expire (licence and insurance dates) or be revocable by admins?
- Does the 'Naya Scooter' category need its own vehicle type and verification (registration and insurance for two-wheelers)?
- On Hermes (iOS and Android release builds), does Intl 'fr-MA' produce ' à ' between date and time, as formatDateTime's replace() assumes (time.ts:22)? Is globalThis.crypto.randomUUID available, or is the Math.random fallback always used (client.ts:269-273)?
- Should the raster illustrations, characters and avatars be deleted, archived, or moved to apps/web for marketing?
- Should apps/web consume @naya/tokens and @naya/domain, or is it deliberately independent?
- Should the NativeWind and Tailwind setup in the mobile apps be removed, or adopted for future screens?
- The domain, api and tokens test suites and typechecks were not run during this read-only audit. Should they be run (pnpm -r test, pnpm -r typecheck) to confirm the current green state?

<a id="demo-api"></a>

## Demo backend (services/demo-api)

**Health 6.5/10.** services/demo-api is a Hono backend over a single-writer JSON store (store.ts). The same createApp() runs as an HTTP server (main.ts) and in-process inside the native apps for standalone TestFlight builds (embedded.ts and platform.native.ts). The v1 core (auth/OTP, verification, quotes with frozen terms, dispatch/offers, ride state machine, driver ledger and wallet projection, signed provider callbacks, idempotency, hash-chained audit) is well designed and covered by 51 Vitest tests. The Oct 6 prototype layer (services/prototype.ts: categories, passenger wallet, Naya Famille, SOS, dispute reasons) is weaker. It uses inline Zod schemas and a lazily created state namespace. Every admin endpoint it adds is gated by config.edit, and its audit entries have no before/after or reason. Some of its safety and money checks only look correct: the driver's API payload includes the child handover codes, passengers can confirm their own wallet top-ups, and SOS alerts are hidden from the support role. Money bugs: cash cancellation fees stay pending forever even though the passenger receipt promises they will be collected, and driver earnings leave out wallet and mobile-wallet credits. Dev affordances fail open: NAYA_DEV defaults to on, the server binds 0.0.0.0, /dev routes have no authentication, and the simulated-payment endpoints stay live even with NAYA_DEV=0. The typed contract in packages/api matches the server routes one-to-one, and the domain machines and money rules port cleanly. A Postgres port still needs real transactions and locks, a job queue instead of the 500 ms tick, opaque IDs, DTOs, migrations, and a rework of the prototype module.

**Strengths**

- Clean layering: pure rules (money, pricing, state machines, wallet projection, Zod schemas) live in @naya/domain; the API calls them, and services/* hold the effects.
- Money is integer centimes and basis points throughout. The driver wallet is a projection of an append-only ledger whose entries carry unique business idempotency keys (finance.ts:47-69), so a replayed event cannot post twice.
- Quotes freeze city rule version, breakdown, commission and cancellation policy into the ride or booking (rides.ts:118-132), so later admin rule changes never re-price existing trips.
- The provider callback verifies HMAC-SHA256 over the raw body with a timing-safe compare (finance.ts:77-82, app.ts:615-620). Simulated provider outcomes reuse the same applyProviderEventIn path, with event-id dedupe.
- Transactions snapshot and roll back on any error (store.ts:32-44), persistence writes a temp file and renames it, and all mutation code is synchronous after the body is parsed, so offer-accept races serialize correctly (tested in S11).
- Idempotency-Key is required on booking and financial POSTs, scoped by (key, subject), with body-hash and route conflict detection (app.ts:125-144).
- Append-only, hash-chained audit with an integrity check shown in admin (audit.ts).
- Offer expiry is checked against the server deadline and committed before refusing a late accept (rides.ts:349-384).
- Uploads are sniffed by magic bytes and SVG is refused; reads are authorized per owner and admin permission.
- The same app runs in-process on device with platform adapters and a serialized request queue (embedded.ts:64-74), and embedded tests block global fetch.
- Client and server routes match one-to-one: every path in packages/api/src/client.ts exists in app.ts or prototype.ts.
- Broad scenario test suite (S01–S16 plus platform guarantees, 51 tests) built on a deterministic ManualClock.
