**Findings (critical / high / medium)**

1. **SOS is a neutral secondary pill and its sheet reads like a log, not an emergency control** — 🟠 high · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: docs/screens/feedback/driver-sos-ios.png; packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:83-88 (label 'SOS · sécurité', variant="secondary"), :97-103 (warning banner + raw '33.97935, -6.86290'), :125-138 (four outline buttons labelled as past outcomes, all with loading={busy}); apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:173 and apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:214 render it unconditionally, including while 'searching'; Button.tsx:13 already has a 'danger' variant
   - Detail: The safety entry point uses the same plum outline style as 'Annuler' and is placed above the primary ride button in the driver footer. In the sheet, the alert uses warning amber and success green, never danger. The position appears as raw lat/lng with no label. The four actions ('Appel urgence simulé', 'Position partagée (simulation)'…) are phrased as completed states, so it is unclear whether tapping them performs or reports an action. They all spin together because they share one busy flag. The sheet does not repeat the Moroccan emergency numbers (19 police, 15 SAMU, 177 gendarmerie), although the older contact sheet mentions 19.
   - Fix: Give SOS a dedicated danger-tone control (icon plus 'SOS'), shown only from driver_assigned onwards. Use imperative verbs (Appeler les secours, Partager ma position, Prévenir mon contact, Contacter Naya) with per-action progress and a checkmark state. Show a mini-map or address instead of coordinates. Pin 19/15/177 at the top.
2. **Admin SOS alerts are a tab inside 'Services et familles', gated by config.edit, and absent from the overview** — 🟠 high · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/router.tsx:75 (path 'services' gated 'config.edit'); apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:101-127 (SOS is tab 6 of 8, between Familles and Portefeuilles); apps/admin/src/pages/Overview.tsx has no SOS/safety reference (grep -i 'sos\|safety' returns nothing); apps/admin/src/components/Layout.tsx:13-23 has no SOS nav entry; docs/screens/feedback/admin-prototype.png
   - Detail: A live safety alert sits next to catalog editing and the logo gallery. A support or operations admin without config.edit cannot see it, and nothing in the sidebar, overview 'Priorités' or a badge tells anyone an alert is open. That is a misleading safety posture even for a demo.
   - Fix: Add an 'Alertes SOS' item to the 'Espace de travail' group with an open-count badge, gated by a support/safety permission, and surface open alerts first in Overview 'Priorités'.
3. **After categories were added, payment method and Maintenant/Planifier are hidden below the quote sheet fold** — 🟠 high · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: docs/screens/feedback/passenger-categories-ios.png (sheet shows route row, 3 category chips, fare card, then the Confirm button; neither title nor payment row visible); apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:133 (map fixed at 36%), :140 (ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}), :141 title, :153 categories, :178 SegmentedControl, :189 payment row; compare the pre-category docs/screens/passenger/P08-quote.png where the payment row sits above the fold
   - Detail: On a 402-pt iPhone, the user can press 'Confirmer · 127,60 MAD' without seeing which payment method will be used or that scheduling exists. The sheet's own title is also scrolled off in the capture. This undermines design principle 4 ('price transparency before commitment').
   - Fix: Reduce the map share or make the sheet expandable. Render categories as compact full-width rows with price and ETA. Keep the payment row pinned in the footer next to the Confirm button, as Uber and Bolt do.
4. **Child handover verification codes are delivered to the driver's app, so the handover check is not a real check** — 🟠 high · security · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:250-256 (family() returns whole p.children objects to a driver whose trips reference them); packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:59 (FamilyRecipient.verificationCode: string); seeds at services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:590 and :596 ('1234', '5678'); comparison at :705 with no attempt counter or lockout; the UI hides the code from drivers only via role === 'passenger' at packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:733-739
   - Detail: The 4-digit code is meant to be known only by the authorised adult and spoken to the driver at handover. GET /prototype/family returns it in plain text to the driver's client, which shows it in the web build's network tab or to any modified client. The code can also be brute-forced without limit. For the flagship child-safety feature this is a design flaw that will carry into production if the shape is reused.
   - Fix: Strip verificationCode, or replace it with a non-reversible flag, in driver-facing serialisation. Verify server-side only. Add an attempt limit with an escalation to the parent and support. Add an API test asserting that the driver payload never contains codes.
5. **Illustration now always draws one generic line SVG, and DESIGN.md still promises the Naya Signature car and generated art** — 🟡 medium · docs · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/Brand.tsx:25 (SvgXml vehicleSvg/journeySvg regardless of `source`, introduced in 89aae18); packages/assets/src/concepts.ts:62-67; docs/DESIGN.md:3, :37 ('signature car art at 112 pt'), :51-53 ('The existing Rencontre identity and transparent generated artwork are retained. No generic … car clip art were introduced'); docs/screens/feedback/passenger-categories-ios.png shows the outline car
   - Detail: The feedback commit swapped every raster illustration (onboarding, empty states, quote/trips car, driver earnings) for two hand-written SVGs. The design record says the opposite, so a new contributor or agent reading docs/DESIGN.md will be misled. All historical captures still show the old art.
   - Fix: Update docs/DESIGN.md with a 'Media policy (Oct 6)' section and record the decision as an ADR. Either restore real per-context art or rename the component (VectorIllustration) and drop the unused `source` contract.
   - Verifier: Brand.tsx:25 ignores `source` except as a lookup key (git log: 89aae18). DESIGN.md is stale at :5 ('original image-generated art (Salma, Amina, Naya Signature car)'), :34 ('signature car art at 112 pt') and :51-53. The auditor's line numbers are slightly off. However, the swap was a deliberate response to feedback item 4 'Authentic media' and is recorded in docs/MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md:12. This i…
6. **Screenshot library is stale, partly blank and internally inconsistent** — 🟡 medium · docs · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: md5: docs/screens/native/402/driver-06-debt.png == driver-05-vehicle-pending.png (both blank pearl screens); docs/screens/native/402/driver-06-online.png == driver-06-offline.png (shows 'Vous êtes hors ligne'); same for native/375 online/offline and driver/D05-status == D05-approved; docs/screens/native/402/sheets/*.jpg dated Oct 1 01:02 while the PNGs are 02:57–03:14; docs/screens/native/390-large-text/sheets/sheet-01.jpg (23:12) shows the clipped offer card that the 23:21 PNG fixed; docs/screens/admin/* all Sep 30 with a light rail and 'API http://localhost:4013' footer, while apps/admin/src/components/Layout.tsx:111 now draws a dark rail #302532; no fresh capture of onboarding, home, tri…
   - Detail: The user wants docs/ to be the source of truth for future sessions, but the visual record mixes three design generations, contains broken frames, and does not show the current media swap, dark mode on most screens, or the SOS placement. Reviewers will draw wrong conclusions.
   - Fix: Regenerate captures for every surface after the next design pass and delete duplicates. Stamp each folder with the commit SHA in a README and build sheets from the same run. Add the feedback routes to the native tour.
   - Verifier: Verified by md5: native/402 driver-06-debt == driver-05-vehicle-pending (779827f8…, both blank pearl frames, viewed). driver-06-online == driver-06-offline (b927b0a9…, shows 'Vous êtes hors ligne'). The same holds for native/375 (d79b4980…) and driver/D05-status == D05-approved (71f1b00b…). Sheets are timestamped Oct 1 01:02 and the PNGs 02:57-03:14. The admin captures are from Sep 30, show a lig…
7. **Two unrelated back-offices with different IA, persona, data and visual language** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: apps/admin (Vite, dark rail, nav 'Vérifications, Personnes & véhicules, Courses, Support, Finance, Villes & règles, Paiements, Services et familles, Journal d’audit', admin 'Meryem Lahlou', rides NY-002 at 100 MAD) in docs/screens/feedback/admin-prototype.png and docs/screens/admin/A06-rides-1440.png vs apps/web/components/backoffice.tsx (light sidebar, 'Courses, Chauffeuses, Passagères, Vérifications, Finance, Support, Journal d’activité, Paramètres', admin 'Meryem Bennis', rides NY-1048 at 65 MAD) in apps/web/review/ux-fixed-backoffice-desktop.jpg; landing links to the latter at apps/web/components/landing.tsx:197-203
   - Detail: Stakeholders following 'Backoffice' from the website land in a backoffice that shows none of the mobile app activity (wallet, SOS, families, categories). It also uses different names for the same person and different fare levels, which reads as two products.
   - Fix: Decide (ADR) which backoffice is canonical. Either retire /backoffice or restyle it as a marketing mock clearly labelled 'aperçu', using the same nav labels, persona and fixtures as apps/admin.
   - Verifier: Confirmed: apps/web backoffice uses 'Meryem Bennis' (backoffice.tsx:329), NY-1048 (lib/store.ts:57) and different nav labels. The landing links to it at landing.tsx:197-203. Also, 'Meryem Bennis' is both the web admin and a passenger (lib/store.ts:149). The split is documented, though: apps/web/README.md says 'Data is not connected to the existing demo API', and the root README.md:188-190 present…
8. **Media policy conflicts across surfaces: vector line art in apps, photoreal AI people on the website, generated portraits as admin selfies** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/Brand.tsx:25 (apps: vector); apps/web/components/landing.tsx:254 and :479 (hero-moroccan.png, driver-moroccan.png generated per apps/web/DESIGN.md 'Image generation'); docs/screens/admin/A05-case-1440.png (selfie for 'Nour Benali' is the generated Salma portrait) via services/demo-api/src/seed.ts:163 (generic applicants reuse 'salma-selfie'); docs/MOBILE-FEEDBACK-AUDIT.md item 4 'Authentic media'
   - Detail: The feedback asked for authentic media. The apps dropped generated art, while the public website now leads with photorealistic generated women presented as Naya's chauffeuse and passenger, without disclosure. For a brand built on trust and verification, presenting synthetic people as staff is a reputational risk. The admin verification demo also shows the same face for different applicants, which undermines the 'vérifier la correspondance' check.
   - Fix: Write one media ADR: real commissioned photography for launch, clearly fictional illustration until then, no synthetic people presented as real. Label generated photos in the prototype and give each demo applicant a distinct specimen selfie.
   - Verifier: The inconsistency is real: apps use vector art (Brand.tsx:25), web uses hero-moroccan.png and driver-moroccan.png (landing.tsx:254, :479), and seed.ts:163 maps every 'generic' applicant's selfie to 'salma-selfie', so different applicants share one face in the verification demo. The 'without disclosure' claim is partly wrong: the landing legal modal states 'Les photographies ont été générées pour…
9. **Family trip form accepts free-text addresses but always plots Hay Riad → Agdal** — 🟡 medium · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:499-508 (free-text Départ/Destination), :550-558 (pickup: {...PLACES.hayRiad, label: pickup}, destination: {...PLACES.agdal, label: destination}); docs/screens/feedback/driver-family-web.png
   - Detail: Whatever the parent types becomes a label on fixed coordinates, so the map, journal positions and driver navigation show a different place than the label. A caption warns 'Adresses et positions de démonstration', but the mismatch is invisible on the trip card.
   - Fix: Reuse the passenger route/place picker (apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/route.tsx) or restrict the demo to a select of known places.
10. **Naya Famille is one long form-heavy column with raw date/time text fields and raw coordinates** — 🟡 medium · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:509 'Date du départ (AAAA-MM-JJ)', :514 'Heure du départ (HH:MM)', :751 always-visible 'Retard ou incident' field on every card, :775-780 timeline prints lat/lng to 4 decimals; docs/screens/feedback/passenger-family-dark-ios.png, driver-family-web.png ('… · 33.9594, -6.8747')
   - Detail: The main app already has a proper SchedulePicker (quote.tsx:186). Family cards stack title, map, multiple buttons, form fields, SOS, an incident form and a coordinate log in one surface, with no hierarchy between the parent's view and the driver's simulation controls ('Simuler les étapes de la chauffeuse', prototype.tsx:661).
   - Fix: Split into list → trip detail. Use SchedulePicker and day chips, hide simulation controls behind the dev launcher, render the timeline as an icon timeline with place names, and open the incident report as a sheet.
11. **Feedback screens use content-width buttons and pills, producing ragged stacks of different widths** — 🟡 medium · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/Button.tsx:61 (non-full buttons are alignItems:'flex-start'); prototype.tsx omits `full` on every Button (e.g. :86, :240, :360, :402); docs/screens/feedback/passenger-wallet-ios.png (provider pills one per line, CTA half width), driver-sos-ios.png, passenger-family-dark-ios.png ('Ajouter un enfant' secondary above the primary 'Planifier un trajet')
   - Detail: Core screens use full-width primary actions, while the new screens stack left-aligned buttons of four different widths. Secondary actions also sit above the primary one, which inverts the hierarchy the design record promises ('One dominant action').
   - Fix: Default task CTAs to `full`. Group payment options as a radio list (ListRow + check) and put the primary action last and full width.
12. **Four different selected-state treatments** — 🟡 medium · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/Form.tsx:314 (Pill selected = ink/near-black fill); apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:154 (category selected = plum border + colors.selected tint); tab bar active = plum-tinted pill (docs/screens/native/402/passenger-06-home.png); admin Prototype tabs selected = primary gradient button (docs/screens/feedback/admin-prototype.png); passenger-wallet-ios.png shows black 'Cash Plus' chip
   - Detail: Users see black, plum-tint and gradient-plum for the same concept ('this is selected'). The black chip is the only near-black filled element in the light UI and reads as a primary button.
   - Fix: Define one selected token pair (e.g. selected bg + accent border + accent check) in packages/tokens. Apply it to Pill, category rows, segmented/tab controls and admin tabs.
13. **Service categories lack price and icon, use car art for Scooter, and are dropped after booking** — 🟡 medium · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:154 (chip shows name + micro text only; catalog `icon: 'scooter'` in packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:184 unused), :157 (vehicle SVG for every category), :156 a11y label 'Naya, …' not the category; grep 'service\|category' in apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx, receipt/[id].tsx, (tabs)/trips.tsx returns nothing; docs/screens/feedback/passenger-categories-ios.png
   - Detail: Comparing categories means tapping each one and reading the single fare card below. The selected 'Naya Scooter' still shows a car. After confirmation the ride, receipt and history never mention the category, while the driver card says 'Naya Signature · Perle'.
   - Fix: Use full-width category rows (icon, name, ETA, price, radio). Carry `service.name` into the ride sheet, receipt header and trip rows, and use category-specific art.
14. **Passenger wallet operations break the money-presentation rules used elsewhere** — 🟡 medium · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: docs/screens/feedback/passenger-wallet-ios.png ('Recharge Cash Plus · confirmée' + '06 oct. à 05h00 · Confirmée' + '50 MAD' left-aligned in title size); packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:251-263 (label already contains status; amount rendered unsigned via Text variant="title"); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:512; compare driver ledger docs/screens/native/402/sheets/sheet-02.jpg ('+85 MAD' green, right-aligned)
   - Detail: Status appears twice per row. Credits are not signed or coloured, and the amount sits in its own line in display size, unlike the driver ledger's 64–80-pt rows with right-aligned signed figures (DESIGN.md principle 3). 'Wallet marocain / paiement mobile' mixes English and French. The capture is scrolled so the balance hero is not visible, which leaves no evidence of the hero figure.
   - Fix: Reuse the driver ledger row component: icon, label, date on the left; signed, coloured amount on the right; a status pill only when not confirmed. Rename the provider to 'Portefeuille mobile'.
15. **Driver dark-mode switch off-state is nearly invisible (1.45:1)** — 🟡 medium · a11y · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/profile/preferences.tsx:38 (trackColor false: colors.line, thumbColor: colors.surface); tokens line #423648 vs surface #231D28 = 1.45:1 (computed); docs/screens/feedback/driver-preferences-dark-ios.png ('Nouveautés Naya' switch)
   - Detail: WCAG 1.4.11 needs 3:1 for component boundaries. The 'on' state also uses a dark thumb on a pink track, which inverts the iOS convention. The passenger app uses the custom `Toggle` (apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/preferences.tsx:50), so the two apps disagree.
   - Fix: Use the shared Toggle in both apps with a dark-mode off-track token of at least 3:1 against surface and a light thumb.
16. **Map attribution chip is illegible in dark mode** — 🟡 medium · a11y · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/map/TileMap.tsx:145-149 (Text tone="muted") and :158 (backgroundColor 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)' hardcoded); dark muted #BBAFBE on white is about 2.1:1; docs/screens/feedback/driver-family-web.png (grey bar, unreadable)
   - Detail: OSM attribution is a licence requirement. In dark mode it renders light text on a light chip over darkened tiles.
   - Fix: Theme the chip (colors.glass background, colors.muted text) and verify at least 4.5:1.
17. **At large text the driver home card grows over the status bar and hides the location and avatar controls** — 🟡 medium · a11y · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/driver/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/index.tsx:107 (absolute card anchored to the bottom with no maxHeight/top bound) vs :91 top controls; docs/screens/native/390-large-text/driver-06-debt.png (title directly under the 9:41 clock, map gone)
   - Detail: Large Dynamic Type pushes the blocker card into the safe area, removing the city pill, the 'Démo' badge and the account avatar.
   - Fix: Constrain the card with top: insets.top + 64 (or maxHeight) and make its content scroll, as the passenger ride sheet does (maxHeight '62%').
18. **At small sizes the Rencontre symbol reads as the ⌘ (Command) key** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: docs/screens/feedback/admin-prototype.png and docs/screens/admin/A01-overview-1440.png top-left '⌘ naya'; apps/web/review/ux-fixed-landing-desktop.jpg header and 'Vous êtes entre de bonnes mains' card; packages/assets/brand/naya-symbol.svg
   - Detail: Four looped corners make the same silhouette as the Mac Command glyph at 16–24 px, the size used in navs, favicons and app chrome. This is probably why the six alternative concepts were requested.
   - Fix: Feed this into the logo decision ADR. Test any candidate at 16/24/32 px next to common UI glyphs.
19. **Website FAQ says children travel only with their mother, while the apps sell unaccompanied child trips** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/web/components/landing.tsx:40 ('Les enfants accompagnés de leur mère sont également les bienvenus.'); packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:286-800 (Naya Famille: dedicated chauffeuse, child handover to authorised person)
   - Detail: Messaging contradicts the flagship new service and excludes other guardians.
   - Fix: Align the FAQ with the Naya Famille rules (who may ride, age limits, authorised adults) once the product policy is fixed.
20. **About 6.8 MB of raster art is still required into both app bundles but never displayed; web serves about 17 MB of unused images** — 🟡 medium · performance · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/assets/src/images.ts:2-39 (require() of illustrations 3.4M, cars 1.9M, characters 1.5M, avatars 28K per du); packages/ui/src/Brand.tsx:25 ignores them; grep shows no consumer of `characters`/`avatars` outside packages/assets; apps/web/public/images has hero.png, driver.png, *-candid.png, *-reference.png, car.png, salma.jpg, amina.jpg while landing.tsx uses only hero-moroccan.png and driver-moroccan.png
   - Detail: This inflates binary and OTA size for no visual benefit, and leaves earlier generated candidates publicly reachable at /images/*.
   - Fix: Remove unused requires and public images (keep them in a design archive outside the app), or restore their use deliberately.
21. **Native capture tour relies on fixed sleeps, producing blank and wrong-state screenshots** — 🟡 medium · testing · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: e2e/scripts/native-tour.mjs:10 (sleep), :24-27 (nav then fixed wait), :110-112 (online then sleep(7) then shot); outputs docs/screens/native/402/driver-06-debt.png (blank) and driver-06-online.png (offline state)
   - Detail: No assertion checks that the expected state is on screen before `simctl io screenshot`, so broken frames are committed silently. The feedback screens are not in the tour at all.
   - Fix: Poll a testID via the dev bridge (or compare against a blank-frame hash) before each shot. Add family, wallet, SOS, categories, brand and dark variants to the tour.
22. **docs/SCREENS.md and the screens READMEs omit all Oct 6 routes** — 🟡 medium · docs · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: grep -i 'famil\|wallet\|brand\|services' docs/SCREENS.md returns only driver wallet/finance rows; routes exist at apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/wallet.tsx, family.tsx, brand.tsx, apps/driver/src/app/(app)/family.tsx, brand.tsx, apps/admin/src/router.tsx:75 ('services')
   - Detail: The screen index used for orientation does not list the new passenger wallet, family, brand gallery, preferences/appearance or admin Services page.
   - Fix: Add a 'Feedback features (Oct 6)' table to docs/SCREENS.md with routes, states and capture names.
23. **An active SOS alert is not kept or shown to the user, and admin 'prise en charge' never reaches her** — 🟡 medium · ux · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:61 (alert lives in local useState, lost on unmount); :62 (contact field pre-filled with its own label 'Contact de confiance'); :83-88 (button label never changes after an alert); packages/api/src/client.ts:459 alerts() has no consumer (grep for 'alerts'/'responding' in packages/ui, apps/passenger, apps/driver returns nothing); apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:262-286 sets 'responding'/'resolved'
   - Detail: After leaving the ride or family screen, the user sees the plain 'SOS · sécurité' pill and a fresh 'Confirmer l’alerte de démo' form, which invites duplicate alerts. Nothing tells her that support has taken charge or resolved the alert. A safety flow needs that feedback loop.
   - Fix: Load the open alert for the ride or family trip (alerts() or a ride field). Show a persistent danger-tone 'Alerte en cours · prise en charge par Naya' banner with status, poll or push updates, and block duplicate creation. Leave the contact field empty with a placeholder, or prefill it from saved trusted contacts.
24. **The admin SOS card shows raw coordinates and has no map, ride link or status-aware actions** — 🟡 medium · ux · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:245-297 (alerts reversed, not sorted by status; 'Position : {lat}, {lng}' raw at :259; 'Course {rideId ?? familyTripId}' as plain text with no link, and family trips are labelled 'Course'; 'Prendre en charge' and 'Résoudre' render even for resolved alerts; status appears only inside the subtitle string with no danger badge)
   - Detail: Even with permission, an operator cannot see where the user is on a map, open the ride or trip, call her or her contact, or tell open from resolved alerts at a glance. This adds to admin-sos-buried.
   - Fix: Give alerts their own queue: open alerts first, a danger status pill, a mini-map from the existing admin map component, links to /courses/:id or the family trip, contact actions, and actions enabled per status with a required note.
25. **In the booking flow, wallet methods look like cash and show no balance, so insufficient funds only fail after Confirm** — 🟡 medium · ux · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:190 and :218 (anything other than 'card' gets the Banknote icon, including 'Portefeuille Naya' and the mobile wallet); quote.tsx:193 subtitle 'Débitée à l’arrivée' for the wallet, while services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:199 reserves funds at booking; services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:131-134 throws 'Solde du portefeuille insuffisant' only on booking; apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/payments.tsx:38 uses the same icon logic
   - Detail: The new wallet payment kinds (prototype.ts:190-197) were added without UI support. The passenger sees a cash icon for a wallet, never sees her available balance when choosing it, and learns about an insufficient balance from an error toast after pressing 'Confirmer'. That breaks the 'price transparency before commitment' principle.
   - Fix: Add a Wallet icon. Show 'Solde disponible X MAD' in the payment row and sheet, disable or flag the wallet when the balance is below the quote total with a 'Recharger' shortcut, and change the copy to 'Montant réservé à la réservation, débité à l’arrivée'.
26. **The driver card lost its visual identification cues: no driver photo, and the car art ignores the real colour** — 🟡 medium · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/Ride.tsx:179 (avatarFor = () => null for everyone, so even a real verified photo has no path to display), :186 DriverCard uses it; Ride.tsx:173-175 VehicleImage always passes cars.pearlSmall and renders the same accent outline, while the pre-89aae18 code chose plumSmall or pearlSmall from the colour (git show 89aae18 -- packages/ui/src/Ride.tsx)
   - Detail: Removing the generated portraits was right, but the change also removed any slot for a real driver photo. The passenger now checks the driver only by name, text colour and plate. In a women-only service where verification is the main promise, a photo and an accurate car depiction at pickup are standard safety cues.
   - Fix: Display the driver's approved profile photo (from the verified selfie, with consent) in DriverCard and the offer/pickup screens, falling back to initials. Tint the vehicle SVG from the vehicle colour, or show a colour swatch next to the plate.
27. **Onboarding and empty states now show the same generic phone glyph, including on consecutive slides** — 🟡 medium · ui · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/src/app/(auth)/welcome.tsx:7-8 (passengerWelcome and passengerPlanning) both map to journeySvg 'journey' in packages/ui/src/Brand.tsx:25; the same art is used at (verify)/verification-status.tsx:48, (tabs)/trips.tsx:48 and the driver welcome.tsx:7; the art (packages/assets/src/concepts.ts:62-67, rendered with rsvg-convert) is a phone outline with a squiggle arrow
   - Detail: The first two passenger onboarding slides are visually identical. The first-impression art no longer communicates women, the city, the car or trust, which the copy promises ('Vos trajets, entre femmes'). No current capture shows this, because the native tour has not been re-run since 89aae18.
   - Fix: Commission distinct, clearly illustrative (non-photoreal) scenes per slide and empty state. As a stopgap, give each slide its own vector motif (route, calendar, ID check, wallet) and re-capture onboarding.

**Open questions raised**

- The passenger wallet capture (docs/screens/feedback/passenger-wallet-ios.png, 06:01) shows no balance card, but the committed code (prototype.tsx:203-221, commit at 06:17) renders one above 'Recharger'. Was the screen scrolled, or was the balance added after the capture?
- Is 'Naya Scooter' (a two-wheeler with a 3-minute ETA) really intended for a women-only VTC launch in Rabat, and what helmet and passenger-safety rules apply? It currently reuses car art.
- Fares look high for Rabat: 100 MAD for 10 km / 25 min, 127,60 MAD for 9.1 km Confort. Is the demo pricing meant to be realistic for stakeholder demos?
- Should SOS be available while the ride is still 'searching' with no driver assigned (apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:173)?
- Which logo direction was chosen, and when should the 'Identité · 6 pistes' gallery leave the passenger and driver apps?
- Are the website's photorealistic generated women meant for public launch, or only as placeholders until commissioned photography exists?
- Did the native map-framing fix in docs/DESIGN.md:54 resolve the regional-zoom in-trip maps? No capture after Oct 1 shows an in-trip state.
- Should the admin and web backoffice support dark mode, now that both mobile apps do?

<a id="docs"></a>

## Documentation & knowledge base audit

**Health 4/10.** The repo has about 1,400 lines of good, honest prose docs: a detailed README, FINANCE/INTEGRATIONS/SCREENS/DESIGN, the Oct 1 standalone demo notes, the Oct 6 feedback audit and demo notes, and the web prototype's design and audit records. But a new Claude/Codex chat has nothing to load. There is no root CLAUDE.md or AGENTS.md. AGENTS.expo.md is a generic Expo template that nothing loads. The original spec and Figma manifest (_handoff/) are git-ignored, and the 10-page feedback PDF that drove the Oct 6 work is not in the repo. The docs are dated snapshots that were appended to, never maintained. README's main Verification table is still the Sep 30 one, and three docs give three different test counts. SCREENS.md and INTEGRATIONS.md predate the Oct 6 wallet, family, SOS, category, brand and Services features. FINANCE.md does not cover the passenger wallet or category pricing. One contradiction affects money: category commission now overrides the city commission, so a Casablanca Standard ride takes 15% while FINANCE.md says 18%. Several Oct 6 changes quietly reversed handoff constraints with no decision record: vector art instead of generated PNGs, a premium Confort tier, dark mode. Documented architecture also differs from the code: NativeWind is documented but never used, the "prototype" naming hides real features, and there are two disconnected admin surfaces. The repo has no ADRs, PRD, feature specs, glossary, API or data-model reference, conventions, testing strategy, backlog or doc-freshness process. A proposed docs/ tree, ADR template, root AGENTS.md outline and freshness process are given in doc_facts.

**Strengths**

- docs/FINANCE.md gives precise, testable invariants (centimes, basis points, half-away-from-zero rounding, ledger projection, idempotency business keys) that match packages/domain/src/money.ts:6,25,40, and it names the test files that assert them.
- docs/INTEGRATIONS.md and every verification section say plainly what is simulated and what was not verified (README.md:175-179, STANDALONE-DEMO.md:28, MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md:56).
- docs/SCREENS.md maps every Figma main screen (with node IDs) to a route and to test evidence, and the S01–S16 scenario index maps to API and E2E tests.
- README.md:109-114 records hard-won native build gotchas: signed simulator builds for SecureStore, .env.production.local for release bundling, pod install after prebuild, and derived-data size.
- docs/MOBILE-FEEDBACK-AUDIT.md has a file:line evidence index and keeps the before-state separate from the after-state in MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md.
- Code comments explain decisions in place (apiBase.ts:7-12, store.ts:5-11, plugins/with-scene-lifecycle.js:1-4, babel.config.js:1), which makes them easy to lift into ADRs.
- Demo accounts and scenarios in README.md:122-146 match services/demo-api/src/seed.ts:28-56 exactly. Versions in README.md:29-49 match the installed packages (expo 57.0.26, react-router 7.18.4, vite 8.3.1, hono 4.13.11, TS 6.0.3).
- The apps/web docs record the design reference lock and image prompts, so visual decisions can be reproduced.

**Findings (critical / high / medium)**

1. **No root CLAUDE.md / AGENTS.md: new AI chats load no Naya-specific context** — 🟠 high · docs · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: Repo root contains only README.md and AGENTS.expo.md (ls -la). grep finds no reference to AGENTS.expo.md anywhere. AGENTS.expo.md:1-41 is a generic Expo template. apps/web/CLAUDE.md:1 is just '@AGENTS.md' (Next-only rules). apps/passenger/.claude/settings.json and apps/driver/.claude/settings.json only enable the expo plugin.
   - Detail: Claude Code auto-loads CLAUDE.md and Codex auto-loads AGENTS.md. Neither exists at the root, so a session opened at the repo root starts with no product summary, map, commands, invariants or gotchas, and has to re-read the codebase. This is exactly what the user wants to avoid. AGENTS.expo.md has a non-standard name, so neither tool picks it up, and it says nothing about Naya.
   - Fix: Create a root AGENTS.md (canonical, under 150 lines) and a CLAUDE.md containing '@AGENTS.md'. Move the Expo rules into apps/passenger/AGENTS.md and apps/driver/AGENTS.md, then delete AGENTS.expo.md. Content outline is in doc_facts ROOT_AGENTS_MD.
2. **Declared source of truth (_handoff) and the only PRD-like spec are git-ignored** — 🟠 high · docs · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: README.md:7 'Source of truth: Figma file … and the handoff package (project-manifest.json, README.md, QA.md)'. .gitignore:13 '_handoff/' (git check-ignore confirms). Knowledge that exists only there: _handoff/Naya-Expo-React-Native-Implementation-Prompt.md:98-103 (state ownership rules), :46 (no admin role switch in mobile apps), :85 (car colour variants never imply a premium fare tier), :164-178 (manual identity review, no gender inference, 30 s offers never auto-accept, scheduled ≠ assigned), :238-240 (completion criteria); _handoff/project-manifest.json (263 frame IDs, 16 scenarioPaths); _handoff/QA.md:22-37 (frame sequences per scenario); _handoff/README.md:74-78 (design direction overr…
   - Detail: A fresh clone, another machine or a cloud agent cannot reach the documents the README calls authoritative. The implementation prompt is the closest thing to a PRD, and it holds non-financial invariants that appear in no committed doc. Licence attributions exist only there.
   - Fix: Commit the text parts (README, QA, implementation prompt, project-manifest.json) to docs/handoff/. Keep the large PNGs ignored or move them to LFS. Move the invariants into docs/00-overview/product.md and the ADRs, and define a source-of-truth order in docs/00-overview/source-of-truth.md.
3. **Category commission silently overrides city commission, contradicting FINANCE.md and admin city rules** — 🟠 high · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: docs/FINANCE.md:18 'Casablanca (test) … Commission 18 %'; _handoff prompt:186 same. Code: apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:55 useState('standard') always sends a category; services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:127 'commissionBp: quote.service?.commissionBp ?? rules.commissionBp'; packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:189,203,217 Scooter/Standard 1500 bp and Confort 1800 bp, the same for every city; rides.ts:80 non-null category fares replace city base/perKm/perMinute/minimum. services/demo-api/test/prototype.test.ts:6-76 asserts only totals and frozen service id, never commission against city.
   - Detail: Every app booking carries a category, so the city commission edited on admin A11 (/villes/:id/regles) is never used. A Casablanca Standard ride takes 15%, not the documented 18%. Scooter and Confort fares are the same in every city, whatever the city rules say. No document, ADR or test states this precedence.
   - Fix: Decide the precedence in an ADR (for example: city commission applies unless the category overrides it per city). Document it in docs/02-specs/pricing-and-cities.md and service-categories.md. Add an API test: Casablanca Standard ride → 18% or whatever is decided. Update the FINANCE.md fare table to cover categories.
4. **No ADRs or decision log; several original constraints were reversed silently** — 🟠 high · docs · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: There is no adr/decisions folder. Decisions live only in commit messages (git log: 96af8c6, 74a1c14, 0e0b066, afac0b1, 89aae18). Silent reversals: dark mode (apps/passenger/app.config.ts userInterfaceStyle 'light' → 'automatic' in 89aae18); vector art replacing generated PNGs (Brand.tsx:25 vs handoff prompt:85); a premium 'Naya Confort' tier with higher fares (packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:209-222) vs 'car colour variants do not imply an additional premium fare tier' (prompt:85, packages/assets/src/images.ts:10); a second admin surface in apps/web (afac0b1); category commission precedence (rides.ts:127).
   - Detail: Without a decision log, new chats cannot tell intentional changes from bugs. They may 'fix' code back to the old spec or repeat rejected alternatives.
   - Fix: Create docs/06-decisions/ with the template from doc_facts and backfill the adr_candidates list (about 28 records). Require an ADR for any change to an invariant listed in AGENTS.md.

6. **No spec or review criterion defines or enforces the product's core 'women-only' eligibility rule, and the Famille feature adds adult male hand-over recipients without any stated policy** — 🟠 high · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: README.md:3 'Women-only mobility service'. `grep -rni 'gender\|sexe\|femme\|women\|female'` over packages/domain/src, services/demo-api/src and apps/admin/src returns only the category tagline at packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:198. The admin review checklist at apps/admin/src/pages/CaseReview.tsx:153 covers only legibility, information match and reasons. The 'no gender inference' rule exists only in the git-ignored _handoff prompt :166. The demo family seed adds an adult male authorized recipient, services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:592-597 ('Youssef', 'Père', code 5678). FamilyChild (packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:61-69) has no eligibility fields.
   - Detail: No document or code says how a woman is verified as eligible (ID-document sex marker? self-declaration? reviewer step?), what reviewers check, or how the rule applies to Naya Famille. In that service, children of any gender ride alone with the driver and adult men are seeded as authorized hand-over recipients. This is the brand-defining and legally sensitive rule of the product. A new agent or reviewer has nothing to implement or test against, and the handoff's 'do not infer gender from appearance' constraint is invisible in git.
   - Fix: Write docs/02-specs/eligibility-and-verification.md, ideally with an ADR. Cover the eligibility evidence, the reviewer checklist item, the appeal path, how Famille applies to children and authorized recipients, and what is explicitly out of scope (no appearance-based inference). Add the reviewer check to CaseReview, and add an API test once the rule is decided.

13. **Docs present NativeWind as the mobile styling system, but mobile code never uses className** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: README.md:20 'Tailwind 3 preset (NativeWind 4 and admin)', README.md:38, and the _handoff prompt:38 require NativeWind. Wiring exists: apps/passenger/babel.config.js:8 jsxImportSource nativewind, metro.config.js:6 withNativeWind, src/app/_layout.tsx:2 imports global.css. But git grep 'className=' finds 0 matches in apps/passenger/src, apps/driver/src and packages/ui/src at 177cbd6, f0f8d46, 92db683, 0e0b066 and HEAD (admin has 549, web 368). Styling is inline style objects using the colors Proxy (packages/tokens/src/index.ts:40-48), and 77 files call useTheme() to re-render on theme change (packages/ui/src/core/theme.tsx:26-28).
   - Detail: An agent following the docs would write className-based UI. That would bypass the runtime dark-mode Proxy and diverge from every existing component. The real styling convention is undocumented.
   - Fix: Write an ADR stating that mobile styles come from @naya/tokens plus useTheme(), with no className. Either remove NativeWind or document it as unused. Add this to the root AGENTS.md conventions.

22. **No privacy, data-protection or safety-operations doc, although the product stores identity documents, child profiles and journey evidence and offers SOS** — 🟡 medium · docs · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: FamilyChild stores firstName, age, school and notes (packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:61-69). FamilyTrip keeps location, arrivalProof and a per-step location timeline (:90-108). SafetyAlert stores location, actions and contactName (:111-122). The only privacy mention in committed docs is a future requirement, docs/INTEGRATIONS.md:10 'DPIA / CNDP declaration'. MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md:56 says staffed emergency response is not integrated. grep for CNDP, 09-08, RGPD, retention and DPIA across README.md, docs/*.md and apps/web/*.md returns only INTEGRATIONS.md:10.
   - Detail: A launch in Morocco requires CNDP (law 09-08) compliance, and minors' data and identity documents are high-risk categories. No doc records data categories, retention, who can access documents and child data (beyond the code permission union), or the SOS operating model (who responds, SLA, escalation). Without that, an agent cannot judge whether a change is acceptable, and the SOS feature's demo-versus-live gap is only partially stated.
   - Fix: Add docs/02-specs/privacy-and-data.md (data inventory, retention, access roles, CNDP/DPIA status) and docs/05-runbooks/safety-operations.md (SOS lifecycle, staffing assumptions, what is simulated). Link both from the root AGENTS.md invariants.

**Open questions raised**

- Which admin surface is canonical: apps/admin (connected, permissioned) or apps/web/backoffice (localStorage, open)? Will the web backoffice be connected to the demo API, merged into apps/admin, or kept as a marketing prototype only?
- Is the category commission meant to override the city commission (Casablanca Standard currently 15% instead of 18%)? Should Scooter and Confort fares vary per city?
- Should the git-ignored _handoff text (implementation prompt, QA, manifest) be committed? Are there licensing or confidentiality limits on committing the Figma-derived material?
- Where is the 10-page mobile feedback PDF? Can it, or a faithful text summary, be committed as a source requirement?
- Were passenger build 3 and driver build 4 (bumped in 89aae18) uploaded to TestFlight, and with which commit?
- Should the plugins/with-ios-build-paths.js workaround also apply to the driver app and be committed, or should the repo move to a path without spaces?
- Is NativeWind to be removed, or adopted for new mobile code (which would require dark-mode support in the Tailwind preset)?
- Which of the six logo concepts will be selected, and what is the media policy: vector illustrations (mobile) or photorealistic generated people (web)?
- Is 'Naya Scooter' (two-wheeler) a real planned offering for a women-only VTC, or a demo placeholder from the feedback?
- Should the 'prototype' modules and /prototype API namespace be renamed and split into domain modules before production work starts?
- Is Android in scope for the next milestone (it is documented as never verified)?
- What language should project docs use: English (current docs) or French (UI and handoff)?
- Is MOBILE-FEEDBACK-AUDIT finding 3 (masked-number copy while no telephony exists) accepted demo debt or a bug to fix?

<a id="product"></a>

## Cross-surface product coherence & feature completeness

**Health 5/10.** Naya covers a lot of ground for a demo. The core ride loop runs end to end in connected mode: quote, frozen terms, dispatch, driver offer, arrival, stops, completion, settlement into the ledger, and the admin view of the ride (traced in services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:70-638). The driver wallet and ledger model is rigorous. The 6 October feedback features (categories, Naya Famille, passenger wallet, SOS, disputes, dark mode, logos, Services admin) do exist in the code, but they sit in a loosely typed `/prototype` side module and several of their loops do not close. SOS alerts never come back to the person who raised them, and only a `config.edit` admin can see them. Family trips ignore the addresses the parent types, show the child's name to the driver and then ask her to type it as "verification", return the handover codes to the driver's API, and are never paid or tied to dispatch. Category commission silently replaces city commission, so Casablanca's 18% becomes 15%. Cash cancellation fees are promised "with your next ride" but are never collected. Cross-surface coherence is the weakest point. There are two unrelated backoffices (the Vite admin on the demo API, and the Next `/backoffice` on browser storage with contradictory personas and a different pricing formula). Naming drifts: "Naya Signature" is used as both a product and a car make. Copy promises masked calls, priority handling and 24 h SLAs that nothing implements. The standalone TestFlight builds cannot share state between apps. None of the prototype screens are gated by `DEMO_MODE`, so a production build would ship "Simuler la confirmation" buttons. The docs folder has useful FINANCE, INTEGRATIONS and SCREENS references but no ADRs, no specs, no glossary and no root agent entry file, and it has drifted from the code since 6 October.

**Strengths**

- Money is modelled correctly at the core: integer centimes and basis points, a single rounding point, quotes frozen into FrozenTerms at booking, a driver wallet derived from an idempotent ledger with unique business keys (docs/FINANCE.md, packages/domain/src/money.ts, services/demo-api/src/services/finance.ts).
- The core ride loop runs end to end in connected mode across passenger, driver and admin, including stops, driver cancellation with re-dispatch, offer expiry, stale tracking and scheduled dispatch (services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts).
- One Hono demo app serves as the backend contract for HTTP, embedded native and tests, with typed client contracts in packages/api. This makes a real backend swap tractable.
- Most simulated parts are honest in the UI ('démo', 'simulation', DemoBadge), and docs/INTEGRATIONS.md lists what live operation needs.
- The admin keeps a hash-chained audit log, idempotency keys on money and booking POSTs, HMAC-signed provider callbacks, and permission checks on most admin routes.
- Dispute evidence is now enforced server-side (services/demo-api/src/services/support.ts:12-22) and viewable in admin (apps/admin/src/pages/Ticket.tsx:44,90).
- There is good API scenario coverage for the feedback features (services/demo-api/test/prototype.test.ts) plus a cross-role Playwright feedback walkthrough (e2e/tests/scenarios-feedback.spec.ts).
- French copy is largely consistent in feminine forms (passagère, chauffeuse, 'Bienvenue', 'prévenue').

**Findings (critical / high / medium)**

1. **Family handover codes are returned to the dedicated driver's API** — 🟠 high · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:250-257 (family() returns raw p.children for role 'driver', including recipients[].verificationCode); packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:55-60 (AuthorizedRecipient.verificationCode); packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:718-725 (driver UI reads child.recipients)
   - Detail: The handover code is the only proof that the child is given to an authorized adult. GET /prototype/family for a driver returns every child linked to her trips with all recipients' 4-digit codes in plain text. The driver UI does not display them, but the codes travel to the driver's device and could be read with any proxy or debugger. That defeats the control.
   - Fix: Return a driver-specific child projection (first name and photo only, no codes). Verify codes server-side only, store them hashed, rate-limit attempts, and rotate the code per trip (a one-time code sent to the recipient).
2. **The child identity check asks the driver to type a name the app already shows her** — 🟠 high · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:627-628 (card title shows t.childName to the driver), :652-655 (age, school, notes), :697-705 ('Prénom de l’enfant vérifié' text field); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:688-698 (only compares the typed name with child.firstName)
   - Detail: The feedback asked for child pickup verification. The implementation only checks that the driver can retype a name displayed on the same card, so it verifies nothing and gives parents a false sense of safety. There is no child photo, no parent-side confirmation and no proof at pickup.
   - Fix: Use a parent-approved child photo plus a pickup code given by the school or parent, and have the parent confirm pickup in the app. At minimum, stop calling this 'vérifié' in copy.
3. **Naya Famille has no payment, no driver payout and no dispatch integration** — 🟠 high · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:290-352 (addSubscription: first verified driver in the city via mustFind, audit 'paiement simulé', no charge); :630-764 (advance never posts a ledger entry or payment); services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:245-249 (busyDriverIds ignores family trips); apps/driver/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/account.tsx:45 (only entry point)
   - Detail: The subscription price is never charged (wallet, card or other), and the dedicated driver earns nothing for family trips. The driver is chosen automatically without consent or availability. She can be on a regular ride, offline or debt-blocked while a family trip is due, because dispatch and eligibility ignore family trips. Family trips appear nowhere on the driver home screen, in either app's trip history, or in admin rides and finance. Past trips never expire or get marked missed.
   - Fix: Model family trips as rides (or scheduled bookings) with a family flag and a dedicated-driver assignment. Bill the subscription through the payment layer, credit drivers through the ledger, block conflicting dispatch, and show upcoming family trips on the driver home and in the admin.
4. **Category commission silently replaces city commission (Casablanca 18% → 15%)** — 🟠 high · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:127 (commissionBp: quote.service?.commissionBp ?? rules.commissionBp); packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:203 (Standard commissionBp 1500); packages/domain/src/cities.ts:27 (Casablanca commissionBp 1800); apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:55,60 (the app always sends a categoryId); docs/FINANCE.md:16 (Casablanca 18 %)
   - Detail: Every app booking now has a category, so city commission set on the admin Rules page no longer applies to any app ride. Category fares with non-null values (Scooter, Confort) are also the same in every city. This contradicts FINANCE.md and makes the Rules page misleading. The tests that check city commission book without a category, so they don't catch it.
   - Fix: Decide the precedence and record it in an ADR (e.g. per-city × per-category matrix, or a category multiplier on city rules). Show the effective commission on the Rules page and add a test that books with a category in Casablanca.
5. **Cash cancellation fees are promised 'with your next ride' but never collected or credited** — 🟠 high · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:439-462 (cash fee: payment created 'pending' with provider 'cash'; nothing later confirms it); apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/receipt/[id].tsx:19 ('… à régler avec votre prochaine course.'); grep shows no code reading pending cancellation_fee payments in createRide/settleRide
   - Detail: A passenger with a cash method who cancels after the grace period is told the fee will be added to her next ride. No code does that. The payment stays pending forever and the driver who travelled is never compensated, so receipts, driver earnings and admin pending payments drift.
   - Fix: Either add outstanding fees to the next ride's settlement (shown in the quote), or require an electronic method for fee-bearing cancellations. Credit the driver when the fee is collected, and add a scenario test.
6. **SOS alerts never reach the person in danger's screen, and only config.edit admins can see them** — 🟠 high · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/api/src/client.ts:459 (alerts() defined) with no consumer in apps/*/src or packages/ui/src; packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:61,67-74 (alert held in local state only); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:877-878, 926-928 (GET/POST admin SOS require 'config.edit'); services/demo-api/src/seed.ts:301 (support agent: support.resolve, rides.read, people.read); apps/admin/src/router.tsx:75; apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:173 (location = driverLocation ?? pickup)
   - Detail: When an operator takes ownership or resolves the alert, the passenger or driver never sees 'help is coming'. Reopening the sheet after navigation loses the alert. SOS sits in a tab of 'Services et familles' that only the super-admin can open. It has no Overview badge, no sound or notification, and shows raw lat/lng with no map. The recorded location is the simulated driver position, not the phone's GPS. Triggering it takes several steps: open the sheet, type a contact, confirm, then tap simulated actions.
   - Fix: Create a dedicated 'safety.respond' permission and a top-level SOS queue with alerting. Poll or push the alert status back to both apps. Use device GPS, make it one tap to trigger with a cancel window, and store trusted contacts in the profile.
7. **Every driver is eligible for every category by default, and vehicles have no type, so car drivers receive Scooter rides** — 🟠 high · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:262 and :370 (driverCategories[id] ?? all catalog category ids); packages/domain/src/entities.ts:108-114 (VehicleDetails: make/model/color/plate/year only); apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:194-234 (manual per-driver checkboxes)
   - Detail: Category eligibility should come from the verified vehicle (two-wheeler vs car, comfort level, seats). It is a per-driver admin checkbox list that defaults to all. Scooter also needs different licence, insurance and helmet checks that the verification items don't model.
   - Fix: Add vehicle type and category to the vehicle case and derive eligibility from the approved vehicle, with an admin override. Default to no category until approved, and decide whether Scooter is in scope for launch.
8. **There is no production backend or live integrations: the production profile points at a placeholder API** — 🟠 high · architecture · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/eas.json:36 and apps/driver/eas.json:36 (EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL 'https://api.naya.example'); docs/INTEGRATIONS.md:18-21 (no push, polling, no masking, JSON-file persistence); services/demo-api/src/store.ts (single-writer JSON store); no .github/ CI directory at repo root
   - Detail: Ranked gap for a Rabat launch. (1) Real backend: Postgres, auth/session, the same packages/api contracts, hosting, observability, CI. (2) Payments: PSP for cards (e.g. a Moroccan acquirer), a licensed partner for any stored-value passenger wallet, cash-network and mobile-wallet connectors, refunds. (3) KYC: encrypted object storage, retention, a reviewer policy for the women-only criterion, CNDP (law 09-08) declarations, and child-data handling for Naya Famille. (4) Remote push (APNs/FCM) so drivers get offers with the phone locked. (5) Telephony masking or in-app chat. (6) Routing, geocoding, ETA and zone polygons for Rabat. (7) A staffed safety desk with SOS escalation. (8) Legal: CGU, privacy policy, transport licensing, in-app account deletion. (9) Unified backoffice with admin user management and MFA.
   - Fix: Write a launch-readiness spec and ADRs for backend platform, payments partner and KYC storage before more UI work. Treat items 1–4 as launch blockers.
9. **Family trips always use Hay Riad → Agdal coordinates, whatever address the parent types** — 🟡 medium · bug · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:498-507 (free-text Départ/Destination), :549-560 (pickup: { ...PLACES.hayRiad, label: pickup }, destination: { ...PLACES.agdal, label: destination }); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:31-36 (accepts any place)
   - Detail: The labels change but the coordinates never do. The map, the driver's position, journey positions and the SOS position for a family trip all point at Hay Riad/Agdal. There is no place search, zone check or pricing, and the date is free text 'AAAA-MM-JJ'.
   - Fix: Reuse the passenger route/place search and zone validation for family trips, store real Place objects, and validate date/time with the existing SchedulePicker.

However, the form itself discloses 'Adresses et positions de démonstration' (:539-542), so this is a labelled demo shortcut rather than a hidden bug. It remains a real gap for launch: no place search, zone check or ScheduleP…

11. **Prototype-only screens and actions are not gated by DEMO_MODE and would ship in production builds** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/core/apiBase.ts:4 (DEMO_MODE); grep 'DEMO_MODE' shows only account.tsx, welcome.tsx, driver index, capture.tsx; packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:264-276 ('Simuler la confirmation' / 'Simuler le refus'), :359-365 ('Charger l’exemple de démo'), :89-138 ('SOS · démonstration'); apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/account.tsx:34-36 and apps/driver/src/app/(app)/(tabs)/account.tsx:45-46 (wallet, family and logo gallery always listed); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:197 ('Wallet marocain (démo)')

   - Fix: Gate simulation controls and the logo gallery on DEMO_MODE in the apps and on devMode in the API. Move logo review to the admin or Figma, and keep the production UI free of simulation affordances.

Wallet, Naya Famille and 'Identité · 6 pistes' rows are unconditional (passenger account.tsx:34-36; driver account.tsx:45-46).
