12. **Passengers can confirm their own wallet top-ups even when the API is not in dev mode** — 🟡 medium · security · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:494-517 (POST /prototype/wallet/:id/resolve: userOf(c,'passenger') with no ctx.config.devMode check), compare :562 and :659 (other demo shortcuts are gated); services/demo-api/src/app.ts:593-607 (provider sandbox also lets owners resolve their own recharges/payments outside dev mode); services/demo-api/src/main.ts:13-18 (NAYA_DEV=0 is a supported mode)
   - Detail: With NAYA_DEV=0, any passenger can create a top-up and mark it confirmed, which mints wallet balance. Spending it on rides credits drivers' ledgers through settleRide (rides.ts:625-628). The driver recharge sandbox has the same owner self-resolution. These are fine as demo affordances, but they are mounted unconditionally.
   - Fix: Mount sandbox and self-resolve routes only when devMode is on. Real top-ups must only be confirmed by signed provider callbacks (the existing /providers/:provider/callback path).
   - Verifier: Verified: POST /prototype/wallet/:id/resolve (prototype.ts:494-517) has no devMode check, unlike /family/example (:562) and passenger advance (:659). /provider-sandbox (app.ts:593-607) lets owners resolve their own refs when NAYA_DEV=0. A related point the auditor missed: rides paid with 'mobile_wallet' are confirmed instantly with no provider step in any mode, and the driver is credited net (rid…
13. **Two backoffices with different data, features, pricing and design, and contradictory personas** — 🟡 medium · architecture · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: apps/admin (Vite, demo API, permissions, audit chain, Services/SOS/family/wallets); apps/web/components/backoffice.tsx:53-59 + apps/web/lib/store.ts:297-468 (Zustand 'naya-web-prototype-v1' localStorage, open, not connected per apps/web/README.md:16); apps/web/lib/store.ts:71 (Nour Benali riding) vs README.md:137 (Nour awaiting review); apps/web/lib/store.ts:211-219 (Khadija 'En ligne', Dacia Sandero) vs services/demo-api/src/seed.ts:49 (vehicle under review, 'Naya Signature' at seed.ts:223); apps/web/components/backoffice.tsx:1261-1269,1326-1327 (float money, one global commission applied retroactively)
   - Detail: The Vite admin has categories, family, SOS, wallets, city rules, an audit chain and RBAC. The Next /backoffice has ticket priority and assignee, a 'next action' work queue, a service kill-switch and driver online toggles, none of which exist in the Vite admin. The same fictional people have incompatible states, vehicles and IDs (CH-001 vs demo user IDs). The web backoffice is unauthenticated, has no noindex, and lives on the public marketing site (apps/web/app/layout.tsx). Stakeholders shown both will get conflicting answers about the product.
   - Fix: Pick one canonical backoffice (likely apps/admin because it is wired to the contract and RBAC). Port the web queue/priority ideas into it, then remove or clearly fence /backoffice. Record the decision in an ADR.
   - Verifier: Verified: apps/web uses a Zustand localStorage store 'naya-web-prototype-v1' (store.ts:468). Nour Benali is riding (store.ts:71) while README.md:137 and seed.ts:42 have her awaiting review. Khadija drives a Dacia Sandero in web (store.ts:212-214) but a 'Naya Signature' in the seed (seed.ts:223) with her vehicle under review (seed.ts:48). The web backoffice uses float money and a global percentage…
14. **Dispute resolution defaults to 'Rejetée', and outcomes such as 'Remboursement accordé' move no money** — 🟡 medium · product · _adjusted_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Ticket.tsx:12 (OUTCOMES[0] = 'Rejetée'), :22 (useState(OUTCOMES[0])); services/demo-api/src/services/support.ts:85 (status set by regex on free-text outcome); no refund/credit path for passengers in services/demo-api/src/services/finance.ts (corrections are driver-ledger only)
   - Detail: An agent who writes a note and confirms without changing the pre-filled outcome rejects the passenger's dispute. 'Remboursement accordé' only adds a message: no card refund, wallet credit or driver ledger adjustment exists. The status depends on string matching, and the agent never sees which granular reason the user picked (no reasonId in Ticket.tsx or Support.tsx).
   - Fix: Make outcome a required explicit choice with no default. Model outcomes as an enum with financial actions (refund to card or wallet, driver adjustment) under finance permissions. Show the reason and its evidence rule in the ticket header.
   - Verifier: Verified: OUTCOMES[0]='Rejetée' is the default (Ticket.tsx:12,22). Status is derived by regex on free text (support.ts:85). Finance corrections touch the driver ledger only, so 'Remboursement accordé' moves no money. reasonId is not displayed in Ticket.tsx or Support.tsx. The agent still has to open the resolve dialog and write a mandatory note, so this is a UX risk plus a missing refund capabili…
15. **Wallet and mobile-wallet payments are labelled 'Carte' on the receipt; the quote says 'Débitée à l’arrivée'** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/receipt/[id].tsx:21 (any confirmed non-cash payment → 'Carte · paiement confirmé'); apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:190,193 (wallet shown with the Banknote/cash icon; subtitle 'Débitée à l’arrivée' although the wallet is reserved at booking per services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:199), :218 (same icon logic)
   - Detail: Payment methods added on 6 October were not threaded through the existing payment UI branches, so receipts and the payment picker misdescribe them.
   - Fix: Use a single PaymentMethodKind → label/icon/explanation map shared by quote, receipt, driver ride and admin (METHOD_LABELS already exists in apps/admin/src/components/status.tsx:47).
16. **The chosen category disappears after booking: not on the receipt, admin ride, histories or driver card** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: grep 'service' finds nothing in apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/receipt/[id].tsx or apps/admin/src/pages/Ride.tsx:88-101 (Conditions figées lists fare lines only); apps/admin/src/pages/Rides.tsx:42-46 (payment filter: cash/card only); only apps/driver/src/app/(app)/offer.tsx:112 shows it
   - Detail: MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md says booking terms keep the category. They do (FrozenTerms.service), but no surface after the offer shows it, so support and finance cannot tell a Confort ride from a Standard one.
   - Fix: Display terms.service on the receipt, driver ride and ride-end screens, the admin ride and rides list (with a category filter), and add wallet/mobile_wallet to the admin payment filter.
17. **Category chips show no price, ETA is a static constant, and Scooter shows a car** — 🟡 medium · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:154 (chips: name + etaMinutes + description, no price), :157 (always cars.pearlSmall), :160 ('Arrivée estimée · {etaMinutes} min (démo)'); packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:188,202,216 (etaMinutes 3/5/7 fixed)
   - Detail: Passengers must tap each chip, which creates a new server quote, to compare prices. The per-category arrival estimate requested in feedback item 1 is a configured number, not based on driver supply.
   - Fix: Quote all enabled categories in one request and show price plus ETA per row. Derive ETA from online eligible drivers, and add category artwork.
18. **Clearing a fare field in the admin catalog editor saves 0 MAD instead of inheriting the city fare** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:470 (dialog says 'Vide sur un tarif = tarif de la ville'), :512-517 (null is kept only when the original value was null; otherwise Math.round(Number('')*100) = 0); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:26,46-49 (cents min 0 accepted)
   - Detail: Editing Scooter or Confort and clearing baseFare or minimumFare stores 0, producing near-free rides. The editor is also a raw key/value form: enum strings, comma-separated city IDs, and an editable id that creates a duplicate row.
   - Fix: Map empty input to null for all fare fields and validate minimums. Replace the generic editor with typed forms (selects, city multi-select, read-only id).
19. **Saving any Services catalog row re-enables recharge providers an admin disabled on the Paiements page** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:913 (prototypeProviders(s, true) after every catalog PUT), :234-237 (force resets existing.enabled from the catalog); apps/admin/src/pages/Providers.tsx:51 (manual toggle)
   - Detail: Two admin pages control the same providers, and the newer one silently overwrites the older one, even when the saved row is a dispute reason or a plan.
   - Fix: Make one source of truth (provider config per city/purpose) and have catalog payment options reference providers rather than regenerate them.
20. **The driver recharge list now duplicates 'Carte bancaire (démo)' and describes the mobile wallet as an agency** — 🟡 medium · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/seed.ts:107-108 (seeded card + agency recharge providers); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:216-232 (adds card, mobile, Cash Plus, Wafacash recharge providers; mobile mapped to kind 'cash_network'); apps/driver/src/app/(app)/recharge/index.tsx:69-73 (non-card subtitle 'En agence avec une référence'; testID provider-${kind} duplicated)
   - Detail: Drivers in Rabat see two identical card entries and four 'agency' entries, including the mobile wallet. Duplicate testIDs also make the e2e selectors ambiguous.
   - Fix: Remove the seeded duplicates, add a 'mobile_wallet' provider kind with its own flow, and key testIDs by provider id.
21. **Rejected tickets count as open and stay in the admin 'À traiter' queue** — 🟡 medium · bug · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/app.ts:801 (status=open → t.status !== 'resolved'), :667 (overview openTickets same predicate); apps/admin/src/pages/Support.tsx:17 (default filter 'open'); services/demo-api/src/services/support.ts:85 (new 'rejected' status)
   - Detail: The 'rejected' status added on 6 October was not reflected in the open-queue predicates, so closed disputes pile up in the default queue and in the Overview KPI.
   - Fix: Define a shared isTicketOpen(status) in packages/domain and use it in both endpoints and the UI.
22. **Dispute reasons are not filtered by role, follow-up messages cannot carry evidence, and naming drifts ('cliente')** — 🟡 medium · ux · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/support.tsx:125 (reasons filtered by category only); packages/domain/src/prototype.ts:259-260 ('Comportement de la chauffeuse' and 'Comportement de la cliente' both shown to both roles); packages/ui/src/features/support.tsx:163 (follow-up messages send attachments: []); services/demo-api/src/services/support.ts:56 (API accepts follow-up attachments)
   - Detail: Drivers are offered 'Comportement de la chauffeuse' and passengers 'Comportement de la cliente'. When an agent asks for a photo (status awaiting_user), the user cannot attach one in the thread. 'cliente' contradicts the product's 'passagère' vocabulary.
   - Fix: Add roles to DisputeReason, allow attachments in the thread composer, and standardize on 'passagère' and 'chauffeuse' in a glossary.
23. **Safety and support copy promises things nothing implements, and mislabels 15 as SAMU** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/support.tsx:149 ('Urgence : 19 (police) · 15 (SAMU)', 'signalements traités en priorité'); apps/passenger/src/features/help/topics.ts:48 (same); apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/help/index.tsx:24; services/demo-api/src/services/support.ts:39 ('répond en général sous 24 heures'); apps/passenger/src/app/(verify)/verification-status.tsx:100, apps/driver/src/app/docs/status.tsx:14 (<24 h); no priority field in SupportTicket (packages/domain/src/entities.ts:562-578)
   - Detail: Safety tickets are sorted like any other, and there is no SLA tracking or staffing. In Morocco, 15 is generally Protection civile and SAMU is 141; verify with local counsel or ops before shipping emergency numbers. The help 'Sécurité' topic does not mention the new SOS button.
   - Fix: Add ticket priority (safety = urgent) with SLA timers in admin, verify the emergency numbers, and update the help topics for SOS, wallet and family.
24. **In the default standalone TestFlight build, SOS says alerts reach the back-office, but they stay on the device** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:99 ('La position et les actions apparaîtront dans le back-office de démonstration.'), :123; packages/ui/src/core/apiBase.ts:5,14 (standalone default); services/demo-api/src/embedded.ts:15-23 (per-app embedded store)
   - Detail: Testers of the TestFlight builds (the default demo) are told something false about a safety feature. The same isolation means family progress, rides and wallet never sync between the two phones or the admin.
   - Fix: Make the copy depend on STANDALONE_DEMO. For client demos involving family, SOS or admin, use connected mode or a hosted demo API.
25. **The active-ride status pill keeps a hard-coded white background in dark mode** — 🟡 medium · ui · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:118 (backgroundColor 'rgba(255,255,255,0.96)') with Text colored from colors.ink, which is '#F4EDF5' in dark mode (packages/tokens/src/index.ts:41); other literals at packages/ui/src/map/TileMap.tsx:158, packages/ui/src/features/capture.tsx:125
   - Detail: The main ride status becomes near-white on white in dark mode. Theming also depends on each screen remembering to call useTheme(), and many route files don't call it directly (they rely on shared components).
   - Fix: Use colors.glass or colors.surface tokens, add a lint rule against color literals in apps and packages/ui, and add a dark-mode snapshot for the ride screen.
26. **A city added in admin can't take bookings until zones are drawn, never offers cash, and reverse-geocodes to Rabat** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/cities.ts:58-69 (only card/bank providers created, all disabled; no zone); packages/domain/src/geo.ts:25 (isInService false with no zones → OUT_OF_ZONE); services/demo-api/src/app.ts:343-349 (reverse uses PLACES only); apps/admin/src/pages/Providers.tsx (no create-provider UI)
   - Detail: The feedback asked for configurable expansion (Marrakech, Tanger, Agadir). The checklist to open a city is implicit, and cash, the dominant method, cannot be added to a new city from the admin.
   - Fix: Add a city launch checklist in admin (zones, providers including cash, categories, places, test status). Create a cash provider by default, and make reverse geocoding city-aware or provider-backed.
27. **Admin Overview shows volume and commission to roles without finance.read** — 🟡 medium · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/app.ts:647-656 (adminOf only, no requirePermission); apps/admin/src/pages/Overview.tsx:38-39 (Volume and Commissions KPIs); services/demo-api/src/seed.ts:56 (support agent 'sans finance')
   - Detail: The support role is described as having no finance access, yet it sees financial aggregates on the landing page.
   - Fix: Strip financial fields from /admin/overview unless finance.read is present, and hide the KPIs in the UI.
28. **The admin login page always lists one-click demo credentials** — 🟡 medium · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/admin/src/pages/Login.tsx:11-14 (DEMO array with passwords), :59 (rendered unconditionally)
   - Detail: Nothing ties this to a demo build flag, so any deployment of the admin exposes full-permission credentials.
   - Fix: Render demo accounts only when a VITE_DEMO flag is set, and fail the production build if it is.
29. **Child data and arrival photos are broadly readable and have no consent or retention model** — 🟡 medium · security · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/uploads.ts:60-64 (any admin with support.resolve can read any support_attachment, which is the purpose used for child arrival photos per packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:685); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:74-92 (child name, age, school, notes stored); GET /admin/prototype returns all children with recipient codes (prototype.ts:877-889)
   - Detail: Minors' data (school, schedules, photos) is among the most sensitive data the product will hold. There is no consent capture, retention period or access scoping, which will matter for CNDP compliance and parent trust.
   - Fix: Use a dedicated upload purpose for family proofs with access limited to the parent, the assigned driver and a family-ops permission. Add consent at child creation and define retention.
30. **The public landing page contradicts the apps: child policy, pricing, product naming and payment methods** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/web/components/landing.tsx:40 ('Les enfants accompagnés de leur mère…') vs Naya Famille unaccompanied children aged 1–17 (services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:76); landing.tsx:89 (price = 38 + \|Δindex\|×12) vs Rabat rules (packages/domain/src/cities.ts:5-9); landing.tsx:702 ('Environ {price/4} min · Naya Signature') vs categories Scooter/Standard/Confort; landing.tsx:57 (card and cash only)
   - Detail: Prospects, investors and the client see different products depending on which surface they open.
   - Fix: Drive landing estimates from the domain pricing (or label them illustrative), adopt the category names, and settle the minors policy before publishing it.
31. **Notification preferences are stored but nothing reads them; there is no remote push; family 'notifications' are never shown** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: grep for rideUpdates/scheduledReminders/supportReplies finds only seed/auth defaults and the preferences screens; apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:53 and apps/driver/src/app/(app)/_layout.tsx:32 (local notifications ignore prefs; driver body hard-codes '30 secondes' vs offerTimeoutSeconds); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:726-729 (trip.notifications written, never rendered)
   - Detail: Users toggle settings that have no effect. Parents get no alert when a child is picked up or handed over unless the screen is open.
   - Fix: Add a server notification service (push plus inbox) that respects preferences, and render family events as notifications.
32. **Ratings go only from passenger to driver; passenger ratings shown to drivers are static; averages drift** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:503-518 (passenger-only rateRide; total computed from a rounded average at :512-514); apps/driver/src/app/(app)/ride.tsx:206 (shows passenger.ratingAverage from seed)
   - Detail: Drivers cannot report or rate passengers. The rating they see never changes, and recomputing from a 1-decimal average accumulates rounding error.
   - Fix: Add driver-to-passenger rating, and store a rating sum and count rather than a rounded average.
33. **Passengers cannot see the driver's photo before boarding** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/ui/src/Ride.tsx:179 (avatarFor returns null), :186 (DriverCard uses initials); apps/passenger/src/app/(auth)/welcome.tsx:7 (promise: name, car, plate)
   - Detail: In a women-only service whose promise is knowing who drives you, a verified driver photo (from the approved selfie) is a standard identification and safety aid.
   - Fix: Expose a reviewer-approved driver portrait derived from verification, with consent, on the driver card and arrival notification.
34. **The women-only eligibility rule is not captured in verification data or reviewer reasons** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: packages/domain/src/entities.ts:100-106 (IdentityDetails has no sex/gender field); services/demo-api/src/services/verification.ts:23-28 (rejection reasons have no eligibility-specific code beyond 'not_eligible'); apps/passenger/src/features/help/topics.ts:38
   - Detail: The central business rule exists only as reviewer knowledge, which makes it unauditable and inconsistent across reviewers. Its legal basis also needs review.
   - Fix: Add an explicit reviewer checklist item recorded in the decision (not inferred automatically), document the policy in a spec or ADR, and get legal review.
35. **No in-app account deletion (passenger only via a support ticket, driver none)** — 🟡 medium · product · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/preferences.tsx:60 ('Supprimer mon compte · écrire à l’assistance'); no equivalent in apps/driver/src
   - Detail: App Store rules for apps with account creation, and data-subject rights under Moroccan law 09-08, expect a deletion flow.
   - Fix: Add a deletion request flow with server-side processing, retention exceptions (financial records), and an admin view.
36. **Feedback features live in a loosely typed '/prototype' side module bolted onto core entities** — 🟡 medium · maintainability · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/state.ts:87 (prototype?: optional, lazily created via ensurePrototype at services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:95-105); packages/domain/src/entities.ts:264,280,395,563 (optional service/reasonId via import('./prototype')); zod schemas inline in services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:25-92 instead of packages/domain/src/schemas.ts; GET handlers mutate state outside tx (services/demo-api/src/app.ts:360,564)
   - Detail: Categories, wallet, family and SOS are product features but are named, routed, seeded and typed as a throwaway prototype. Scenarios don't seed them, contracts are optional, and the API path names would leak into production.
   - Fix: Promote each into a first-class domain module (entities, schemas, state machine, routes without the 'prototype' prefix), seed them in scenarios, and make the fields required where they are business-critical.
37. **No ADRs, specs, glossary or root agent entry file; existing docs have drifted from the code** — 🟡 medium · docs · _confirmed_
   - Evidence: docs/ contains DESIGN, FINANCE, INTEGRATIONS, SCREENS, STANDALONE-DEMO and two MOBILE-FEEDBACK files only; repo root has AGENTS.expo.md but no AGENTS.md or CLAUDE.md; README.md:12-22 layout omits apps/web; README.md:162-168 verification dated 30 Sep (36 API tests) vs docs/MOBILE-FEEDBACK-DEMO.md (51); README.md:181-186 does not list the feedback or standalone docs; docs/SCREENS.md has no wallet, family, brand or Services routes; docs/INTEGRATIONS.md and docs/FINANCE.md do not cover the passenger wallet, mobile wallet, Cash Plus/Wafacash, SOS or category commission
   - Detail: A new Claude or Codex session has no single entry point describing architecture, invariants, the two backoffices, demo modes, or which decisions were deliberate. The knowledge is spread across README, docs and git history.
   - Fix: Create docs/README.md (index), docs/architecture.md, docs/glossary.md (FR/EN product terms), docs/adr/NNNN-*.md seeded from the ADR candidates below, docs/specs/<feature>.md for each feedback feature, and a root AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md pointing to them. Refresh SCREENS/INTEGRATIONS/FINANCE.
38. **Passenger stored-value wallet (customer funds) is absent from admin Finance and visible only to config.edit** — 🟡 medium · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/app.ts:816-837 (/admin/finance/summary covers only driver wallets, driver recharges and withdrawals, and ride payments; no passenger wallets or top-ups); apps/admin/src/pages/Finance.tsx:14,50 (tabs: wallets = drivers, transfers, payments, ledger); apps/admin/src/pages/Prototype.tsx:378-380 (passenger wallets listed only under Services, route gated config.edit at router.tsx:75); app.ts:835 (pendingPayments includes the never-resolved cash cancellation fees)
   - Detail: Passenger top-ups are a liability Naya holds on behalf of customers, but a finance.read user cannot see balances, pending top-ups or reserved holds, and reconciliation ignores them. Only a super-admin can find them, under 'Services et familles'. In a real launch, a stored-value wallet needs a licensed partner and daily reconciliation, and the admin model doesn't represent it.
   - Fix: Add passenger wallet totals (balance, reserved, pending top-ups) and a top-up ledger to the Finance page under finance.read. Record the wallet's regulatory and partner model in an ADR before adding further wallet UI.
39. **Parents cannot cancel or modify a family trip, end a subscription, or edit or delete a child; the UI allows one recipient only** — 🟡 medium · product · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:446-1027 (family routes: GET family, POST children, subscribe, trips, example, advance, incident; there is no cancel, PATCH or DELETE for trips, subscriptions or children); packages/ui/src/features/prototype.tsx:454-471 (child form posts exactly one recipient, with relationship hard-coded 'Responsable'); prototype.ts:731-759 (completion auto-creates the next recurring trip)
   - Detail: If a child is sick or school is closed, the parent has no way to cancel. The dedicated driver still sees the scheduled trip, and the recurrence keeps generating. Recipients and codes cannot be changed after creation, for example to revoke a person who is no longer authorized, which is a child-safety requirement. Regular scheduled rides have modify and cancel (rides.ts:750-782); family trips do not.
   - Fix: Add cancel, skip-occurrence and pause for family trips, subscription cancellation and renewal, and child and recipient CRUD with code rotation and revocation. Reuse the scheduled-booking state machine and its cutoffs.
40. **The quote screen always requests category 'standard', so quotes fail if Standard is disabled or not offered in the city** — 🟡 medium · bug · _found-by-verifier_
   - Evidence: apps/passenger/src/app/(app)/quote.tsx:55 (useState('standard')), :58-60 (quote fired immediately with that categoryId), :154 (the chip list filters out disabled or out-of-city categories but the state is unchanged); services/demo-api/src/services/prototype.ts:240-248 (categoryFor → mustFind throws NOT_FOUND 'Catégorie disponible introuvable.'); services/demo-api/src/services/rides.ts:262,370 (dispatch and accept also fall back to 'standard')
   - Detail: The admin Services page can disable Standard or restrict it with cityIds. Every passenger then lands on a 'Prix indisponible · Catégorie disponible introuvable.' banner, with no selected chip, until she taps another category. This breaks the main booking flow because of a configuration change.
   - Fix: Initialize categoryId from the first enabled category for the city once the catalog loads, or let the server pick a default category per city. Show an empty state if none is enabled.

**Open questions raised**

- Which backoffice is the product going forward: apps/admin (connected, RBAC) or the apps/web /backoffice (better queue/priority UX but local-only)? Should /backoffice be removed from the public site?
- Is 'Naya Scooter' (two-wheeler) really in scope for a Rabat launch, given licence, insurance, helmet and passenger-safety implications? If so, which vehicle data and verification items are required?
- What is the policy on minors? The landing says children only with their mother; Naya Famille allows unaccompanied children up to 17. Are boys included, and up to what age?
- How should commission work: per city, per category, or a matrix? Which value applies in Casablanca for 'Naya Standard'?
- Should the passenger stored-value wallet exist at launch? Holding customer funds in Morocco likely needs a Bank Al-Maghrib-licensed payment institution partner; please confirm with counsel.
- What is the business model for Naya Famille: how and when is the subscription charged, how is the dedicated driver paid per trip, and what happens on driver absence or conflict?
- Who staffs SOS response, during which hours, with what escalation (police 19, Protection civile 15, SAMU 141), and which admin role owns it?
- What is the regulatory status of app-based VTC and a women-only transport service in Rabat? Which authorizations, insurance and CNDP declarations are needed before launch?
- Should standalone TestFlight builds remain the client demo default, given they cannot show passenger-to-driver-to-admin loops, SOS response or family sync? Or should a hosted demo API be used?
- Which emergency numbers should the app show? Please verify the '15 (SAMU)' label with local ops.
- Should drivers be able to rate or report passengers, and should passenger ratings affect dispatch?
- Should the six logo concepts stay visible in customer apps, and how will the client's selection be captured?
